# AI Usage Dashboard v4b — Every LLM, Wave 2 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

> **Status (2026-09-05):** Tasks 1–17 DONE on branch `v4b-wave2` (20 commits; suite 298/298 ×2, exit 0; 441 KB, 0 control bytes; screenshots `tests/artifacts/v4b-{light,dark,print,modal}.png`, gitignored). Deviations are recorded in the spec's "Wave 2 deviations" list. The whole-branch integration review and the merge decision are run by the controller after Task 17 — nothing is merged by this plan.

**Goal:** Deliver Wave 2 of the every-LLM spec — every remaining platform in the spec's platform table gets a hub card with a real data path (local server history / metrics-poll / API key / cloud token / export file) or an honest link-only card — plus the two items deferred from Plan 4a (Ollama desktop `db.sqlite` history, folder-log projects in the Projects table), in the same single file with the suite green.

**Architecture:** Plan 4a gave us generic `folder-log` and `local-server` drivers. Wave 2 adds three more generic pieces — a **key driver** (`App.connectKey(c)`: one code path for `api-key` / `admin-key` / `cloud-token` entries, key + optional extra `fields`, session-only secrets), a **metrics-poll** loop for local servers that only expose cumulative counters (`App.startPoll(c)`), and a **link-only** / **export-file** card kind — so each new platform is ONE `CONNECTORS` entry plus ONE small global function (a fetcher, validator, poller, or parser). Four small utilities support the parsers: `parsePrometheus`, `readZipEntries` (DecompressionStream, no library), `readSqliteTable` (minimal SQLite b-tree reader, no library), and `sigv4Sign` (WebCrypto, for Bedrock). All copy states data quality first, matching the spec's platform table.

**Tech Stack:** Single self-contained `AI Usage Dashboard.html` (vanilla JS, SVG, localStorage/IndexedDB, File System Access API, WebCrypto, DecompressionStream). Test rig: `tests/run.mjs` + puppeteer-core headless Chrome (`ONLY=<token> node tests/run.mjs` for the dev loop; the full suite is the gate), fixtures in `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs` (Node ≥ 22; `node:sqlite` used for the Ollama fixture, guarded).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-16-every-llm-and-view-design.md` (platform table = card copy source of truth; "Wave assignment"; honesty rules). Research: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-16-llm-platform-research.json`. Previous plan (patterns to copy): `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-16-v4a-view-and-folder-logs.md`.

**Baseline:** `main` at Plan 4a merged (209 checks green, 271 KB). Work on branch `v4b-wave2` (`git checkout -b v4b-wave2 main`).

---

## Binding conventions (unchanged from Plans 1–4a — read before every task)

- **Strict TDD.** Every task: write/extend the check FIRST, run `ONLY=<file-token> node tests/run.mjs`, keep the RED line as evidence; implement; GREEN; then the FULL suite `node tests/run.mjs` must be all-pass, exit 0. Never weaken an assertion to pass — if the plan's expected number is wrong, fix the plan's number and say so in the commit/report.
- **Check shape.** `export async function run({ browser, ok })`; body `try { … } finally { await page.close(); }`; `openDashboard(browser, { tz?, clockAt?, seedData?, keepStorage? })` from `tests/lib/harness.mjs` (clears `aiusage:*` unless seedData/keepStorage; `clockAt` shims Date). Stateful blocks reset: `Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.projects = []; Store.connectors = {}; App._drill = null; App.filters.hiddenPlatforms = new Set(); LS.del('view'); View.load();` — copy the idiom from an existing check. `App._render()` is the synchronous render; `App.render()` is rAF-batched. Stub network with `window.fetch = async (url, init) => …` inside `page.evaluate` (see `tests/checks/99-connectors.mjs` and `80-race.mjs`) and RESTORE it in the same block.
- **Hygiene.** Zero raw control bytes (< 0x09) in the HTML — `\u0001` separators are the visible 6-char escape text. When writing code through a Node script, build such strings with `String.fromCharCode`. After every edit: `node -e "const b=require('fs').readFileSync('AI Usage Dashboard.html');let n=0;for(const x of b)if(x<9)n++;console.log('control bytes:',n,'bytes:',b.length)"` → `control bytes: 0` and ≤ 600 KB (expect ≈ 330 KB at the end of 4b). `tests/checks/95-ui-misc.mjs` postRun guards this.
- **Anchors, not line numbers.** Every edit is anchored on a unique string quoted in the task. `Edit`/`Write` tool parameters collapse `\u0001` escapes into raw bytes — use the Node-script route for any code containing them.
- **Git.** Explicit paths only: `git add "AI Usage Dashboard.html" tests/checks/<file>.mjs tests/fixtures/gen.mjs` — never `-A`/`.`. One commit per task, prefix `feat:`/`fix:`/`test:`/`docs:`. Do not touch `docs/` from implementation tasks (the controller syncs docs between tasks; the final task updates docs).
- **Honesty & security.** Imported/remote strings reach the DOM via `textContent` / `el(tag, {text})` only — never innerHTML with data. Keys persist only with the explicit "Remember" opt-in (`LS.set(c.keyLS, key)`), `sessionOnly` connectors NEVER persist a key or secret field. Estimated rows carry `est:1`; local-runner platforms (`category:'local'`) are $0 by platform; coding-tool rows from local providers carry `local:1`. Card copy states data quality first and must match the spec's platform-table row (quality, method, path). No card claims consumer web-login access.
- **Registry rule.** One platform = one `CONNECTORS` entry + one global function. Entries for data kinds list their own id in `srcIds` (scanFiles / connectKey / connectServer / poll store rows under `c.id`). `statusEl` is always `c.id + '-status'` for Wave 2 entries (`ensureSourceCard` creates it). `PLATFORM_ORDER`, `LOCAL_PLATFORMS`, hub groups and the budgets editor derive from the registry — never hand-sync a list.

## Shared contracts (names every task must use exactly)

### Registry fields added in 4b

| field | kinds | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `kind: 'link-only'` | — | honest card: `reason` text + `links`; never "connected"; no Connect/Sync buttons; quality `'none'` |
| `kind: 'export-file'` | — | chat-app front door: card copy + "Import file…" button that opens the Imports modal; status `'ok'` when any of `srcIds` has rows |
| `kind: 'api-key' \| 'admin-key' \| 'cloud-token'` with `fetch` | key driver | `fetch` = global fn name: `async function fetchX(key, fields, opts) → { label, detail, rows?, billed?, meta? }` (`fields` = `{ [field.id]: string }`, `opts = { days }`; `days` = 90). `rows` → `Store.setSource(c.id, rows, meta)`; `billed` → `Store.setBilled(c.id, billed)` |
| `keyLabel`, `keyHint` | key driver | label/placeholder for the key input `<c.id>-key` (password, autocomplete off) |
| `fields: [{ id, label, placeholder, type: 'text' \| 'password' \| 'textarea', secret?: true }]` | key driver | extra inputs rendered as `<c.id>-f-<field.id>`; non-secret values persist to `LS 'field:<c.id>:<field.id>'`; secret fields follow the key's remember rule (persist only when remembered, never when `sessionOnly`) |
| `sessionOnly: true` | key driver | no "Remember" checkbox; key + secrets live only in the inputs; `refreshAll` re-fetches only while the key input still holds a value |
| `validate` (existing) | local-server | `async (base) → { label, detail, rows?, meta?, billed? }` — **4b extension:** when `rows` is returned `connectServer` stores them under `c.id` (LocalAI history) |
| `poll`, `pollEvery` | local-server | `poll` = global fn name `async function pollX(base) → { counters: { [model]: { in, out, cr?, msgs? } }, label? }` (cumulative process-lifetime counters; `msgs` optional — vLLM's request counter, absent for llama.cpp); `pollEvery` ms (default 60000). Driver: `App.startPoll(c)` after a successful validate, `App.stopPoll(c)` on disconnect |
| `folderLabel` | local-server (dual-driver: `baseUrl` + `patterns`) | noun for the folder button / folder status line (`'Choose <folderLabel> folder…'`, `'No <folderLabel> folder chosen.'`), default `'usage-log'` (Ollama) — e.g. `'Jan data'`, `'user_data'` (Task 6 wires it; Task 14 uses it on the Ollama entry) |
| `reason` | link-only | one sentence shown on the card (why there is no data path) |
| `corsHint` (existing) | local-server | `{base}` placeholder |

### App methods added in 4b

- `App.connectKey(c)` — generic key driver (gen slot `this._gen[c.id]`, status `<c.id>-status`, remember opt-in, `fields`, calls `globalThis[c.fetch]`, stores rows/billed, `Store.setConnector(c.id, { label, lastSync, err: null })`, `this.render()`; error → `setConnector({ err })` + status line; superseded-gen bail clears only its own placeholder like `fetchGemini`).
- `App.startPoll(c)`, `App.stopPoll(c)`, `App._pollTick(c)`, `App._pollTimers = {}` — metrics-poll loop; baseline in `LS 'poll:' + c.id` = `{ counters, at }`; each tick: `delta = max(0, cur - base)` per model and field (counter reset → new baseline, no negative); deltas merge into `Store.sources[c.id].rows` at `{ d: todayKey(), hour: new Date().getHours(), platform: c.platform, model }` (load existing rows, add, `Store.setSource`); label `'counts while this page is open'`.
- `App.openImportsFor(c)` — opens the Imports connector modal (used by export-file cards).
- `App.hubCard` gains `link-only` and `export-file` branches; `App.ensureSourceCard` renders key kinds (+fields), link-only, export-file; `App.syncConnector` routes `c.fetch` kinds → `connectKey`, link-only/export-file → no-op (export-file → `openImportsFor`); `App.disconnectConnector` stops polls, clears field inputs + `LS 'field:*'` + `LS 'poll:<id>'`; `App.refreshAll` covers key kinds.

### Utilities added in 4b (top-level functions)

- `parsePrometheus(text) → [{ name, labels: {…}, value }]` (text exposition format; `#` lines ignored; label values unescaped).
- `readZipEntries(file) → Promise<[{ name, size, text(): Promise<string>, bytes(): Promise<Uint8Array> }]>` — central-directory ZIP reader; methods 0 (stored) and 8 (deflate via `new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw')`); throws `Error('ZIP entry uses an unsupported method — unzip it first')` for anything else; ZIP64 not supported (throws the same).
- `readSqliteTable(arrayBuffer, tableName) → { columns: string[], rows: any[][] }` — minimal reader: header (page size, reserved bytes), `sqlite_master` on page 1, table b-tree (leaf 0x0D / interior 0x05), overflow chains, record serial types 0–9, 12+ blob, 13+ text; WITHOUT ROWID tables and WAL are not read (throws `Error('unsupported SQLite layout')`).
- `sigv4Sign({ method, url, headers, body, service, region, accessKeyId, secretAccessKey, sessionToken, now }) → Promise<Record<string,string>>` returning the headers to send (input headers + `host`, `x-amz-date`, `x-amz-content-sha256`, optional `x-amz-security-token`, `authorization`), using `crypto.subtle` (`sha256Hex`, `hmacSha256` helpers). Canonical request per AWS SigV4 (sorted, lower-cased, trimmed headers; query sorted by key then value; payload hash of `body` or empty string).
- `parseAnthropicUsage(payload, platform = 'Anthropic API')` (platform parameter added) and `parseAnthropicCost(payload)` — **cents fix:** amounts that are strings/numbers are in lowest units (cents) per Anthropic docs → `/ 100`; object amounts `{ value }` (OpenAI shape) stay as-is.

### Platform names (exact strings; `platform` field)

Local (category `'local'`, $0 by platform): `'LocalAI'`, `'vLLM'`, `'llama.cpp'`, `'GPT4All'`, `'Jan'`, `'text-generation-webui'`. API (category `'api'`): `'Fireworks AI'`, `'DeepSeek API'`, `'Mistral'`, `'Cohere'`, `'Together AI'`, `'Groq'`, `'Hugging Face'`, `'xAI API'`, `'Perplexity API'`, `'Replicate'` (link-only). Cloud (category `'cloud'`): `'Azure OpenAI'`, `'Google Vertex AI'`, `'AWS Bedrock'`. Chat (category `'chat'`): `'Claude Enterprise (org)'`, export-file cards `'ChatGPT'`, `'Claude.ai'`, `'Grok'`, `'Microsoft Copilot'`, `'Perplexity'`, `'Meta AI'`, `'DeepSeek chat'`. Coding (category `'coding'`): link-only `'Amazon Q / Kiro'`, `'Windsurf'`.

### Source ids (`srcIds`) for export formats (all stored by `handleImportFiles`)

`'grok-export'` → platform Grok · `'deepseek-chat-export'` → DeepSeek chat · `'meta-ai-export'` → Meta AI · `'perplexity-export'` → Perplexity · `'copilot-activity-csv'` → Microsoft Copilot · `'deepseek-usage-zip'` → DeepSeek API (listed in the `deepseek` connector's `srcIds`, not Imports). Existing: `'chatgpt'`, `'claudeai'`, `'csv'`, `'gemini-takeout'`, `'cursor-csv'`. The `imports` entry's `srcIds` gains the five new chat-export ids; the export-file cards list the same id (membership by source, like Copilot PAT vs CLI).

### Price rows added (DEFAULT_PRICES, before the `.*` fallback)

`{ pat: '^grok-4|^grok$', label: 'Grok 4 (xAI list)', in: 3, out: 15, cw: 0, cr: 0.75 }`, `{ pat: 'grok-3-mini|grok-4-fast', label: 'Grok fast/mini', in: 0.2, out: 0.5, cw: 0, cr: 0.05 }`, `{ pat: 'deepseek-reasoner|deepseek-r1', label: 'DeepSeek Reasoner', in: 0.55, out: 2.19, cw: 0, cr: 0.14 }`, `{ pat: 'deepseek', label: 'DeepSeek chat (V3 list)', in: 0.27, out: 1.10, cw: 0, cr: 0.07 }`, `{ pat: '^sonar|^perplexity$', label: 'Perplexity Sonar', in: 1, out: 1, cw: 0, cr: 0 }`, `{ pat: '^meta-ai$', label: 'Meta AI (consumer, no API price)', in: 0, out: 0, cw: 0, cr: 0 }` and extend the ChatGPT row pattern to `'^auto$|^gpt$|chatgpt|^copilot$'` (Microsoft Copilot rows use model `'copilot'`). Prices are August 2026 list prices from the vendors' pricing pages; all estimated rows are labeled est. (Task 16 owns this edit; earlier tasks may stage a row if their test needs it and say so.)

## Tasks

### Task 1: `link-only` kind + Amazon Q/Kiro, Replicate, Windsurf cards

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/checks/97-registry.mjs`, `tests/checks/98-hub.mjs`, `tests/checks/99-connectors.mjs`

Research rows (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-16-llm-platform-research.json`): **Amazon Q Developer / Kiro CLI** — `dataQuality: none`; personal accounts (Free tier / Builder ID) have no usage API or export (the IDE shows remaining monthly limits; Kiro individual plans show credits on the kiro.dev account page, web only); Pro/Enterprise admin reports are per-user daily CSVs in S3 + CloudWatch metrics (counts, not tokens or dollars) behind SigV4; Kiro CLI (Linux/macOS/WSL) keeps chats in `data.sqlite3` with an undocumented schema. **Replicate** — `dataQuality: none`; `GET /v1/account` and `GET /v1/predictions` answer preflight with no `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` (verified), so even key validation fails from `file://`; no spend API, no CSV export; billing only at replicate.com/account/billing. **Windsurf / Codeium** — `dataQuality: validate-only` but the only API (`POST server.codeium.com/api/v1/UserPageAnalytics`, Teams/Enterprise service key, credits in cents, current cycle) is CORS-blocked and not available on Free/Pro; individuals see prompt credits on windsurf.com/subscription/usage only; local Cascade storage is undocumented/binary with no counts. Spec honesty rule: "Amazon Q / Replicate / Windsurf link-only with the reason." Kind contract (header): `kind: 'link-only'` → `reason` + `links`, quality `'none'`, never "connected", no Connect/Sync/Disconnect buttons.

- [ ] **Step 1 — failing checks (97-registry, 98-hub, 99-connectors).**

```js
    // 4b T1: link-only cards — off dot, reason as the label, Details + vendor usage link only (no Connect/Sync/Disconnect),
    // Details opens the modal hosting the generic source card (reason + links), ✕ hides / chip restores, sync is a no-op
    const lo = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.projects = []; Store.connectors = {};
      App._drill = null; App.filters.hiddenPlatforms = new Set(); LS.del('view'); View.load(); App._hubQuery = ''; document.getElementById('hub-search').value = '';
      App._render();
      const c = connectorById('replicate');
      const card = document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="replicate"]');
      const btns = [...card.querySelectorAll('.acct-actions button')].map(b => b.textContent);
      const usage = card.querySelector('.acct-actions a');
      const ui = { dot: card.querySelector('.dot').className, label: card.querySelector('.acct-label').textContent, btns, sync: !!card.querySelector('.acct-sync-btn'),
        usageHref: usage && usage.getAttribute('href'), usageRel: usage && usage.getAttribute('rel'), group: card.closest('.hub-group').dataset.group, cls: card.className,
        aria: card.getAttribute('aria-label'), reasonOnCard: card.querySelector('.acct-label').textContent === c.reason, hide: !!card.querySelector('.acct-hide'),
        groups: { amazonq: document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="amazonq"]').closest('.hub-group').dataset.group,
                  windsurf: document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="windsurf"]').closest('.hub-group').dataset.group } };
      // Details → modal hosts the generic source card with the reason + links; close → card goes home hidden (it is never connected)
      const dlg = document.getElementById('connector-modal');
      card.querySelector('.acct-manage').click();
      const src = dlg.querySelector('#src-replicate');
      const modal = { open: dlg.open, has: !!src, reason: !!src && src.textContent.includes(c.reason), usageLink: !!src && !!src.querySelector('a[href="' + c.links.usage + '"]'),
        statusText: document.getElementById('replicate-status').textContent, badge: src && src.querySelector('.badge').textContent };
      const closed = new Promise(r => dlg.addEventListener('close', r, { once: true }));
      dlg.close(); await closed;
      const home = document.querySelector('#sources #src-replicate');
      const after = { home: !!home, hidden: !!home && home.hidden, status: Store.connectorStatus(c), noState: Store.connectors.replicate === undefined };
      // ✕ hides the card; the "N hidden — show" chip restores it
      card.querySelector('.acct-hide').click(); App._render();
      const gone = !document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="replicate"]');
      const chip = document.querySelector('#hub-chips .hub-hidden-chip');
      const chipText = chip && chip.textContent;
      if (chip) chip.click(); App._render();
      const back = !!document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="replicate"]');
      // sync is a no-op for link-only (unreachable from the UI; guard the API): returns undefined, opens nothing, creates no state
      const r = App.syncConnector('replicate');
      const syncNoop = r === undefined && !dlg.open && Store.connectors.replicate === undefined;
      return { ui, modal, after, gone, chipText, back, syncNoop };
    });
    ok('link-only: card shows off dot, reason as label, Details + vendor usage link only (no Connect/Sync/Disconnect); Amazon Q + Windsurf in Coding tools, Replicate in API vendors',
      /\boff\b/.test(lo.ui.dot) && lo.ui.reasonOnCard && JSON.stringify(lo.ui.btns) === JSON.stringify(['Details']) && !lo.ui.sync && lo.ui.hide &&
      lo.ui.usageHref === 'https://replicate.com/account/billing' && lo.ui.usageRel === 'noopener' && /link-only/.test(lo.ui.cls) && /no data path/.test(lo.ui.aria) &&

    ok('link-only: Details opens the modal hosting the generic card (no-data badge, reason, usage link, "No data path" status); close hides it again; sync is a no-op',
      lo.modal.open && lo.modal.has && lo.modal.reason && lo.modal.usageLink && /no data path/i.test(lo.modal.statusText) && lo.modal.badge === 'no data' &&

    ok('link-only: ✕ hides the card and the hidden chip restores it', lo.gone && /^1 hidden — show$/.test(lo.chipText || '') && lo.back, JSON.stringify({ gone: lo.gone, chipText: lo.chipText, back: lo.back }));
```

```js
    // 4b T1: link-only entries never fetch, never get connector state, never prompt (refreshAll / sync / disconnect are no-ops)
    const lo = await page.evaluate(async () => {

      const origFetch = window.fetch, origConfirm = window.confirm;
      let fetches = 0, confirms = 0;

      window.confirm = () => { confirms++; return true; };
      try {
        await App.refreshAll();
        const sync = ids.map(id => App.syncConnector(id));
        for (const id of ids) App.disconnectConnector(id);
        return { fetches, confirms, syncUndefined: sync.every(r => r === undefined), noState: ids.every(id => Store.connectors[id] === undefined),
          statuses: ids.map(id => Store.connectorStatus(connectorById(id))), texts: ids.map(id => document.getElementById(id + '-status').textContent) };
      } finally { window.fetch = origFetch; window.confirm = origConfirm; }
    });
```

### Task 2: Generic key driver `App.connectKey` + `ensureSourceCard` key UI + fields + sessionOnly (+ Groq validate-only card as the first real entry)

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, Create `tests/checks/87c-wave2-keys.mjs`

Contract (header): entries with `kind: 'api-key' | 'admin-key' | 'cloud-token'` and `fetch: 'fetchX'` where `async function fetchX(key, fields, opts) → { label, detail, rows?, billed?, meta? }` (`fields = { [field.id]: string }`, `opts = { days: 90 }`); `rows` → `Store.setSource(c.id, rows, meta)`, `billed` → `Store.setBilled(c.id, billed)`; `keyLabel` / `keyHint`; `fields: [{ id, label, placeholder, type: 'text'|'password'|'textarea', secret?: true }]` rendered as `<c.id>-f-<field.id>` (non-secret values persist to `LS 'field:<c.id>:<field.id>'`, secrets follow the key's Remember rule and never persist when `sessionOnly`); `sessionOnly: true` → no Remember checkbox, `refreshAll` re-fetches only while the key input still holds a value. Every key-driver entry also carries `keyLS: 'key:<id>'` (the existing `syncConnector` / `refreshAll` key plumbing and the Remember opt-in key on it) and `srcIds: [c.id]`. `CONNECTORS` is `const`, so the driver is exercised through a REAL Wave-2 entry: **Groq** (research: `dataQuality: validate-only`, `GET https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models` with Bearer, CORS `*` verified; no usage/spend API; console-only usage + billing; Enterprise-only Prometheus). Task 3 adds the other six validate-only vendors with the same entry shape; Task 13 owns `App.openImportsFor` / `export-file`; Task 5 adds `App.stopPoll` to `disconnectConnector`; Task 10 adds the first `sessionOnly` / `fields` entries (this task proves the mechanics by patching the Groq entry inside the check and restoring it).

```js
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { openDashboard, OUT } from '../lib/harness.mjs';

const read = (...p) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(OUT, ...p), 'utf8');   // Tasks 7 and 9 append blocks that read wave2/ fixtures through this helper

export async function run({ browser, ok }) {
  const { page } = await openDashboard(browser);
  try {
    // ---- T2 (a): key UI + happy path + Remember opt-in + bad key, through the real Groq entry (validate-only) ----
    const kd = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.projects = []; Store.connectors = {};
      App._drill = null; App.filters.hiddenPlatforms = new Set(); LS.del('view'); View.load();
      const c = connectorById('groq');
      if (!c) throw new Error('no groq entry in CONNECTORS');
      const keyEl = document.getElementById('groq-key'), rem = document.getElementById('remember-groq');
      const ui = { src: !!document.getElementById('src-groq'), keyType: keyEl && keyEl.type, keyAuto: keyEl && keyEl.getAttribute('autocomplete'), keyPh: keyEl && keyEl.placeholder,
        rem: !!rem, btn: !!document.getElementById('groq-validate'), status: !!document.getElementById('groq-status'), badge: document.querySelector('#src-groq .badge').textContent,
        kind: c.kind, keyLS: c.keyLS, srcIds: c.srcIds, quality: c.quality, hiddenAtStart: document.getElementById('src-groq').hidden,
        note: /validate-only/i.test(c.qualityNote) && /no usage or spend API/i.test(c.qualityNote) };
      const calls = [];
      const origFetch = window.fetch;
      window.fetch = async (url, init) => {
        calls.push({ url: String(url), auth: init && init.headers && init.headers.Authorization });
        if (String(url) === 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models') return new Response(JSON.stringify({ object: 'list', data: [{ id: 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile' }, { id: 'openai/gpt-oss-120b' }, { id: 'whisper-large-v3' }] }), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
        return new Response('{}', { status: 404, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
      };
      try {
        keyEl.value = 'gsk_test_1234'; rem.checked = false;
        await App.connectKey(c);
        const st = Store.connectors.groq;
        const okState = { label: st && st.label, err: st && st.err, status: Store.connectorStatus(c), text: document.getElementById('groq-status').textContent,
          hasSrc: !!Store.sources.groq, notRemembered: LS.get('key:groq', null), leak: document.documentElement.outerHTML.includes('gsk_test_1234'),
          calls: calls.length, url: calls[0] && calls[0].url, auth: calls[0] && calls[0].auth };
        App.renderHub();
        const hub = document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="groq"]');
        const hubState = { dot: hub.querySelector('.dot').className, label: hub.querySelector('.acct-label').textContent, sync: !!hub.querySelector('.acct-sync-btn'),
          group: hub.closest('.hub-group').dataset.group, srcShown: !document.getElementById('src-groq').hidden };
        // Remember opt-in → key persists; unchecking forgets it
        rem.checked = true;
        await App.connectKey(c);
        const remembered = LS.get('key:groq', null);
        rem.checked = false; rem.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        const forgotten = LS.get('key:groq', null);
        // bad key → attention + message, state keeps the entry (warn, not off)
        window.fetch = async () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: { message: 'Invalid API Key' } }), { status: 401, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
        await App.connectKey(c);
        const bad = { err: Store.connectors.groq.err, status: Store.connectorStatus(c), text: document.getElementById('groq-status').textContent };
        // empty key → message only, no network, no state change
        const before = calls.length; keyEl.value = '';
        await App.connectKey(c);
        const empty = { calls: calls.length - before, text: document.getElementById('groq-status').textContent };
        return { ui, okState, hubState, remembered, forgotten, bad, empty };
      } finally { window.fetch = origFetch; }
    });
    ok('key driver: Groq card renders from the registry (password input, autocomplete off, gsk_ hint, Remember, Validate, status, validate-only badge, hidden until connected; honest note)',
      kd.ui.src && kd.ui.keyType === 'password' && kd.ui.keyAuto === 'off' && /gsk_/.test(kd.ui.keyPh) && kd.ui.rem && kd.ui.btn && kd.ui.status && kd.ui.badge === 'validate-only' &&
      kd.ui.kind === 'api-key' && kd.ui.keyLS === 'key:groq' && JSON.stringify(kd.ui.srcIds) === JSON.stringify(['groq']) && kd.ui.quality === 'validate' && kd.ui.hiddenAtStart && kd.ui.note, JSON.stringify(kd.ui));
    ok('key driver: connectKey validates with Bearer GET /openai/v1/models (3 models → label "3 models"), connector ok, hub card green in API vendors + Sync, key never in DOM text, not remembered unless opted in; Remember persists, unchecking forgets',
      kd.okState.calls === 1 && kd.okState.url === 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models' && kd.okState.auth === 'Bearer gsk_test_1234' && kd.okState.label === '3 models' && kd.okState.err === null &&
      kd.okState.status === 'ok' && /Connected/.test(kd.okState.text) && /3 models/.test(kd.okState.text) && !kd.okState.hasSrc && kd.okState.notRemembered === null && !kd.okState.leak &&
      /\bok\b/.test(kd.hubState.dot) && kd.hubState.label === '3 models' && kd.hubState.sync && kd.hubState.group === 'api' && kd.hubState.srcShown &&
      kd.remembered === 'gsk_test_1234' && kd.forgotten === null, JSON.stringify({ okState: kd.okState, hubState: kd.hubState, remembered: kd.remembered, forgotten: kd.forgotten }));
    ok('key driver: invalid key → warn + "HTTP 401 — Invalid API Key"; empty key → prompt text, no network call',
      /HTTP 401/.test(kd.bad.err) && /Invalid API Key/.test(kd.bad.err) && kd.bad.status === 'warn' && /Validation failed/.test(kd.bad.text) && kd.empty.calls === 0 && /Paste/.test(kd.empty.text), JSON.stringify({ bad: kd.bad, empty: kd.empty }));

    // ---- T2 (b): rows/billed/meta/opts pass-through, superseded generation, sessionOnly + fields, refreshAll, disconnect ----
    const kd2 = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.projects = []; Store.connectors = {};
      const c = connectorById('groq');
      const origFetchGroq = window.fetchGroq;
      const saved = { fields: c.fields, sessionOnly: c.sessionOnly };   // test-local patch of the real entry (restored in finally)
      const today = dayKey(new Date());
      try {
        // (a) rows + billed + meta from the fetcher land under c.id; fields ({}) and opts.days (90) are passed through
        let seen = null;
        window.fetchGroq = async (key, fields, opts) => { seen = { key, fields, days: opts && opts.days }; return { label: 'k-' + key.slice(-4), detail: 'test detail',
          // in = 1000 + 500 = 1500 · out = 250 + 125 = 375 · msgs = 4 + 2 = 6 · billed = 1.25
          rows: [{ d: today, platform: 'Groq', model: 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile', in: 1000, cw: 0, cr: 0, out: 250, msgs: 4 }, { d: today, platform: 'Groq', model: 'openai/gpt-oss-120b', in: 500, cw: 0, cr: 0, out: 125, msgs: 2 }],
          billed: [{ d: today, amount: 1.25 }], meta: { note: 'from-test' } }; };
        document.getElementById('groq-key').value = 'gsk_rows_9999';
        await App.connectKey(c);
        const rows = Store.sources.groq.rows;
        const t = rows.reduce((a, r) => ({ in: a.in + r.in, out: a.out + r.out, msgs: a.msgs + r.msgs }), { in: 0, out: 0, msgs: 0 });
        const a = { label: Store.connectors.groq.label, ...t, billed: Store.billedRows('groq').reduce((s, b) => s + b.amount, 0), metaNote: Store.sources.groq.meta.note,

        // (b) superseded generation: a slow first call resolving after a newer one must not commit, and must not blank the newer status line
        let release;
        window.fetchGroq = () => new Promise(res => { release = res; });
        const p1 = App.connectKey(c);                                 // slow (gen N)
        window.fetchGroq = async () => ({ label: 'fast', detail: 'fast detail' });
        await App.connectKey(c);                                      // fast (gen N+1) finishes first
        release({ label: 'SLOW', detail: 'slow detail', rows: [{ d: today, platform: 'Groq', model: 'stale', in: 1, cw: 0, cr: 0, out: 1, msgs: 1 }] });
        await p1;
        const b = { label: Store.connectors.groq.label, text: document.getElementById('groq-status').textContent, rowsIn: Store.sources.groq.rows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.in, 0) };
        // (c) sessionOnly + extra fields: patch the entry and re-render its card
        LS.del('key:groq');
        c.fields = [{ id: 'region', label: 'Region', placeholder: 'us-east-1' }, { id: 'secret', label: 'Secret', type: 'password', secret: true }, { id: 'notes', label: 'Notes', type: 'textarea' }];
        c.sessionOnly = true;
        document.getElementById('src-groq').remove(); App.ensureSourceCard(c);
        const fUI = { region: !!document.getElementById('groq-f-region'), regionType: document.getElementById('groq-f-region').type, secretType: document.getElementById('groq-f-secret').type,
          notesTag: document.getElementById('groq-f-notes').tagName, noRemember: !document.getElementById('remember-groq'), note: /session only/i.test(document.getElementById('src-groq').textContent) };
        document.getElementById('groq-key').value = 'tok_session_1111';
        document.getElementById('groq-f-region').value = 'us-east-1';
        document.getElementById('groq-f-secret').value = 's3cr3t';
        document.getElementById('groq-f-notes').value = 'n';
        window.fetchGroq = async (key, fields) => { seen = { key, fields }; return { label: 'ok', detail: '' }; };
        await App.connectKey(c);
        const cPersist = { keyLS: LS.get('key:groq', null), region: LS.get('field:groq:region', null), secret: LS.get('field:groq:secret', null), notes: LS.get('field:groq:notes', null),
          fields: JSON.stringify(seen.fields), key: seen.key, leak: document.documentElement.outerHTML.includes('tok_session_1111') || document.documentElement.outerHTML.includes('s3cr3t') };
        // refreshAll: a sessionOnly card re-fetches while its input still holds a token; with the input cleared it does nothing
        let calls = 0; window.fetchGroq = async () => { calls++; return { label: 'ok', detail: '' }; };
        await App.refreshAll(); const refreshWithValue = calls;
        document.getElementById('groq-key').value = '';
        await App.refreshAll(); const refreshEmpty = calls;
        // (d) disconnect clears the key, every field input + its LS value, the connector state and (OK) the data
        document.getElementById('groq-key').value = 'tok_session_1111';
        const origConfirm = window.confirm; window.confirm = () => true;
        App.disconnectConnector('groq');
        window.confirm = origConfirm;
        const d = { key: document.getElementById('groq-key').value, region: document.getElementById('groq-f-region').value, regionLS: LS.get('field:groq:region', null),
          st: Store.connectors.groq === undefined, src: Store.sources.groq === undefined, text: document.getElementById('groq-status').textContent };
        return { a, b, fUI, cPersist, refreshWithValue, refreshEmpty, d };
      } finally {
        window.fetchGroq = origFetchGroq;
        if (saved.fields === undefined) delete c.fields; else c.fields = saved.fields;
        if (saved.sessionOnly === undefined) delete c.sessionOnly; else c.sessionOnly = saved.sessionOnly;
        const card = document.getElementById('src-groq'); if (card) card.remove(); App.ensureSourceCard(c);
        LS.del('key:groq'); LS.del('field:groq:region'); LS.del('field:groq:secret'); LS.del('field:groq:notes');
        Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.connectors = {}; Store.persist(); App._render();
      }
    });

      kd2.a.label === 'k-9999' && kd2.a.in === 1500 && kd2.a.out === 375 && kd2.a.msgs === 6 && Math.abs(kd2.a.billed - 1.25) < 1e-9 && kd2.a.metaNote === 'from-test' &&
      kd2.a.days === 90 && kd2.a.fields === '{}' && kd2.a.key === 'gsk_rows_9999' && /test detail/.test(kd2.a.text), JSON.stringify(kd2.a));
    ok('key driver: a superseded (slower, older) connect commits nothing and leaves the newer status line intact',

      kd2.fUI.region && kd2.fUI.regionType === 'text' && kd2.fUI.secretType === 'password' && kd2.fUI.notesTag === 'TEXTAREA' && kd2.fUI.noRemember && kd2.fUI.note &&

        && !!document.getElementById(c.statusEl) && c.srcIds.includes(c.id) && typeof globalThis[c.fetch] === 'function' && typeof c.keyLabel === 'string'
        && /^(exact|validate|estimated)$/.test(c.quality) && typeof c.qualityNote === 'string' && c.qualityNote.length > 80 && !!document.getElementById(c.id + '-key')

    });

      inv.ids.includes('groq') && inv.every, JSON.stringify(inv));
  } finally {
    await page.close();

}
```

- [ ] **Step 5 — `App.connectKey`.** Insert directly BEFORE the line `  async pickFolder() {` (i.e. right after `connectServer`'s closing `  },`):

```js
  /* =================== generic key driver (api-key / admin-key / cloud-token entries with `fetch`) =================== */
  /** One code path for every key-bearing Wave-2 card: reads '<id>-key' (+ extra `fields` '<id>-f-<field>'), honors the Remember opt-in
   *  (never for sessionOnly), calls globalThis[c.fetch](key, fields, { days: 90 }) → { label, detail, rows?, billed?, meta? } and stores
   *  rows → Store.setSource(c.id), billed → Store.setBilled(c.id) (null = soft-fail → stale envelope), then the connector entry. Own gen slot
   *  this._gen[c.id]: a superseded call (newer connect, disconnect, clear-data) commits nothing and clears only its own placeholder —
   *  the fetchGemini rule (status.dataset.gen). */
  async connectKey(c) {
    const status = $(c.id + '-status');
    const gen = this._gen[c.id] = (this._gen[c.id] || 0) + 1;
    const keyEl = $(c.id + '-key');
    const key = keyEl ? keyEl.value.trim() : '';
    if (!key) { status.textContent = `Paste ${c.keyLabel ? 'your ' + c.keyLabel : 'a key'} first${c.keyHint ? ' (' + c.keyHint + ')' : ''}.`; return; }
    const fields = {};
    for (const f of c.fields || []) { const fe = $(c.id + '-f-' + f.id); fields[f.id] = fe ? fe.value.trim() : ''; }
    const rem = $('remember-' + c.id);
    const remember = !c.sessionOnly && !!(rem && rem.checked);
    if (remember) LS.set(c.keyLS, key);
    for (const f of c.fields || []) {
      const lsKey = 'field:' + c.id + ':' + f.id;
      if (fieldIsSecret(f)) { if (remember && fields[f.id]) LS.set(lsKey, fields[f.id]); }          // secrets follow the key's Remember rule; sessionOnly never persists them
      else if (fields[f.id]) LS.set(lsKey, fields[f.id]); else LS.del(lsKey);                        // non-secret values (region, subscription id, …) persist for convenience
    }
    status.textContent = 'Validating…'; status.dataset.gen = String(gen);
    try {
      const fn = globalThis[c.fetch];
      if (typeof fn !== 'function') throw new Error('No fetcher registered: ' + c.fetch);
      const res = (await fn(key, fields, { days: 90 })) || {};
      if (gen !== this._gen[c.id]) { if (status.dataset.gen === String(gen)) status.textContent = ''; return; }   // superseded — only clear the placeholder if it's still ours
      if (Array.isArray(res.rows)) Store.setSource(c.id, res.rows, res.meta || {});
      if (res.billed !== undefined) Store.setBilled(c.id, res.billed);                               // array = fresh (or truthful empty), null = fetch failed → stale envelope
      Store.setConnector(c.id, { label: res.label || ((c.keyLabel || 'key') + ' · last 4: ' + key.slice(-4)), lastSync: Date.now(), err: null });
      status.textContent = ''; status.dataset.gen = '';
      status.appendChild(el('span', { class: 'ok', text: '● Connected. ' }));
      status.appendChild(document.createTextNode((res.detail ? res.detail + ' · ' : '') + 'updated ' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })));
      this.render();
    } catch (e) {
      if (gen !== this._gen[c.id]) { if (status.dataset.gen === String(gen)) status.textContent = ''; return; }   // superseded — only clear the placeholder if it's still ours
      Store.setConnector(c.id, { err: (e.message || String(e)).slice(0, 80) });
      status.textContent = ''; status.dataset.gen = '';
      status.appendChild(el('span', { class: 'err', text: 'Validation failed: ' }));
      status.appendChild(document.createTextNode(e.message || String(e)));
      this.render();
    }
  },

```

**Requires Task 2** (`App.connectKey(c)`, `ensureSourceCard` key UI: `<c.id>-key` input + `<c.id>-status`, registry fields `fetch` / `keyLabel` / `keyHint`, `syncConnector` → `connectKey`). **Groq is Task 2's worked example** (`platform: 'Groq'`, `GET https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models`) — do NOT add a second Groq entry; this task only asserts it exists in the registry-invariant check.

Research rows (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-16-llm-platform-research.json`), all `dataQuality: validate-only`, all browser-callable with a plain `Authorization: Bearer` header:
- **Mistral** — standard La Plateforme keys have NO usage/billing endpoint; `GET https://api.mistral.ai/v1/models` validates + lists models (`{ object:'list', data:[{id}] }`). The Enterprise Admin API (`/v1/admin/usage`) is monthly spend by category (no tokens per model) and is CORS-blocked from `file://` → not used.
- **Cohere** — `POST https://api.cohere.com/v1/check-api-key` → `{ valid, organization_id, owner_id }`; `GET https://api.cohere.com/v2/models` → `{ models:[{name}] }`. CORS `*` verified. No usage endpoint (usage = per-response `meta.billed_units` + dashboard.cohere.com/billing).
- **Together AI** — `GET https://api.together.xyz/v1/models` → bare array `[{id, display_name}]`. CORS `*` verified; `/v1/billing` is 404. Prepaid; usage/cost are console pages only.
- **Hugging Face** — `GET https://huggingface.co/api/whoami-v2` → `{ type, name, fullname, isPro, orgs:[{name}] , … }` (CORS echoes Origin null). Inference spend only at huggingface.co/settings/inference-providers/overview.
- **xAI** — regular key: `GET https://api.x.ai/v1/api-key` → key metadata (`name`, `team_id`, `api_key_blocked`, `api_key_disabled`, `team_blocked`, `acls`), `GET https://api.x.ai/v1/models` → `{ data:[{id}] }`; CORS `*`. Billed history lives on the Management API (management-api.x.ai) which sends NO CORS headers → not used.
- **Perplexity API (Sonar)** — `GET https://api.perplexity.ai/v1/models` is **unauthenticated** ("no auth required"), so it proves reachability, not the key; a real validation would need a paid completion. No balance/usage endpoint; the org analytics endpoint covers the "Computer" product only.

```js
          calls, rows: !!Store.sources[k.id], billed: Store.billedRows(k.id).length };

      && /^4 models available$/.test(vend.together.label) && vend.together.calls.length === 1 && vend.together.calls[0].u === 'https://api.together.xyz/v1/models' && vend.together.calls[0].auth === 'Bearer tg-test'

        if (!Array.isArray(c.srcIds) || !c.srcIds.includes(id)) bad.push(id + ': srcIds');

        if (!c.links || !c.links.usage || !c.links.docs) bad.push(id + ': links');
        if (!Array.isArray(c.paths) || !c.paths.length) bad.push(id + ': paths');
        if (!document.getElementById('src-' + id)) bad.push(id + ': generic card');

      const groq = CONNECTORS.find(c => c.platform === 'Groq');
      if (!groq || groq.kind !== 'api-key' || groq.quality !== 'validate') bad.push('groq (Task 2) missing or not validate-only');
      return { bad, inOrder: Object.values(want).every(p => PLATFORM_ORDER.includes(p)) };
    });
    ok('validate-only vendors: registry invariants (api-key · api · validate · fetch fn · srcIds self · statusEl + card · honest notes/links; Groq from Task 2 present; PLATFORM_ORDER derived)',
      vinv.bad.length === 0 && vinv.inOrder, JSON.stringify(vinv));
```

- [ ] **Step 4 — fetchers.** Insert directly BEFORE the line `async function fetchCopilotAll(token) {`:

```js
/* ---------- Wave 2 validate-only vendors — key-driver fetchers: async (key, fields, opts) → { label, detail } (no rows: nothing to read) ---------- */
/** Model-list length in any of the shapes these vendors return: bare array (Together), {data:[…]} (OpenAI-style: Mistral, xAI, Perplexity), {models:[…]} (Cohere v2). */
const countModels = (j) => Array.isArray(j) ? j.length : Array.isArray(j && j.data) ? j.data.length : Array.isArray(j && j.models) ? j.models.length : 0;
/** Mistral La Plateforme — standard keys have no usage/billing endpoint; GET /v1/models proves the key works. */
async function fetchMistral(key) {
  const j = await fetchJSON('https://api.mistral.ai/v1/models', { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key });
  const n = countModels(j);
  return { label: `${n} models available`, detail: `key valid · ${n} models · Mistral has no usage or spend API for standard keys — see the console for spend (the Enterprise Admin API is CORS-blocked from this page)` };
}
/** Cohere — POST /v1/check-api-key (valid + organization), then GET /v2/models for a count. No usage endpoint exists. */
async function fetchCohere(key) {
  const h = { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key };
  const res = await fetch('https://api.cohere.com/v1/check-api-key', { method: 'POST', headers: h });
  if (!res.ok) {
    let detail = '';
    try { const j = await res.json(); detail = (j && (j.message || (j.error && j.error.message))) || ''; } catch {}
    throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}${detail ? ' — ' + detail : ''}`);
  }
  const chk = await res.json();
  if (chk && chk.valid === false) throw new Error('Cohere reports this key as not valid');
  let n = 0;
  try { n = countModels(await fetchJSON('https://api.cohere.com/v2/models', h)); } catch (e) { console.warn('cohere models unavailable', e); }
  const org = chk && chk.organization_id ? ' · org ' + String(chk.organization_id).slice(0, 12) : '';
  return { label: `valid key · ${n} models`, detail: `key valid${org} · ${n} models · usage is per-response metadata + dashboard.cohere.com/billing only (no usage API)` };
}
/** Together AI — GET /v1/models (bare array). No usage, credits or billing endpoint is documented. */
async function fetchTogether(key) {
  const j = await fetchJSON('https://api.together.xyz/v1/models', { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key });
  const n = countModels(j);
  return { label: `${n} models available`, detail: `key valid · ${n} models · Together exposes no usage, credits or billing endpoint — spend lives in the console billing / cost-analytics pages` };
}
/** Hugging Face — GET /api/whoami-v2: account, plan (isPro), orgs. Inference spend is settings-UI only. */
async function fetchHuggingFace(key) {
  const j = await fetchJSON('https://huggingface.co/api/whoami-v2', { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key });
  const who = String(j.name || j.fullname || 'hf user');
  const orgs = Array.isArray(j.orgs) ? j.orgs.map(o => o && (o.name || o.fullname)).filter(Boolean).map(String) : [];
  const plan = j.isPro ? 'PRO' : (j.type === 'org' ? 'org' : 'free');
  const orgLabel = orgs.length ? ` · ${orgs.length} org${orgs.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}` : '';
  return { label: `${who} · ${plan}${orgLabel}`,
    detail: `token valid for ${who} (${plan})${orgs.length ? ' · orgs: ' + orgs.join(', ') : ''} · inference spend and monthly credits are visible only at huggingface.co/settings/inference-providers/overview (no usage API)` };
}
/** xAI — regular key: GET /v1/api-key (name, team, blocked/disabled flags) + GET /v1/models. Usage needs the CORS-blocked Management API. */
async function fetchXai(key) {
  const h = { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key };
  const j = await fetchJSON('https://api.x.ai/v1/api-key', h);
  if (j.api_key_disabled || j.api_key_blocked || j.team_blocked) throw new Error('xAI reports this key as ' + (j.api_key_disabled ? 'disabled' : j.api_key_blocked ? 'blocked' : 'on a blocked team'));
  let n = 0;
  try { n = countModels(await fetchJSON('https://api.x.ai/v1/models', h)); } catch (e) { console.warn('xai models unavailable', e); }
  const team = j.team_id ? ' · team ' + String(j.team_id).slice(0, 8) : '';
  return { label: `${j.name || 'xAI key'}${team}`,
    detail: `key "${j.name || 'unnamed'}" valid${team} · ${n} models · usage and spend need the Management API (management-api.x.ai), which sends no CORS headers — use the console Usage Explorer` };
}
/** Perplexity API — GET /v1/models is unauthenticated: proves reachability, NOT the key (only a paid completion would). No usage endpoint. */
async function fetchPerplexityApi(key) {
  const j = await fetchJSON('https://api.perplexity.ai/v1/models', { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key });
  const n = countModels(j);
  return { label: 'reachable — Sonar keys cannot be verified without a paid request',
    detail: `api.perplexity.ai reachable · ${n} models listed · GET /v1/models needs no auth, so this does NOT prove the key is valid (only a paid completion would) · usage and spend are in the API console Billing tab (no usage API)` };
}
```

### Task 4: `connectServer` rows extension + LocalAI `/api/usage` history

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`; Create `tests/checks/87b-wave2-local.mjs` (if Task 5/6/14 already created it, append the T4 block inside its `try` instead)

Research row (LocalAI, confidence medium): `http://localhost:8080` — `GET /api/usage?period=day|week|month|all` → `{ usage: [{ bucket, model, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, total_tokens, request_count, … }], totals: {…}, viewer: {…} }` (LocalAI v4.4+, routing/billing middleware merged May 2026). Storage: SQLite when `--auth` is on (durable), an **in-memory ring buffer** when it is off (lost on restart), `--disable-stats` → the endpoint answers **503**. CORS: default (`LOCALAI_CORS` unset) is permissive (`Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`); `--cors=true` needs an explicit `--cors-allow-origins` list. `--api-keys` protects the endpoint (Bearer / `x-api-key`) — this card sends **no key** (kept simple; documented on the card). Quality **exact** (tokens + request counts; no cost). This is the only local runner with a real server-side history, so it is the first `local-server` whose validator returns `rows` — the generic `connectServer` gains the **rows extension** from the Shared contracts (`{ label, detail, rows?, meta?, billed? }` → `Store.setSource(c.id, rows, meta)`), plus an `answered` error flag so a real HTTP answer (503 stats disabled) is never mis-reported as CORS.

```js
/** LocalAI history: GET /api/usage?period=all → { usage: [{ bucket, model, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, total_tokens, request_count }] }.
 *  Exact tokens + request counts per UTC day bucket. 503 = --disable-stats (the server answered → e.answered, not a CORS guess).
 *  Without --auth the server keeps an in-memory ring buffer that a restart empties: rows for days OLDER than the fresh payload are kept
 *  from earlier syncs (Store.sources.localai), days the payload covers are replaced (never doubled); an empty payload keeps everything. */
async function validateLocalAI(base) {
  const res = await fetch(base + '/api/usage?period=all', { cache: 'no-store' });
  if (res.status === 503) { const e = new Error('LocalAI answered 503 on /api/usage: usage stats are disabled (--disable-stats) — restart LocalAI without that flag, then validate again.'); e.answered = true; throw e; }
  if (!res.ok) { const e = new Error(`HTTP ${res.status} on /api/usage — not a LocalAI v4.4+ server? (llama.cpp and other servers also default to port 8080)`); e.answered = true; throw e; }
  const j = await res.json();
  const list = Array.isArray(j && j.usage) ? j.usage : [];
  const agg = makeAgg(); const models = new Set(); let tin = 0, tout = 0, minDay = null;
  for (const u of list) {
    if (!u || typeof u !== 'object') continue;
    const b = typeof u.bucket === 'number' && u.bucket < 1e12 ? u.bucket * 1000 : u.bucket;   // epoch seconds → ms; ISO strings pass through
    const d = dayKeyUTC(b); if (!d) continue;                                                  // provider day buckets are UTC days
    const model = String(u.model || 'unknown');
    const t = { in: finTok(+u.prompt_tokens || 0), out: finTok(+u.completion_tokens || 0), msgs: finTok(+u.request_count || 0) };
    agg.add(d, 'LocalAI', model, t); models.add(model); tin += t.in; tout += t.out;
    if (!minDay || d < minDay) minDay = d;
  }
  const existing = ((Store.sources.localai || {}).rows || []);
  const kept = minDay ? existing.filter(r => r.d < minDay) : existing;
  const fresh = agg.rows();
  const n = list.length, nm = models.size;
  return { label: `history: ${n} bucket${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`,
    detail: `${n} usage bucket${n === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${fmtInt(tin)} in / ${fmtInt(tout)} out tokens · ${nm} model${nm === 1 ? '' : 's'} · all-time history from /api/usage`
      + (kept.length ? ` (+ ${kept.length} older row${kept.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} kept from earlier syncs)` : ''),
    rows: kept.concat(fresh), meta: { buckets: n, period: 'all', msgs: fresh.reduce((a, r) => a + r.msgs, 0) } };
}
```

### Task 5: `parsePrometheus` + metrics-poll driver + vLLM + llama.cpp

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, `tests/checks/87b-wave2-local.mjs`. **Requires Task 4** (the `connectServer` `answered` branch — vLLM/llama.cpp HTTP errors on `/metrics` use it; the rows extension is not used here).

Research rows: **vLLM** (`http://localhost:8000`) — `GET /metrics` is on by default (CORS `--allowed-origins ['*']` by default): cumulative process-lifetime counters per `model_name` label — `vllm:prompt_tokens_total`, `vllm:generation_tokens_total`, `vllm:prompt_tokens_cached_total`, `vllm:request_success_total{finished_reason}`; exact usage in each response; no history endpoint; counters reset on restart. **llama.cpp (llama-server)** (`http://localhost:8080`) — `GET /metrics` only with `--metrics` (`LLAMA_ARG_ENDPOINT_METRICS=1`): `llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total`, `llamacpp:tokens_predicted_total` (no model label in single-model mode, a `model` label in router mode; no request counter); `GET /props` (model_path) and `GET /health` are on by default; CORS open by default (`--cors-origins *`) but `--tools` / `--agent` / `--mcp-servers-*` silently restrict CORS to localhost origins (a file:// page is origin `null` → blocked). Both: no account, no export, no log file worth parsing. Quality **exact**, label **"counts while this page is open"**: the generic **metrics-poll driver** (`App.startPoll` / `stopPoll` / `_pollTick`, baseline `LS 'poll:<id>' = { counters, at }`) diffs the counters and adds the positive deltas to today's rows (current hour); a counter lower than its baseline = restart → baseline reset, never negative; ticks are skipped while `document.hidden`. Polling starts on Validate / Sync / Refresh (no auto-fetch on page load, same rule as Ollama) and stops on disconnect / clear-data / tab close. Poll result contract (Shared contracts + one optional field): `{ counters: { [model]: { in, out, cr?, msgs? } }, label? }` — `msgs` is task-local and optional (vLLM's `request_success_total`; llama.cpp exposes none → 0).

```js
  //   Running totals after D: in=700 out=180 cr=30 msgs=3, one model (Llama), platform vLLM
  const LLAMA = 'meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct', QWEN = 'Qwen/Qwen3-8B';
  const vllmMetrics = (L, Q) => [
    '# HELP vllm:prompt_tokens_total Number of prefill tokens processed.',
    '# TYPE vllm:prompt_tokens_total counter',
    `vllm:prompt_tokens_total{model_name="${LLAMA}"} ${L.in}`,
    `vllm:prompt_tokens_total{model_name="${QWEN}"} ${Q.in}`,
    '# HELP vllm:generation_tokens_total Number of generation tokens processed.',
    '# TYPE vllm:generation_tokens_total counter',
    `vllm:generation_tokens_total{model_name="${LLAMA}"} ${L.out}`,
    `vllm:generation_tokens_total{model_name="${QWEN}"} ${Q.out}`,
    '# HELP vllm:prompt_tokens_cached_total Number of prefill tokens served from the prefix cache.',
    '# TYPE vllm:prompt_tokens_cached_total counter',
    `vllm:prompt_tokens_cached_total{model_name="${LLAMA}"} ${L.cr}`,
    '# HELP vllm:request_success_total Count of successfully processed requests.',
    '# TYPE vllm:request_success_total counter',
    `vllm:request_success_total{finished_reason="stop",model_name="${LLAMA}"} ${L.stop}`,
    `vllm:request_success_total{finished_reason="length",model_name="${LLAMA}"} ${L.len}`,
    '# HELP vllm:num_requests_running Number of requests currently running on GPU.',
    '# TYPE vllm:num_requests_running gauge',
    `vllm:num_requests_running{model_name="${LLAMA}"} 0.0`,
    '',
  ].join('\n');
  w('wave2/vllm-metrics-a.txt', vllmMetrics({ in: '1000.0', out: '200.0', cr: '100.0', stop: '3.0', len: '1.0' }, { in: '50.0', out: '10.0' }));
  w('wave2/vllm-metrics-b.txt', vllmMetrics({ in: '1.6e+03', out: '350.0', cr: '130.0', stop: '5.0', len: '1.0' }, { in: '50.0', out: '10.0' }));   // exponent form, as real exposition prints large counters
  w('wave2/vllm-metrics-c.txt', vllmMetrics({ in: '120.0', out: '30.0', cr: '0.0', stop: '1.0', len: '0.0' }, { in: '5.0', out: '1.0' }));
  w('wave2/vllm-metrics-d.txt', vllmMetrics({ in: '220.0', out: '60.0', cr: '0.0', stop: '2.0', len: '0.0' }, { in: '5.0', out: '1.0' }));

  // T5c — llama-server /metrics (--metrics on): no model label, no request counter. Expected counters: in=4321 out=876 under the /props model_path basename 'Qwen3-8B-Q4_K_M'
  w('wave2/llamacpp-metrics.txt', [
    '# HELP llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total Number of prompt tokens processed.',
    '# TYPE llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total counter',
    'llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total 4321',
    '# HELP llamacpp:tokens_predicted_total Number of generation tokens processed.',
    '# TYPE llamacpp:tokens_predicted_total counter',
    'llamacpp:tokens_predicted_total 876',
    '# HELP llamacpp:requests_processing Number of requests processing.',
    '# TYPE llamacpp:requests_processing gauge',
    'llamacpp:requests_processing 0',
    'llamacpp:kv_cache_usage_ratio 0.0123',
    '',
  ].join('\n'));
```

```js
/* ---------- metrics-poll helpers (vLLM / llama.cpp: Prometheus counters diffed by App._pollTick) ---------- */
/** Prometheus text exposition → [{ name, labels: {…}, value }]. '#' (HELP/TYPE/comment) and blank lines are skipped; label values are
 *  unescaped (\" \\ \n); values may be exponent floats, +Inf/-Inf/NaN; an optional trailing timestamp is ignored; unparseable lines are dropped. */
function parsePrometheus(text) {
  const out = [];
  const line = /^([a-zA-Z_:][a-zA-Z0-9_:]*)(?:\{(.*)\})?\s+(\S+)(?:\s+(-?\d+))?\s*$/;
  const label = /([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)="((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)"/g;
  for (const raw of String(text || '').split('\n')) {
    const s = raw.trim();
    if (!s || s[0] === '#') continue;
    const m = line.exec(s);
    if (!m) continue;
    const labels = {};
    if (m[2]) { let lm; label.lastIndex = 0; while ((lm = label.exec(m[2]))) labels[lm[1]] = lm[2].replace(/\\(["\\n])/g, (_, ch) => ch === 'n' ? '\n' : ch); }
    const v = m[3] === '+Inf' ? Infinity : m[3] === '-Inf' ? -Infinity : m[3] === 'NaN' ? NaN : Number(m[3]);
    if (m[3] !== 'NaN' && Number.isNaN(v)) continue;
    out.push({ name: m[1], labels, value: v });
  }
  return out;
}
/** vLLM metrics-poll: GET base + '/metrics' (Prometheus, on by default). Cumulative process-lifetime counters per model_name label:
 *  vllm:prompt_tokens_total (in), vllm:generation_tokens_total (out), vllm:prompt_tokens_cached_total (cr), vllm:request_success_total
 *  (msgs, summed over finished_reason). An HTTP error is the server's own answer → e.answered (connectServer shows it, no CORS guess). */
async function pollVllm(base) {
  const res = await fetch(base + '/metrics', { cache: 'no-store' });
  if (!res.ok) { const e = new Error(`HTTP ${res.status} on /metrics — is this vLLM? (vllm serve exposes /metrics by default)`); e.answered = true; throw e; }
  const FIELD = { 'vllm:prompt_tokens_total': 'in', 'vllm:generation_tokens_total': 'out', 'vllm:prompt_tokens_cached_total': 'cr', 'vllm:request_success_total': 'msgs' };
  const counters = {};
  for (const m of parsePrometheus(await res.text())) {
    const f = FIELD[m.name]; if (!f) continue;
    const model = String(m.labels.model_name || 'vllm');
    const s = counters[model] || (counters[model] = { in: 0, out: 0, cr: 0, msgs: 0 });
    s[f] += finTok(m.value);
  }
  const n = Object.keys(counters).length;
  return { counters, label: `${n} model${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}` };
}
/** vLLM validate = one successful /metrics parse ('<n> models' from the model_name labels; a fresh server has no counters until its first
 *  request → fall back to GET /v1/models for the count). No history exists on the server — the poll loop (startPoll) is the history. */
async function validateVllm(base) {
  const p = await pollVllm(base);
  let n = Object.keys(p.counters).length;
  if (!n) { try { const j = await fetchJSON(base + '/v1/models', {}); n = Array.isArray(j && j.data) ? j.data.length : 0; } catch {} }
  return { label: `${n} model${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`, detail: `${n} model${n === 1 ? '' : 's'} served · counting tokens from /metrics while this page is open (no history on the server)` };
}
/** llama-server model name: GET /props → model_alias or model_path basename without .gguf; null when /props is unavailable. */
async function llamaCppModelName(base) {
  try {
    const p = await fetchJSON(base + '/props', {});
    const mp = p && (p.model_alias || p.model_path || (p.default_generation_settings && p.default_generation_settings.model));
    if (mp) return String(mp).split(/[\\/]/).pop().replace(/\.gguf$/i, '') || null;
  } catch {}
  return null;
}
/** llama.cpp metrics-poll: GET base + '/metrics' (needs --metrics / LLAMA_ARG_ENDPOINT_METRICS=1): llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total (in),
 *  llamacpp:tokens_predicted_total (out). No request counter (msgs stay 0). Single-model mode has no model label → the model comes from
 *  GET /props ('llama.cpp' when that is unavailable); router mode's `model` label is used when present. */
async function pollLlamaCpp(base) {
  const res = await fetch(base + '/metrics', { cache: 'no-store' });
  if (!res.ok) { const e = new Error(`HTTP ${res.status} on /metrics — start llama-server with --metrics (LLAMA_ARG_ENDPOINT_METRICS=1)`); e.answered = true; throw e; }
  const FIELD = { 'llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total': 'in', 'llamacpp:tokens_predicted_total': 'out' };
  const hits = parsePrometheus(await res.text()).filter(m => FIELD[m.name]);
  const fallback = hits.some(m => !m.labels.model) ? (await llamaCppModelName(base)) || 'llama.cpp' : null;
  const counters = {};
  for (const m of hits) {
    const model = String(m.labels.model || fallback);
    const s = counters[model] || (counters[model] = { in: 0, out: 0 });
    s[FIELD[m.name]] += finTok(m.value);
  }
  const models = Object.keys(counters);
  return { counters, label: models.length ? models.join(', ') : 'no counters yet' };
}
/** llama.cpp validate: GET /props (model name; on by default) or GET /health; then ONE /metrics read so the card can say whether tokens will be
 *  counted (--metrics off → honest detail, validation still passes). No history on the server — the poll loop is the history. */
async function validateLlamaCpp(base) {
  const model = await llamaCppModelName(base);
  if (!model) await fetchJSON(base + '/health', {});   // neither /props nor /health → throws → connectServer's CORS / not-running handling
  let metricsNote;
  try { const p = await pollLlamaCpp(base); metricsNote = `counting tokens from /metrics while this page is open (${p.label})`; }
  catch (e) { metricsNote = `${e.message || e} — tokens are NOT counted until then`; }
  return { label: model || 'llama-server', detail: `${model ? 'model ' + model : 'llama-server healthy'} · ${metricsNote} · no history on the server` };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5 — App: metrics-poll driver + wiring.** Insert the driver directly BEFORE `  async pickFolder() {` (after Task 2 this lands right after `connectKey`, which sits between `connectServer` and `pickFolder` — that is the intended spot):

```js
  /* =================== metrics-poll driver (vLLM / llama.cpp: cumulative /metrics counters → today's rows) =================== */
  _pollTimers: {},   // connectorId → setInterval handle. Never persisted: a fresh page starts no poll until Validate / Sync / Refresh (no auto-fetch on load)
  /** Start (or restart) polling for a local-server entry that names a `poll` fn. Called by connectServer after a successful validate; idempotent.
   *  The first tick runs now: it seeds the baseline (nothing counted yet) or counts what accrued since the stored baseline. */
  startPoll(c) {
    if (!c || !c.poll) return;
    this.stopPoll(c);
    this._pollTimers[c.id] = setInterval(() => { this._pollTick(c); }, c.pollEvery || 60000);
    return this._pollTick(c);
  },
  /** Stop polling (disconnect, clear-data, restart). Bumps the poll generation so an in-flight tick cannot commit afterwards. */
  stopPoll(c) {
    const id = typeof c === 'string' ? c : c.id;
    if (this._pollTimers[id]) { clearInterval(this._pollTimers[id]); delete this._pollTimers[id]; }
    this._gen[id + ':poll'] = (this._gen[id + ':poll'] || 0) + 1;
  },
  /** One poll. Reads the cumulative counters, diffs them against the LS baseline ('poll:<id>' = { counters, at }), merges the positive deltas
   *  into TODAY's rows (this hour) under c.id, then stores the new baseline. A counter LOWER than its baseline = server restart → that field
   *  restarts from the current value (never negative; tokens between the restart and this tick are lost — the card says so). Skipped while the
   *  tab is hidden, so a backgrounded page does not pile hours of usage onto the minute it wakes up; a failed fetch (server gone, metrics off)
   *  keeps the last good state and tries again next tick. Hub label: '<serverLabel> · counts while this page is open'. */
  async _pollTick(c) {
    if (document.hidden) return;
    const fn = globalThis[c.poll];
    if (typeof fn !== 'function') return;
    const slot = c.id + ':poll';
    const gen = this._gen[slot] = this._gen[slot] || 0;   // read, don't bump — stopPoll / disconnect bump it to invalidate in-flight ticks
    const base = (LS.get('url:' + c.id, null) || c.baseUrl).replace(/\/+$/, '');
    let res;
    try { res = await fn(base); } catch { return; }
    if (gen !== this._gen[slot]) return;   // stopped / disconnected while the fetch was in flight
    const FIELDS = ['in', 'out', 'cw', 'cr', 'msgs'];
    const cur = {};
    for (const [model, cnt] of Object.entries((res && res.counters) || {})) { cur[model] = {}; for (const f of FIELDS) cur[model][f] = finTok(+(cnt && cnt[f]) || 0); }
    const key = 'poll:' + c.id, prev = LS.get(key, null);
    const d = todayKey(), hour = new Date().getHours();
    const rows = ((Store.sources[c.id] || {}).rows || []).slice();
    let merged = false;
    if (prev && prev.counters) {
      for (const [model, now] of Object.entries(cur)) {
        const was = prev.counters[model] || {};
        const delta = {}; let any = false;
        for (const f of FIELDS) { const b = finTok(+was[f] || 0); delta[f] = now[f] >= b ? now[f] - b : 0; if (delta[f] > 0) any = true; }
        if (!any) continue;
        let row = rows.find(r => r.d === d && r.hour === hour && r.platform === c.platform && r.model === model);
        if (!row) { row = { d, hour, platform: c.platform, model, in: 0, cw: 0, cr: 0, out: 0, msgs: 0 }; rows.push(row); }
        for (const f of FIELDS) row[f] += delta[f];
        merged = true;
      }
    }
    LS.set(key, { counters: cur, at: Date.now() });
    if (merged) { const meta = (Store.sources[c.id] || {}).meta || {}; Store.setSource(c.id, rows, { ...meta, polls: (meta.polls || 0) + 1, lastPoll: Date.now() }); }
    const st = Store.connectors[c.id] || {};
    Store.setConnector(c.id, { label: [st.serverLabel, 'counts while this page is open'].filter(Boolean).join(' · '), lastSync: Date.now() });
    if (merged) this.render();
  },

```

Research rows (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-16-llm-platform-research.json`): **GPT4All** — `http://localhost:4891/v1` (`GET /v1/models`, `POST /v1/chat/completions` with `usage{}`); server is OFF by default (Settings > Application > Advanced > Enable Local API Server), binds 127.0.0.1 only, no auth, sends `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` so a `file://` page can call it; no history endpoint; GUI chats are binary Qt `.chat` files without tokens; project dormant since early 2025 → **validate-only**. **Jan** — data folder `%APPDATA%\Jan\data` (macOS `~/Library/Application Support/Jan/data`, Linux `~/.local/share/Jan/data`, older `~/jan`; configurable in Settings > General) with `threads/<thread_id>/messages.jsonl` (one JSON message per line: role, content, model, timestamps, `metadata` with output `tokenCount`/`tokenSpeed`; prompt tokens never recorded) → **estimated**; Local API Server `http://127.0.0.1:1337` (`GET /v1/models`; CORS on by default; optional Bearer key) — live validate only, no history endpoint. **text-generation-webui** — `user_data/logs/chat/<character>/<id>.json` and `user_data/logs/instruct/<id>.json`: `{internal:[[user, assistant],…], visible, metadata:{"user_i":{timestamp}, "assistant_i":{timestamp, model_name, versions…}}}` — timestamps + model per turn, NO token counts → **estimated**; API `http://127.0.0.1:5000` (`--api`; `GET /v1/models`), CORS allows only `localhost`/`127.0.0.1` origins unless started with `--listen` or `--public-api`, so a `file://` page (`Origin: null`) is blocked; `/v1/billing/usage` is a stub returning 0. All three are `category:'local'` → `$0` by platform (`LOCAL_PLATFORMS`). Every estimated row carries `est:1`; local-server cards never auto-validate on load (button / Sync / Refresh only — see Plan 4a T15).

- [ ] **Step 4 — validators.** Insert directly BEFORE the line `async function fetchCopilotAll(token) {` (i.e. right after `validateOllama`'s closing `}`):

```js
/** GPT4All live validate: GET /v1/models on the app's Local API Server (off by default; CORS * when on). No history endpoint. */
async function validateGpt4All(base) {
  const j = await fetchJSON(base + '/v1/models', {});
  const n = Array.isArray(j && j.data) ? j.data.length : 0;
  return { label: `${n} models`, detail: `${n} models listed · validate-only: no usage history on the server and the app's .chat files carry no token counts` };
}
/** Jan live validate: GET /v1/models on the Local API Server (CORS on by default; an optional API key is NOT sent). No history endpoint —
 *  thread history comes from the data folder (parseJanThreads). */
async function validateJan(base) {
  const j = await fetchJSON(base + '/v1/models', {});
  const n = Array.isArray(j && j.data) ? j.data.length : 0;
  return { label: `${n} models`, detail: `${n} models listed · no usage history on the server — thread history (estimated) is read from the Jan data folder below` };
}
/** text-generation-webui live validate: GET /v1/models on the --api server (CORS-blocked from file:// unless --listen / --public-api).
 *  No history endpoint (/v1/billing/usage is a stub) — history comes from user_data/logs (parseTgwebuiLogs). */
async function validateTgwebui(base) {
  const j = await fetchJSON(base + '/v1/models', {});
  const n = Array.isArray(j && j.data) ? j.data.length : 0;
  return { label: `${n} models`, detail: `${n} models listed · no usage history on the server — chat/instruct logs (estimated) are read from the user_data folder below` };
}
```

```js
 *  (ms, or unix seconds in older builds), model?{id}, metadata?{ tokenCount | tokenSpeed{tokenCount}, model? } }. content is
 *  [{type:'text', text:{value}}] (or text as a string, or a plain string). Output = metadata tokenCount when present (Jan writes only
 *  the OUTPUT count), else estTok(text); input = estTok(previous user message in the thread) — always estimated → est:1 on every row.
 *  Model: message.model.id → metadata.model → metadata.assistant.model → 'jan'. sid = 'jan:<thread_id>' (folder name). */
async function parseJanThreads(files, onProgress, seen = new Set(), c) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); let msgs = 0; const k = { lines: 0, badLines: 0 };
  const list = files.filter(f => /(^|\/)messages\.jsonl$/i.test(f.path));
  const textOf = (content) => {
    if (typeof content === 'string') return content;
    if (!Array.isArray(content)) return '';
    return content.map(p => { if (!p) return ''; if (typeof p === 'string') return p; if (typeof p.text === 'string') return p.text; if (p.text && typeof p.text.value === 'string') return p.text.value; return ''; }).join('\n');
  };
  const idOf = (v) => (v && typeof v === 'object') ? (typeof v.id === 'string' ? v.id : '') : (typeof v === 'string' ? v : '');
  const when = (m, fallback) => {
    let v = m.created_at ?? m.created ?? m.updated_at ?? m.updated;
    if (typeof v === 'string' && /^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(v.trim())) v = +v;
    if (typeof v === 'number' && v > 0) return new Date(v < 1e12 ? v * 1000 : v);   // unix seconds vs milliseconds
    if (typeof v === 'string') { const d = new Date(v); if (!isNaN(d)) return d; }
    return new Date(fallback);
  };
  for (let fi = 0; fi < list.length; fi++) {
    const { path, file } = list[fi];
    const sid = 'jan:' + (path.split('/').slice(-2, -1)[0] || path);
    let lastUser = '';
    await eachJsonl(file, (m) => {
      const role = String(m.role || '').toLowerCase();
      const text = textOf(m.content);
      if (role === 'user') { lastUser = text; return; }
      if (role !== 'assistant') return;
      const md = m.metadata && typeof m.metadata === 'object' ? m.metadata : {};
      const tc = md.tokenCount ?? (md.tokenSpeed && typeof md.tokenSpeed === 'object' ? md.tokenSpeed.tokenCount : undefined);
      const out = tc != null && tc !== '' && Number.isFinite(+tc) && +tc >= 0 ? +tc : estTok(text.length);
      const model = idOf(m.model) || idOf(md.model) || idOf(md.assistant && md.assistant.model) || 'jan';
      const ts = when(m, file.lastModified);
      agg.add(dayKey(ts), (c && c.platform) || 'Jan', model, { in: estTok(lastUser.length), out, msgs: 1, est: 1 }, sid, ts.getHours());
      msgs++;
    }, k);
    if (onProgress) onProgress(fi + 1, list.length);
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), projects: [], meta: { files: list.length, msgs, lines: k.lines, badLines: k.badLines, addedIds: [] } };
}

/** text-generation-webui — user_data/logs/chat/<character>/<id>.json and logs/instruct/<id>.json (whole-file JSON):
 *  { internal: [[user, assistant], …], visible, metadata: { 'user_<i>': {timestamp}, 'assistant_<i>': {timestamp, model_name, versions?} } }.
 *  No token counts → in = estTok(user), out = estTok(assistant) per pair, est:1; model = metadata['assistant_i'].model_name || the platform
 *  name; timestamps are LOCAL time strings ('YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS') → parsed as local. The '<|BEGIN-VISIBLE-CHAT|>' greeting pair is
 *  not a generation and is skipped; only the current text of a regenerated turn counts (versions[] ignored). */
async function parseTgwebuiLogs(files, onProgress, seen = new Set(), c) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); let msgs = 0, lines = 0, badLines = 0;
  const list = files.filter(f => /(^|\/)logs\/(chat|instruct)\/.*\.json$/i.test(f.path));
  const parseTs = (s) => { if (typeof s !== 'string') return null; const d = new Date(s.trim().replace(' ', 'T')); return isNaN(d) ? null : d; };
  for (let fi = 0; fi < list.length; fi++) {
    const { path, file } = list[fi];
    let obj; try { obj = JSON.parse(await file.text()); } catch { badLines++; continue; }
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(obj.internal)) { badLines++; continue; }
    lines++;
    const md = obj.metadata && typeof obj.metadata === 'object' ? obj.metadata : {};
    const sid = 'tgwebui:' + path.replace(/\.json$/i, '');
    obj.internal.forEach((pair, i) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(pair)) return;
      const user = typeof pair[0] === 'string' ? pair[0] : '', reply = typeof pair[1] === 'string' ? pair[1] : '';
      if (user === '<|BEGIN-VISIBLE-CHAT|>') return;
      const am = md['assistant_' + i] || {}, um = md['user_' + i] || {};
      const ts = parseTs(am.timestamp) || parseTs(um.timestamp);
      agg.add(dayKey(ts || file.lastModified), (c && c.platform) || 'text-generation-webui', String(am.model_name || 'text-generation-webui'),
        { in: estTok(user.length), out: estTok(reply.length), msgs: 1, est: 1 }, sid, ts ? ts.getHours() : undefined);
      msgs++;
    });
    if (onProgress) onProgress(fi + 1, list.length);
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), projects: [], meta: { files: list.length, msgs, lines, badLines, addedIds: [] } };
}
```

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, `tests/checks/87c-wave2-keys.mjs`

Research row (`dataQuality: exact`, confidence high): any regular Fireworks key (`fw_…`) as Bearer + the account id → `GET https://api.fireworks.ai/v1/accounts/{account_id}/billingUsage?startTime=&endTime=&usageType=SERVERLESS|DEDICATED_DEPLOYMENT&groupBy=model_name&timezone=` → daily buckets with `promptTokens` / `completionTokens` **as JSON strings**; **max 31-day window per request**, daily granularity only, cached a few minutes. `GET …/billing/summary?granularity=DAILY&startTime&endTime` → rated USD line items by category with per-day `usageBuckets`. CORS `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` verified on both. `account_id` is shown in app.fireworks.ai (user supplies it). The exact bucket field names are not fully pinned in the docs → the parser reads them defensively (`usageBuckets[]` / `startTime` / `dimensions.model_name` / `promptTokens` / `completionTokens`; summary `lineItems[].usageBuckets[].amount|cost`). Request counts are not part of the export → `msgs` stays 0 (honest). Quality **exact** (tokens) + **billed** (USD).

```js
  // ---- Plan 4b (Wave 2) fixtures: API vendors ----
  // T7 — Fireworks billingUsage (tokens as JSON STRINGS; day = bucket start UTC; groupBy=model_name) + billing/summary (USD per day).
  //   Days are RELATIVE so the 90-day fetch window always covers them: A = 3 days ago, B = 40 days ago (a different 31-day window), Z = 200 days ago (outside the range → must be dropped).
  //   serverless: A llama in 12000 out 3400 · A qwen in 5000 out 900 · A llama promptTokens "abc" (rejected → 0) out 10 · B llama in 800 out 120 · Z 99999/99999 (dropped)
  //   dedicated:  A dep-1 in 2000 out 500
  //   Expected rows (serverless + dedicated): in = 12000+5000+0+800+2000 = 19800 · out = 3400+900+10+120+500 = 4930 · msgs = 0 (no request count in the export)
  //     4 day·model rows: A/llama (in 12000, out 3410), A/qwen, B/llama, A/dep-1 · model names lose the accounts/<acct>/models/ (serverless) and accounts/<acct>/deployments/ (dedicated → 'dep-1') prefixes
  //   summary: A serverless 1.84 + dedicated 4.50 = 6.34 · B 0.12 · Z 9.99 (dropped) → 2 billed days, total 6.46
  const fwDay = (n) => new Date(Date.now() - n * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const fwBucket = (n, model, p, c) => ({ startTime: fwDay(n) + 'T00:00:00Z', endTime: fwDay(n - 1) + 'T00:00:00Z', dimensions: { model_name: model }, promptTokens: p, completionTokens: c });
  w('wave2/fireworks/billingUsage-serverless.json', JSON.stringify({ usageBuckets: [
    fwBucket(3, 'accounts/fireworks/models/llama-v3p3-70b-instruct', '12000', '3400'),
    fwBucket(3, 'accounts/fireworks/models/qwen3-235b-a22b', '5000', '900'),
    fwBucket(3, 'accounts/fireworks/models/llama-v3p3-70b-instruct', 'abc', '10'),
    fwBucket(40, 'accounts/fireworks/models/llama-v3p3-70b-instruct', '800', '120'),
    fwBucket(200, 'accounts/fireworks/models/llama-v3p3-70b-instruct', '99999', '99999'),
  ] }, null, 1));
  w('wave2/fireworks/billingUsage-dedicated.json', JSON.stringify({ usageBuckets: [
    fwBucket(3, 'accounts/acme/deployments/dep-1', '2000', '500'),
  ] }, null, 1));
  w('wave2/fireworks/billing-summary.json', JSON.stringify({ lineItems: [
    { category: 'SERVERLESS', usageBuckets: [{ startTime: fwDay(3) + 'T00:00:00Z', amount: '1.84', currency: 'USD' }, { startTime: fwDay(40) + 'T00:00:00Z', amount: '0.12', currency: 'USD' }] },
    { category: 'DEDICATED_DEPLOYMENT', usageBuckets: [{ startTime: fwDay(3) + 'T00:00:00Z', amount: '4.50', currency: 'USD' }] },
    { category: 'TRAINING', usageBuckets: [{ startTime: fwDay(200) + 'T00:00:00Z', amount: '9.99', currency: 'USD' }] },
  ] }, null, 1));
```
