- [ ] **Step 5 — fetcher.** Insert directly BEFORE the line `async function fetchCopilotAll(token) {`:

```js
/* ---------- Fireworks AI: exact tokens (billingUsage) + billed USD (billing/summary) with a regular key ---------- */
/** fields.account = Fireworks account id (app.fireworks.ai). `days` back from today in ≤31-day half-open windows [s, e); two
 *  passes (SERVERLESS, DEDICATED_DEPLOYMENT); token fields are JSON strings; day = bucket start (UTC); model = dimensions.model_name
 *  without the accounts/<acct>/models/ (serverless) or accounts/<acct>/deployments/ (dedicated) prefix. A bucket is counted only if its start falls inside the window that requested it,
 *  so padded or cached responses never double count. Summary failure → billed null (tokens still land). */
async function fetchFireworks(key, fields, opts) {
  const account = String((fields && fields.account) || '').trim();
  if (!account) throw new Error('Enter your Fireworks account id (app.fireworks.ai → Settings → Account; it is also the first path segment in the console URL)');
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(account)) throw new Error('Account id may only contain letters, digits, - and _');
  const days = (opts && opts.days) || 90;
  const h = { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key };
  const base = 'https://api.fireworks.ai/v1/accounts/' + encodeURIComponent(account);
  const iso = (d) => d.toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, 'Z');
  const dayMs = 864e5, endAll = new Date();
  const startAll = new Date(Date.UTC(endAll.getUTCFullYear(), endAll.getUTCMonth(), endAll.getUTCDate()) - days * dayMs);
  const windows = [];
  for (let s = startAll.getTime(); s < endAll.getTime();) { const e = Math.min(s + 31 * dayMs, endAll.getTime()); windows.push([new Date(s), new Date(e)]); s = e; }
  const inWin = (t, s, e) => { const ms = Date.parse(t); return Number.isFinite(ms) && ms >= s.getTime() && ms < e.getTime(); };
  const modelOf = (b) => { const dims = b.dimensions || {}; const raw = dims.model_name || dims.modelName || b.model_name || b.modelName || b.model || 'fireworks (all models)'; return String(raw).replace(/^accounts\/[^/]+\/(models|deployments)\//, ''); };   // serverless 'accounts/fireworks/models/<m>' and dedicated 'accounts/<acct>/deployments/<dep>' both lose their resource prefix (the check expects 'dep-1')
  const agg = makeAgg();
  for (const usageType of ['SERVERLESS', 'DEDICATED_DEPLOYMENT']) {
    for (const [s, e] of windows) {
      const url = new URL(base + '/billingUsage');
      url.searchParams.set('startTime', iso(s)); url.searchParams.set('endTime', iso(e));
      url.searchParams.set('usageType', usageType); url.searchParams.set('groupBy', 'model_name'); url.searchParams.set('timezone', 'UTC');
      const j = await fetchJSON(url, h);
      for (const b of (j.usageBuckets || j.buckets || j.data || [])) {
        const t = b.startTime || b.start_time || b.bucketStart;
        if (!inWin(t, s, e)) continue;
        agg.add(dayKeyUTC(t), 'Fireworks AI', modelOf(b), { in: finTok(+b.promptTokens || 0), out: finTok(+b.completionTokens || 0), msgs: finTok(+(b.requests ?? b.numRequests ?? b.requestCount) || 0) });
      }
    }
  }
  let billed = null;
  try {
    const bm = new Map();
    for (const [s, e] of windows) {
      const url = new URL(base + '/billing/summary');
      url.searchParams.set('granularity', 'DAILY'); url.searchParams.set('startTime', iso(s)); url.searchParams.set('endTime', iso(e));
      const j = await fetchJSON(url, h);
      const items = Array.isArray(j.lineItems) ? j.lineItems : Array.isArray(j.items) ? j.items : [j];   // per-category line items, or buckets at the top level
      for (const it of items) for (const b of (it.usageBuckets || it.buckets || [])) {
        const t = b.startTime || b.start_time || b.bucketStart;
        if (!inWin(t, s, e)) continue;
        const raw = b.cost ?? b.amount ?? b.totalCost ?? b.total;
        const amt = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? parseFloat(raw.value ?? raw.amount) : parseFloat(raw);
        const d = dayKeyUTC(t);
        bm.set(d, (bm.get(d) || 0) + finAmt(amt));
      }
    }
    billed = [...bm.entries()].map(([d, amount]) => ({ d, amount })).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1);
  } catch (e) { console.warn('fireworks billing summary failed', e); }
  const rows = agg.rows();
  return { label: `account ${account} · last 4: ${key.slice(-4)}`, rows, billed,
    detail: `${rows.length} day·model rows${billed ? ` · ${billed.length} billed day${billed.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}` : ' · billing summary unavailable (tokens only)'} · last ${days} days in ${windows.length} windows`,
    meta: { days, account, windows: windows.length } };
}
```
(`parseFloat(undefined)` → NaN → `finAmt` → 0; `+'abc'` → NaN → `|| 0` → 0 — garbage never reaches totals. No `\u0001` in this code.)

### Task 8: `readZipEntries` + DeepSeek API (balance validate + monthly usage ZIP) + ZIP routing on Imports

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`; Create `tests/checks/87e-wave2-exports.mjs`

**Requires Task 2** (the key driver: `App.connectKey`, the key input `<c.id>-key`, `ensureSourceCard` for key kinds). Uses Task 2's names only; nothing is re-implemented here.

Research rows: **DeepSeek API** — `GET https://api.deepseek.com/user/balance` (`Authorization: Bearer sk-…`) → `{ is_available, balance_infos: [{ currency: 'CNY'|'USD', total_balance, granted_balance, topped_up_balance }] }` — balance only, string numbers, CORS verified (server echoes the `null` origin with allow-credentials) → browser-callable, **no usage history**. Exact history = `platform.deepseek.com/usage` → pick a month → **Export monthly data** → ZIP with `amount-*.csv` + `cost-*.csv`; the column layout is documented only by community parsers (verified against `github.com/shajanjp/deepseek-usage`: a **long** shape `utc_date, model, api_key_name, type, [price,] amount` with `type ∈ {input_cache_miss_tokens, input_cache_hit_tokens, output_tokens, request_count}`, `utc_date` as `YYYYMMDD` or `YYYY-MM-DD`; `deepseek-usage.xyz` accepts the ZIP or the CSV pair) — so the importer **sniffs headers** and accepts both that long shape and a wide per-column shape (`date, model, input…, cache hit…, output…`). Quality **exact** for the export; the key itself is validate-only. Assumptions stated in the code: in the wide shape the input column is the total prompt tokens and the cache-hit column a subset (uncached = input − hit, clamped at 0; a `…miss…` column, when present, is taken as uncached directly); in the long shape a `price` column is a per-unit price (`price × amount`, the community parser's reading) and is used for billed amounts ONLY when no `cost-*.csv` is in the same batch. Each monthly export is a separate file → rows **merge** by (day, model) and billed by day, so July never deletes June.

- [ ] **Step 1 — fixtures: tiny ZIP writer + ZIPs.** In `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, after `import path from 'node:path';` add `import zlib from 'node:zlib';` (skip if another task already added it). Directly BEFORE `export async function generate() {` add the module-level helpers:

```js
// ---- minimal ZIP writer for Wave 2 fixtures: local headers + central directory + EOCD, CRC32 (table), stored or deflate-raw entries ----
const CRC_TABLE = (() => { const t = new Uint32Array(256); for (let n = 0; n < 256; n++) { let c = n; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xEDB88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1; t[n] = c >>> 0; } return t; })();
const crc32 = (buf) => { let c = 0xFFFFFFFF; for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) c = CRC_TABLE[(c ^ buf[i]) & 0xFF] ^ (c >>> 8); return (c ^ 0xFFFFFFFF) >>> 0; };
/** files: [{ name, data: string|Buffer, deflate?: boolean, method?: number }] → Buffer holding a valid .zip (DOS date fixed at 2026-08-01;
 *  `method` overrides the stored/deflate choice to write an entry the reader must refuse, e.g. 12 = bzip2 — data is left raw). */
function zipBuffer(files) {
  const locals = [], centrals = []; let off = 0;
  const dosTime = 0, dosDate = ((2026 - 1980) << 9) | (8 << 5) | 1;
  for (const f of files) {
    const nameB = Buffer.from(f.name, 'utf8'), raw = Buffer.isBuffer(f.data) ? f.data : Buffer.from(f.data, 'utf8');
    const method = f.method != null ? f.method : (f.deflate ? 8 : 0), data = method === 8 ? zlib.deflateRawSync(raw) : raw, crc = crc32(raw);
    const lh = Buffer.alloc(30);
    lh.writeUInt32LE(0x04034b50, 0); lh.writeUInt16LE(20, 4); lh.writeUInt16LE(0x0800, 6); lh.writeUInt16LE(method, 8);
    lh.writeUInt16LE(dosTime, 10); lh.writeUInt16LE(dosDate, 12); lh.writeUInt32LE(crc, 14); lh.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 18); lh.writeUInt32LE(raw.length, 22);
    lh.writeUInt16LE(nameB.length, 26); lh.writeUInt16LE(0, 28);
    const ch = Buffer.alloc(46);
    ch.writeUInt32LE(0x02014b50, 0); ch.writeUInt16LE(20, 4); ch.writeUInt16LE(20, 6); ch.writeUInt16LE(0x0800, 8); ch.writeUInt16LE(method, 10);
    ch.writeUInt16LE(dosTime, 12); ch.writeUInt16LE(dosDate, 14); ch.writeUInt32LE(crc, 16); ch.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 20); ch.writeUInt32LE(raw.length, 24);
    ch.writeUInt16LE(nameB.length, 28); ch.writeUInt16LE(0, 30); ch.writeUInt16LE(0, 32); ch.writeUInt16LE(0, 34); ch.writeUInt16LE(0, 36); ch.writeUInt32LE(0, 38); ch.writeUInt32LE(off, 42);
    locals.push(lh, nameB, data); centrals.push(ch, nameB);
    off += lh.length + nameB.length + data.length;
  }
  const cdBuf = Buffer.concat(centrals), cdOff = off;
  const eocd = Buffer.alloc(22);
  eocd.writeUInt32LE(0x06054b50, 0); eocd.writeUInt16LE(0, 4); eocd.writeUInt16LE(0, 6); eocd.writeUInt16LE(files.length, 8); eocd.writeUInt16LE(files.length, 10);
  eocd.writeUInt32LE(cdBuf.length, 12); eocd.writeUInt32LE(cdOff, 16); eocd.writeUInt16LE(0, 20);
  return Buffer.concat([...locals, cdBuf, eocd]);
}
```
Then, inside `generate()`, directly BEFORE the `}` that closes it (the line above the blank line that precedes `if (process.argv[1] === import.meta.filename) await generate();`), append (`w` writes Buffers as well as strings):

```js
      n2: { id: 'n2', parent: 'n1', children: [], message: { id: 'm2', author: { role: 'assistant' }, create_time: gptSec('2026-08-10T09:00:05.000Z'), content: { content_type: 'text', parts: ['y'.repeat(80)] }, metadata: { model_slug: 'gpt-5.4' } } } } }]) },
```

```js
import fs from 'node:fs';

import { openDashboard, OUT } from '../lib/harness.mjs';

const read = (...p) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(OUT, ...p), 'utf8');
const b64 = (...p) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(OUT, ...p)).toString('base64');   // binary fixtures cross into the page as base64 → Uint8Array → File
const near = (a, b) => Math.abs(a - b) < 1e-9;

      try { await entries[2].bytes(); out.err = null; } catch (e) { out.err = e.message; }
```

- [ ] **Step 3 — `readZipEntries`.** Insert directly BEFORE the comment line `/* ---------- import router (drop zone) ---------- */`:

```js
/* ---------- ZIP reader (no library): DeepSeek monthly usage export, chat-app export archives ---------- */
/** Minimal ZIP reader. Reads the tail (EOCD + central directory) up front and each entry's bytes lazily from File.slice.
 *  Methods 0 (stored) and 8 (deflate-raw via DecompressionStream); anything else, encrypted entries (flag bit 0) and ZIP64
 *  (0xFFFFFFFF sizes/offsets) throw Error('ZIP entry uses an unsupported method — unzip it first'). Sizes come from the
 *  central directory, so entries written with a data descriptor (flag bit 3) read fine. Names are decoded as UTF-8.
 *  → [{ name, size, method, bytes(): Promise<Uint8Array>, text(): Promise<string> }] (directory entries are skipped). */
async function readZipEntries(file) {
  const size = file.size;
  if (size < 22) throw new Error('Not a ZIP file (too small)');
  const tailLen = Math.min(size, 65536 + 22);   // EOCD = 22 bytes + a comment of at most 64 KB
  const tail = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(size - tailLen, size).arrayBuffer());
  const dv = new DataView(tail.buffer, tail.byteOffset, tail.byteLength);
  let eocd = -1;
  for (let i = tail.length - 22; i >= 0; i--) { if (dv.getUint32(i, true) === 0x06054b50) { eocd = i; break; } }
  if (eocd < 0) throw new Error('Not a ZIP file (no end-of-central-directory record)');
  const count = dv.getUint16(eocd + 10, true), cdSize = dv.getUint32(eocd + 12, true), cdOff = dv.getUint32(eocd + 16, true);
  if (count === 0xFFFF || cdSize === 0xFFFFFFFF || cdOff === 0xFFFFFFFF) throw new Error('ZIP entry uses an unsupported method — unzip it first');   // ZIP64
  const cd = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(cdOff, cdOff + cdSize).arrayBuffer());
  const cdv = new DataView(cd.buffer, cd.byteOffset, cd.byteLength);
  const dec = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
  const entries = [];
  let p = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < count && p + 46 <= cd.length; i++) {
    if (cdv.getUint32(p, true) !== 0x02014b50) throw new Error('Not a ZIP file (bad central directory)');
    const flags = cdv.getUint16(p + 8, true), method = cdv.getUint16(p + 10, true);
    const csize = cdv.getUint32(p + 20, true), usize = cdv.getUint32(p + 24, true);
    const n = cdv.getUint16(p + 28, true), m = cdv.getUint16(p + 30, true), k = cdv.getUint16(p + 32, true);
    const lho = cdv.getUint32(p + 42, true);
    const name = dec.decode(cd.subarray(p + 46, p + 46 + n));
    p += 46 + n + m + k;
    if (name.endsWith('/')) continue;   // directory entry
    const unsupported = (flags & 1) !== 0 || csize === 0xFFFFFFFF || usize === 0xFFFFFFFF || (method !== 0 && method !== 8);
    const bytes = async () => {
      if (unsupported) throw new Error('ZIP entry uses an unsupported method — unzip it first');
      const lh = new DataView(await file.slice(lho, lho + 30).arrayBuffer());
      if (lh.getUint32(0, true) !== 0x04034b50) throw new Error('Not a ZIP file (bad local header)');
      const start = lho + 30 + lh.getUint16(26, true) + lh.getUint16(28, true);   // local name + extra lengths may differ from the central ones
      const blob = file.slice(start, start + csize);
      if (method === 0) return new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());
      return new Uint8Array(await new Response(blob.stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw'))).arrayBuffer());
    };
    entries.push({ name, size: usize, method, bytes, text: async () => new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(await bytes()) });
  }
  return entries;
}
```

```js
/** DeepSeek API — GET /user/balance: validate + remaining balance only (no usage history exists on the API; history comes
 *  from the monthly usage ZIP through Imports). Numbers are strings; the currency may be CNY — both are shown as reported. */
async function fetchDeepSeekBalance(key, fields, opts) {
  const j = await fetchJSON('https://api.deepseek.com/user/balance', { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key, 'Accept': 'application/json' });
  const infos = Array.isArray(j && j.balance_infos) ? j.balance_infos : [];
  const money = (v) => { const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n.toFixed(2) : String(v == null ? '?' : v); };
  const label = infos.length ? `balance ${money(infos[0].total_balance)} ${infos[0].currency || ''}`.trim() : (j && j.is_available === false ? 'no balance' : 'validated');
  const detail = (infos.length ? infos.map(b => `${b.currency || '?'}: total ${money(b.total_balance)} (granted ${money(b.granted_balance)}, topped up ${money(b.topped_up_balance)})`).join(' · ') : 'key accepted')
    + (j && j.is_available === false ? ' · account not available for API calls (balance exhausted?)' : '')
    + ' · no usage history on this endpoint — drop the monthly usage ZIP (platform.deepseek.com/usage → Export monthly data) on Imports for exact tokens and billed amounts';
  return { label, detail };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5 — DeepSeek CSV parsers + ZIP parser.** Insert directly BEFORE the comment block that starts `/* =================================================================\n * 4) Provider API payloads (fetched directly or imported as JSON files)` (anchor line: ` * 4) Provider API payloads (fetched directly or imported as JSON files)`; insert above the `/* ====` line that precedes it):

```js
/* DeepSeek API monthly usage export (platform.deepseek.com/usage → Export monthly data → ZIP: amount-YYYY-MM.csv + cost-YYYY-MM.csv).
 * The layout is documented only by community parsers (github.com/shajanjp/deepseek-usage, deepseek-usage.xyz) and has changed once
 * (utc_date YYYY-MM-DD → YYYYMMDD), so both files are sniffed by header. Two shapes are accepted:
 *   long (seen in the wild): utc_date, model, api_key_name, type, [price,] amount — type ∈ input_cache_miss_tokens | input_cache_hit_tokens |
 *        output_tokens | request_count; one row per (day, model, key, type). miss → in, hit → cr, output → out, request_count → msgs.
 *        A price column is read as a PER-UNIT price (price × amount; the community reading) and only used when no cost CSV is present.
 *   wide: date, model, input/prompt…, cache-hit…, output/completion…, [requests]. ASSUMPTION: the input column is the TOTAL prompt
 *        tokens and cache-hit a subset → uncached in = input − hit (clamped at 0); a …miss… column, when present, is uncached directly.
 * `head` = header cells normalized to lowercase_with_underscores. Days are UTC days (the export's own granularity). */
function isDeepSeekAmountCSV(head, fileName) {
  const has = (re) => head.some(h => re.test(h));
  if (!has(/date|^day$/) || !has(/^model/)) return false;
  if (has(/^type$/) && has(/^amount$/)) return true;                                   // long shape
  return has(/input|prompt/) && has(/output|completion/) && (has(/cache.*hit|hit/) || /(^|\/)amount-[^/]*\.csv$/i.test(String(fileName || '')));   // wide: a cache-hit column (or the export's file name) keeps generic CSVs out
}
function isDeepSeekCostCSV(head, fileName) {
  const has = (re) => head.some(h => re.test(h));
  if (/(^|\/)cost-[^/]*\.csv$/i.test(String(fileName || '')) && has(/date/)) return true;
  return has(/date|^day$/) && has(/^model/) && has(/cost|spend|bill|^total/) && !has(/token|platform|^type$/);
}
const deepSeekDay = (raw) => { const s = String(raw || '').trim(); if (/^\d{8}$/.test(s)) return s.slice(0, 4) + '-' + s.slice(4, 6) + '-' + s.slice(6, 8); if (RE_DATEONLY.test(s)) return s; return dayKeyUTC(s); };
function parseDeepSeekAmountCSV(text) {
  const rows = parseCSV(text);
  if (rows.length < 2) throw new Error('Empty CSV');
  const head = rows[0].map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, ''));
  const find = (re) => head.findIndex(h => re.test(h));
  const iDate = find(/date|^day$/), iModel = find(/^model/), iType = find(/^type$/), iAmount = find(/^amount$/), iPrice = find(/^price/),
        iMiss = find(/miss/), iIn = iMiss >= 0 ? iMiss : find(/input|prompt/), iHit = find(/cache.*hit|hit/), iOut = find(/output|completion/), iReq = find(/request|count|calls/);
  if (iDate < 0 || iModel < 0) throw new Error('Not a DeepSeek usage CSV (needs date + model columns)');
  const long = iType >= 0 && iAmount >= 0;
  const agg = makeAgg(); const billed = new Map(); let n = 0, rejected = 0;
  const num = (c, i) => { if (i < 0) return 0; const raw = String(c[i] ?? '').trim(); if (!raw) return 0; const v = Number(raw.replace(/[,\s]/g, '')); if (Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 0) return v; rejected++; return 0; };
  for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const c = rows[r]; const d = deepSeekDay(c[iDate]); if (!d) { rejected++; continue; }
    const model = String(c[iModel] || '').trim() || 'deepseek';
    if (long) {
      const type = String(c[iType] || '').trim().toLowerCase(), amt = num(c, iAmount), t = { in: 0, cr: 0, out: 0, msgs: 0 };
      if (/miss/.test(type)) t.in = amt; else if (/hit/.test(type)) t.cr = amt; else if (/output|completion/.test(type)) t.out = amt; else if (/request|count/.test(type)) t.msgs = amt; else if (/input|prompt/.test(type)) t.in = amt;
      else continue;   // an unknown type (a future reasoning_tokens row?) is skipped, not an error
      agg.add(d, 'DeepSeek API', model, t);
      if (iPrice >= 0 && !t.msgs) { const p = Number(String(c[iPrice] ?? '').trim()); if (Number.isFinite(p) && p >= 0 && amt) billed.set(d, (billed.get(d) || 0) + p * amt); }
      n++;
    } else {
      const hit = num(c, iHit), inRaw = num(c, iIn);
      agg.add(d, 'DeepSeek API', model, { in: iMiss >= 0 ? inRaw : Math.max(0, inRaw - hit), cr: hit, out: num(c, iOut), msgs: iReq >= 0 ? num(c, iReq) : 1 });
      n++;
    }
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), billed: [...billed.entries()].map(([d, amount]) => ({ d, amount })).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1), meta: { rows: n, rejected, hasPrice: iPrice >= 0, shape: long ? 'long' : 'wide' } };
}
function parseDeepSeekCostCSV(text) {
  const rows = parseCSV(text);
  if (rows.length < 2) throw new Error('Empty CSV');
  const head = rows[0].map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, ''));
  const find = (re) => head.findIndex(h => re.test(h));
  const iDate = find(/date|^day$/), iCost = find(/cost|spend|bill|^total/), iCur = find(/currency/);
  if (iDate < 0 || iCost < 0) throw new Error('Not a DeepSeek cost CSV (needs date + cost columns)');
  const billed = new Map(); let n = 0, rejected = 0, currency = '';
  for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const c = rows[r]; const d = deepSeekDay(c[iDate]); if (!d) { rejected++; continue; }
    const raw = String(c[iCost] ?? '').trim(); if (raw === '') continue;
    const v = Number(raw.replace(/[,\s$¥]/g, '')); if (!Number.isFinite(v)) { rejected++; continue; }   // money: negatives allowed (credits)
    billed.set(d, (billed.get(d) || 0) + v); n++;
    if (iCur >= 0 && !currency && c[iCur]) currency = String(c[iCur]).trim();
  }
  return { billed: [...billed.entries()].map(([d, amount]) => ({ d, amount })).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1), meta: { rows: n, rejected, currency } };
}
/** The whole monthly ZIP (entries from readZipEntries): every .csv entry is classified by name or header; rows from all amount
 *  files are re-aggregated (two amount files may share a day/model); billed = the cost CSVs (the vendor's own figure) when present,
 *  else price × amount from the amount file, else none. meta.files === 0 ⇒ "this is not a DeepSeek usage ZIP". */
async function parseDeepSeekUsageZip(entries) {
  const norm = (cells) => cells.map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, ''));
  const rowsAll = [], priceBilled = new Map(), costBilled = new Map(); let n = 0, rejected = 0, amountFiles = 0, costFiles = 0, currency = '';
  for (const e of entries) {
    if (!/\.csv$/i.test(e.name) || e.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) continue;
    const text = await e.text();
    const head = norm(parseCSV(text.split(/\r?\n/, 1)[0])[0] || []);
    if (isDeepSeekAmountCSV(head, e.name)) { const r = parseDeepSeekAmountCSV(text); rowsAll.push(...r.rows); for (const b of r.billed) priceBilled.set(b.d, (priceBilled.get(b.d) || 0) + b.amount); n += r.meta.rows; rejected += r.meta.rejected; amountFiles++; }
    else if (isDeepSeekCostCSV(head, e.name)) { const r = parseDeepSeekCostCSV(text); for (const b of r.billed) costBilled.set(b.d, (costBilled.get(b.d) || 0) + b.amount); rejected += r.meta.rejected; costFiles++; if (r.meta.currency && !currency) currency = r.meta.currency; }
  }
  const agg = makeAgg(); for (const r of rowsAll) agg.add(r.d, r.platform, r.model, r);
  const billedMap = costFiles ? costBilled : priceBilled;
  return { rows: agg.rows(), billed: [...billedMap.entries()].map(([d, amount]) => ({ d, amount })).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1),
    meta: { rows: n, rejected, files: amountFiles + costFiles, amountFiles, costFiles, currency, billedFrom: costFiles ? 'cost CSV' : (priceBilled.size ? 'price column' : 'none') } };
}
```

```js
  /** One parsed JSON document → its import branch. `f` = { name, size } (a File, or a ZIP-entry wrapper — name then reads
   *  "archive.zip/entry.json"). Pushes its own note. Returns the number of imports (0 = recognized but nothing landed, e.g. an
   *  all-empty report) or null when the shape is not recognized (the caller words that: loose file vs. ZIP entry). */
  async _importJson(obj, f, notes) {
    const kind = sniffJSON(obj, f.name);
    let imported = 0;
    if (kind === 'chatgpt-export' || kind === 'chatgpt-export-wrapped') {
      const conv = kind === 'chatgpt-export' ? obj : obj.conversations;
      // big exports parse off the main thread so the tab doesn't freeze; small ones stay
      // inline — worker startup (Blob + postMessage round trip) isn't worth it for them.
      const res = f.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024 ? await parseInWorker('chatgpt', JSON.stringify(conv)) : parseChatGPTExport(conv);
      Store.setSource('chatgpt', res.rows, res.meta);
      notes.push(`${f.name}: ChatGPT export — ${res.meta.convs} conversations, ${fmtInt(res.meta.msgs)} replies (estimated tokens)`);
      imported++;
    } else if (kind === 'claudeai-export') {
      const assumedModel = $('assumed-claude-model').value;
      const res = f.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024 ? await parseInWorker('claudeai', JSON.stringify(obj), assumedModel) : parseClaudeAiExport(obj, assumedModel);
      Store.setSource('claudeai', res.rows, res.meta);
      notes.push(`${f.name}: Claude.ai export — ${res.meta.convs} conversations, ${fmtInt(res.meta.msgs)} replies (estimated tokens)`);
      imported++;
    } else if (kind === 'gemini-takeout') {
      const res = parseGeminiTakeout(obj);
      Store.setSource('gemini-takeout', res.rows, res.meta);
      notes.push(`${f.name}: Gemini Takeout — ${fmtInt(res.meta.msgs)} prompts (estimated tokens${res.meta.skipped ? `; ${res.meta.skipped} non-Gemini/undated record(s) skipped` : ''})`);
      imported++;
    } else if (kind === 'anthropic-usage') {
      const res = parseAnthropicUsage(obj);
      this._gen.anthropic++;   // an import supersedes any in-flight fetch — it must not overwrite this
      Store.setSource('anthropic', res.rows, {});
      notes.push(`${f.name}: Anthropic usage report — ${res.rows.length} rows`);
      imported++;
    } else if (kind === 'anthropic-cost') {
      this._gen.anthropic++;   // an import supersedes any in-flight fetch — it must not overwrite this
      Store.setBilled('anthropic', parseAnthropicCost(obj));
      notes.push(`${f.name}: Anthropic billed costs imported`);
      imported++;
    } else if (kind === 'openai-usage') {
      const res = parseOpenAIUsage(obj);
      this._gen.openai++;   // an import supersedes any in-flight fetch — it must not overwrite this
      Store.setSource('openai', res.rows, {});
      notes.push(`${f.name}: OpenAI usage report — ${res.rows.length} rows`);
      imported++;
    } else if (kind === 'openai-cost') {
      this._gen.openai++;   // an import supersedes any in-flight fetch — it must not overwrite this
      Store.setBilled('openai', parseOpenAICost(obj));
      notes.push(`${f.name}: OpenAI billed costs imported`);
      imported++;
    } else if (kind === 'empty-report') {
      notes.push(`${f.name}: recognized usage/cost report, but every day in it is empty — nothing to import`);
    } else {
      return null;
    }
    return imported;
  },
```

  2. **DeepSeek landing closure.** Directly AFTER the line `    let imported = 0;   // count of successful import branches — only these justify a connected 'imports' card` insert:
```js
    // DeepSeek API usage export (monthly ZIP, or its loose amount-/cost- CSVs): every month is a separate export, so rows MERGE by
    // (day, model) and billed by day — importing August never deletes July. Rows live under the deepseek connector's srcId
    // 'deepseek-usage-zip' (platform 'DeepSeek API'), not under Imports; the deepseek card reads connected through the export alone.
    const landDeepSeek = (res, label) => {
      const keyOf = (r) => JSON.stringify([r.d, r.model]);
      const prev = Store.sources['deepseek-usage-zip'] || { rows: [], meta: {} };
      if (res.rows.length) {
        const merged = new Map(prev.rows.map(r => [keyOf(r), r]));
        for (const r of res.rows) merged.set(keyOf(r), r);
        Store.setSource('deepseek-usage-zip', [...merged.values()], { buckets: merged.size, rows: ((prev.meta || {}).rows || 0) + res.meta.rows, rejected: res.meta.rejected, files: ((prev.meta || {}).files || 0) + res.meta.files, currency: res.meta.currency || (prev.meta || {}).currency || '' });
      }
      if (res.billed.length) {
        const bm = new Map(Store.billedRows('deepseek-usage-zip').map(b => [b.d, b]));
        for (const b of res.billed) bm.set(b.d, b);   // same-day re-import replaces
        Store.setBilled('deepseek-usage-zip', [...bm.values()].sort((a, b2) => a.d < b2.d ? -1 : 1));
      }
      if (!Store.connectors.deepseek) Store.setConnector('deepseek', { label: 'usage export (no key)', lastSync: Date.now(), err: null });
      const what = res.rows.length ? `${fmtInt(res.meta.rows)} rows (exact tokens; months merge by day/model)` : 'no token rows';
      const money = res.billed.length ? ` + billed amounts from the ${res.meta.billedFrom || 'cost CSV'}${res.meta.currency ? ' in ' + res.meta.currency + (res.meta.currency !== 'USD' ? ' (shown as-is in the $ fields)' : '') : ''}`
        : (res.rows.length ? ' — no cost file: drop the month\'s cost-*.csv too for billed amounts' : '');
      notes.push(`${label}: DeepSeek API usage export — ${what}${money}` + (res.meta.rejected ? ` — ${res.meta.rejected} value(s) rejected (non-numeric, negative or undated)` : ''));
      imported++;
    };
```
  3. **ZIP branch.** Directly AFTER the line `        if (f.name.endsWith('.jsonl')) { jsonlFiles.push({ path: f.name, file: f }); continue; }` insert:
```js
        if (/\.zip$/i.test(f.name)) {
          // ZIP archives, read in-page (no library): DeepSeek's monthly usage export (amount-*.csv + cost-*.csv) and chat-app exports
          // as downloaded (ChatGPT / Claude.ai conversations.json, Perplexity thread JSON, a DeepSeek chat archive). Entries we do not
          // know (user.json, chat.html, …) are skipped quietly; an archive with nothing we know gets one honest note.
          const entries = await readZipEntries(f);
          let handled = 0;
          const ds = await parseDeepSeekUsageZip(entries);
          if (ds.meta.files) { landDeepSeek(ds, `${f.name} (${ds.meta.amountFiles} amount + ${ds.meta.costFiles} cost CSV)`); handled++; }
          for (const e of entries) {
            if (!/\.json$/i.test(e.name) || e.size > 200 * 1024 * 1024) continue;
            if (/(^|\/)shared_conversations\.json$/i.test(e.name)) continue;   // ChatGPT's shared-links file has conversations.json's shape and would replace it
            let obj; try { obj = JSON.parse(await e.text()); } catch (err) { notes.push(`${f.name}/${e.name}: failed — ${err.message || err}`); continue; }
            const r = await this._importJson(obj, { name: f.name + '/' + e.name, size: e.size }, notes);
            if (r === null) continue;
            handled++; imported += r;
          }
          if (!handled) notes.push(`${f.name}: ZIP — no recognized files (${entries.length} ${entries.length === 1 ? 'entry' : 'entries'})`);
          continue;
        }
```

### Task 9: Claude Enterprise Analytics (usage_report + cost_report) + `parseAnthropicCost` cents fix

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, `tests/checks/87c-wave2-keys.mjs`, `tests/checks/80-race.mjs`

**Requires Task 2** (key driver; `admin-key` kind routes through `App.connectKey`).

Research row (Claude.ai entry, `admin-key` method): the **Claude Enterprise Analytics API** — Enterprise orgs only (NOT Pro / Max / Team). A primary owner creates an Analytics API key at claude.ai → Organization settings → API (scope `read:analytics`). Endpoints under `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/organizations/analytics/` — `usage_report` (daily, per model: `uncached_input_tokens`, `cache_read_input_tokens`, `cache_creation: { ephemeral_5m_input_tokens, ephemeral_1h_input_tokens }`, `output_tokens`, `requests`) and `cost_report` (`amount` **decimal string in cents**, `currency`, `model`, `cost_type`, `token_type`), both `{ data:[{ starting_at, ending_at, results:[…] }], has_more, next_page }`. Headers `x-api-key` + `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01` (+ `anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access: true` for a browser call, like the Anthropic Admin card). `starting_at` may not be earlier than `2026-01-01T00:00:00Z`; 60 rpm/org. Quality **exact** tokens + **billed** USD (cost rows exist only on usage-based plans).

**Cents fix:** Anthropic's Admin `cost_report` and the Analytics `cost_report` both document `amount` as "decimal string in lowest units (cents)". `parseAnthropicCost` has been treating string/number amounts as dollars (100× too high). OpenAI-style object amounts `{ value, currency }` are already in currency units and stay as-is.

```js
  // T9 — Claude Enterprise Analytics: usage_report + cost_report (Anthropic bucket shape). Days RELATIVE: B = 45 days ago (window 2 of 3), C = 10 days ago, A = 2 days ago (both in window 3 → the stub paginates them).
  //   usage: B sonnet in 2000 cr 100 cw 0 out 900 req 20 · C opus in 300 cr 0 cw 0 out 250 req 3 · A sonnet in 1000 cr 400 cw 150+50=200 out 700 req 12

  //   cost (CENTS as decimal strings): B '30' → 0.30 · C '15.5' → 0.155 · A '184.50' → 1.845 → total 2.30 (= 230 cents)
  const ceDay = (n) => new Date(Date.now() - n * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10) + 'T00:00:00Z';
  const ceBucket = (n, results) => ({ starting_at: ceDay(n), ending_at: ceDay(n - 1), results });
  const ceUse = (model, u, cr, c5, c1, out, req) => ({ model, uncached_input_tokens: u, cache_read_input_tokens: cr, cache_creation: { ephemeral_5m_input_tokens: c5, ephemeral_1h_input_tokens: c1 }, output_tokens: out, requests: req });

    ceBucket(2, [ceUse('claude-sonnet-4-5', 1000, 400, 150, 50, 700, 12)]),

  w('wave2/claude-enterprise/cost_report.json', JSON.stringify({ data: [

    ceBucket(2, [{ amount: '184.50', currency: 'USD', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-5', cost_type: 'tokens', token_type: 'uncached_input_tokens' }]),
  ], has_more: false, next_page: null }, null, 1));
```

- [ ] **Step 5 — parsers.** In `AI Usage Dashboard.html` replace the whole `parseAnthropicUsage` function (from `function parseAnthropicUsage(payload) {` through its closing `}` and the blank line before `function parseAnthropicCost(payload) {`) and the whole `parseAnthropicCost` function (through its closing `}` before `function parseOpenAIUsage(payload) {`) with:

```js
/** Anthropic bucket shape — Admin usage_report/messages AND the Enterprise Analytics usage_report (identical fields; the
 *  Analytics report also carries `requests`). `platform` = the row's platform: 'Anthropic API' (default, Admin card) or
 *  'Claude Enterprise (org)' (Analytics card) — never mixed, each card is its own source. */
function parseAnthropicUsage(payload, platform = 'Anthropic API') {
  const agg = makeAgg();
  const data = payload && payload.data;
  if (!Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error('Not an Anthropic usage report');
  for (const bucket of data) {
    const d = dayKeyUTC(bucket.starting_at || bucket.ending_at);
    for (const r of bucket.results || []) {
      const model = r.model || 'claude (all models)';
      let cw = 0;
      if (typeof r.cache_creation === 'object' && r.cache_creation) {
        cw = Object.values(r.cache_creation).reduce((a, b) => a + (typeof b === 'number' ? b : 0), 0);
      } else cw = r.cache_creation_input_tokens || 0;
      agg.add(d, platform, model, {
        in: finTok(r.uncached_input_tokens ?? r.input_tokens ?? 0),
        cw: finTok(cw), cr: finTok(r.cache_read_input_tokens || 0),
        out: finTok(r.output_tokens || 0),
        msgs: finTok(r.num_requests || r.request_count || r.requests || 0)
      });
    }
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows() };
}

/** Anthropic cost_report (Admin and Enterprise Analytics): `amount` is a DECIMAL STRING IN THE CURRENCY'S LOWEST UNITS
 *  (cents for USD) per the docs — "123.45" = $1.2345 — so string/number amounts are divided by 100. An OpenAI-style
 *  object amount { value, currency } is already in currency units and is taken as-is (the import router feeds both shapes here). */
function parseAnthropicCost(payload) {
  const billed = new Map();
  const data = payload && payload.data;
  if (!Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error('Not an Anthropic cost report');
  for (const bucket of data) {
    const d = dayKeyUTC(bucket.starting_at || bucket.ending_at);
    for (const r of bucket.results || []) {
      let amt = 0;
      if (r.amount && typeof r.amount === 'object') amt = parseFloat(r.amount.value) || 0;   // {value} → dollars as-is
      else amt = (parseFloat(r.amount) || 0) / 100;                                         // "cents" string / number → dollars
      billed.set(d, (billed.get(d) || 0) + finAmt(amt));
    }
  }
  return [...billed.entries()].map(([d, amount]) => ({ d, amount })).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7 — fetcher.** Insert directly BEFORE the line `async function fetchOpenAIAll(key, days) {`:

```js
/* ---------- Claude Enterprise Analytics API (Enterprise orgs; Analytics key, scope read:analytics) ---------- */
/** Key-driver fetcher: usage_report + cost_report under /v1/organizations/analytics/, daily buckets grouped by model, fetched in
 *  ≤31-day half-open windows [starting_at, ending_at) aligned to UTC midnight (today included), each window paginated via
 *  has_more/next_page. starting_at is clamped to 2026-01-01T00:00:00Z (the API refuses earlier dates). Same headers as the
 *  Anthropic Admin card. Rows carry platform 'Claude Enterprise (org)'; cost amounts are cents (parseAnthropicCost divides). */
async function fetchClaudeEnterprise(key, fields, opts) {
  const days = (opts && opts.days) || 90;
  const headers = { 'x-api-key': key, 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01', 'anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access': 'true' };
  const dayMs = 864e5, now = new Date();
  const todayUTC = Date.UTC(now.getUTCFullYear(), now.getUTCMonth(), now.getUTCDate());
  const floor = Date.parse('2026-01-01T00:00:00Z');
  const startAll = Math.max(todayUTC - days * dayMs, floor);
  const endAll = todayUTC + dayMs;   // through the end of today (UTC)
  const iso = (ms) => new Date(ms).toISOString().slice(0, 10) + 'T00:00:00Z';
  const windows = [];
  for (let s = startAll; s < endAll;) { const e = Math.min(s + 31 * dayMs, endAll); windows.push([s, e]); s = e; }
  const pull = async (report) => {
    const merged = { data: [] };
    for (const [s, e] of windows) {
      let page = null;
      for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
        const url = new URL('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/organizations/analytics/' + report);
        url.searchParams.set('starting_at', iso(s)); url.searchParams.set('ending_at', iso(e));
        url.searchParams.set('bucket_width', '1d');
        url.searchParams.append('group_by[]', 'model');
        url.searchParams.set('limit', '31');
        if (page) url.searchParams.set('page', page);
        const j = await fetchJSON(url, headers);
        merged.data.push(...(j.data || []));
        if (!j.has_more || !j.next_page) break;
        page = j.next_page;
      }
    }
    return merged;
  };
  const usage = parseAnthropicUsage(await pull('usage_report'), 'Claude Enterprise (org)');
  let billed = null;
  try { billed = parseAnthropicCost(await pull('cost_report')); } catch (e) { console.warn('claude enterprise cost report failed', e); }
  const since = iso(startAll).slice(0, 10);
  return { label: 'Analytics key · last 4: ' + key.slice(-4), rows: usage.rows, billed,
    detail: `${usage.rows.length} usage rows${billed ? (billed.length ? ' + billed costs' : ' · no cost rows (seat-based plan, or no usage-based charges yet)') : ' · cost report unavailable'} · since ${since} in ${windows.length} window${windows.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`,
    meta: { days, since, windows: windows.length } };
}
```

Research row (`Azure OpenAI (Microsoft Foundry)`): no API-key path — everything is an Entra ID bearer token that a `file://` page cannot acquire itself (no valid redirect URI), so the user pastes `az account get-access-token --resource https://management.azure.com --query accessToken -o tsv` (about 1 h). Tokens: Azure Monitor metrics `GET https://management.azure.com/{resourceId}/providers/microsoft.insights/metrics?api-version=2024-02-01` — exact per-model token totals (`ProcessedPromptTokens` / `GeneratedTokens`, and the newer `InputTokens` / `OutputTokens`), dimension `ModelName`, 93-day retention, CORS `*` (probed). Cost: `POST https://management.azure.com/{scope}/providers/Microsoft.CostManagement/query?api-version=2025-03-01` (ActualCost, Custom timeframe, Daily, PreTaxCost Sum, filter ServiceName In [Cognitive Services]) → `PreTaxCost, UsageDate (yyyymmdd), Currency` rows; data lags 8–24 h; CORS `*`.
```js
/* ---------- cloud-token fetchers (Azure / Vertex / Bedrock): pasted CLI token or IAM key, session-only, one fetcher per platform ---------- */
/** Cloud REST call for the cloud-token fetchers: JSON in → JSON out. 401 → auth.expired (the platform's "mint a new token" message,
 *  verbatim), 403 → auth.denied (+ the server's own reason when it gives one), any other non-2xx → 'HTTP <status> — <server message>'.
 *  The bearer token / SigV4 signature travels only inside this request — nothing is logged or stored. */
async function cloudJSON(url, init = {}, auth = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(url, init);
  if (res.ok) return res.status === 204 ? {} : res.json();
  let detail = '';
  try {
    const j = await res.json();
    detail = String((j && j.error && (typeof j.error === 'string' ? j.error : (j.error.message || j.error.code || j.error.type))) || (j && (j.message || j.Message || j.__type || j.error_description)) || '').slice(0, 140);
  } catch {}
  if (res.status === 401 && auth.expired) throw new Error(auth.expired);
  if (res.status === 403 && auth.denied) throw new Error(auth.denied + (detail ? ` (${detail})` : ''));
  throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}${detail ? ' — ' + detail : ''}`);
}

/** Azure OpenAI (Microsoft Foundry) — cloud-token fetcher. token: Entra access token (az account get-access-token --resource
 *  https://management.azure.com), fields: { resources: one Cognitive Services resource id per line, scope?: '/subscriptions/<id>' or a
 *  resource group }, opts: { days }. (1) per resource: Azure Monitor metrics → exact tokens per ModelName × UTC day. Azure exposes two
 *  metric generations — ProcessedPromptTokens/GeneratedTokens and the newer InputTokens/OutputTokens — which overlap on some deployments,
 *  so per model-day and per field the newer metric wins when it has a data point (total > 0), else the older one; never both. Request
 *  counts are not pulled → msgs 0. (2) when scope is given: Cost Management query → billed PreTaxCost per UsageDate (yyyymmdd) for
 *  ServiceName 'Cognitive Services' (all Cognitive Services in the scope — narrow the scope to the resource group that holds only
 *  Azure OpenAI if you also run Speech/Vision there). 401 → 'token expired — mint a new one (az account get-access-token)'. */
async function fetchAzureOpenAI(token, fields, opts = {}) {
  const days = Math.min(Math.max(1, opts.days || 90), 90);   // Azure Monitor keeps 93 days of platform metrics
  const resources = String(fields.resources || '').split(/[\r\n,]+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  if (!resources.length) throw new Error('Enter at least one Cognitive Services resource id (Azure portal → the Azure OpenAI resource → Properties → Resource ID)');
  for (const r of resources) if (!/^\/subscriptions\/[^/]+\/resourceGroups\/[^/]+\/providers\/Microsoft\.CognitiveServices\/accounts\/[^/]+$/i.test(r)) throw new Error('Not a Cognitive Services resource id: ' + r.slice(0, 60));
  const scope = String(fields.scope || '').trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
  const auth = { expired: 'token expired — mint a new one (az account get-access-token)', denied: 'HTTP 403 — this identity lacks Monitoring Reader / Cost Management Reader on that resource or scope' };
  const headers = { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
  const iso = (ms) => new Date(ms).toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, 'Z');
  const endMs = Date.now(), startMs = endMs - days * 864e5;
  // per (day, model): the older metric pair and the newer pair are kept apart, merged below (key = JSON of [day, model] — no separator bytes)
  const older = new Map(), newer = new Map();
  const bump = (map, key, field, v) => { let b = map.get(key); if (!b) { b = { in: null, out: null }; map.set(key, b); } b[field] = (b[field] || 0) + v; };
  const FIELD = { processedprompttokens: ['in', older], generatedtokens: ['out', older], inputtokens: ['in', newer], outputtokens: ['out', newer] };
  for (const rid of resources) {
    const url = `https://management.azure.com${rid}/providers/microsoft.insights/metrics?api-version=2024-02-01&metricnames=ProcessedPromptTokens,GeneratedTokens,InputTokens,OutputTokens&timespan=${encodeURIComponent(iso(startMs) + '/' + iso(endMs))}&interval=P1D&aggregation=Total&$filter=${encodeURIComponent("ModelName eq '*'")}`;
    const j = await cloudJSON(url, { headers }, auth);
    for (const m of (j && j.value) || []) {
      const spec = FIELD[String((m.name && m.name.value) || '').toLowerCase()];
      if (!spec) continue;
      for (const ts of m.timeseries || []) {
        const md = (ts.metadatavalues || []).find(x => x && x.name && /^modelname$/i.test(String(x.name.value)));
        const model = (md && md.value) || 'azure-openai';
        for (const p of ts.data || []) {
          if (!p || typeof p.total !== 'number' || !(p.total > 0)) continue;   // Azure omits `total` when there is no data for that day
          const d = dayKeyUTC(p.timeStamp); if (!d) continue;
          bump(spec[1], JSON.stringify([d, model]), spec[0], finTok(p.total));
        }
      }
    }
  }
  const agg = makeAgg();
  for (const key of new Set([...older.keys(), ...newer.keys()])) {
    const [d, model] = JSON.parse(key);
    const o = older.get(key) || {}, n = newer.get(key) || {};
    const tin = n.in != null ? n.in : (o.in || 0), tout = n.out != null ? n.out : (o.out || 0);   // newer metric wins per field when it has data
    if (!tin && !tout) continue;
    agg.add(d, 'Azure OpenAI', model, { in: tin, out: tout, msgs: 0 });
  }
  const rows = agg.rows();
  let billed;
  if (scope) {
    const body = { type: 'ActualCost', timeframe: 'Custom', timePeriod: { from: dayKeyUTC(startMs) + 'T00:00:00Z', to: dayKeyUTC(endMs) + 'T23:59:59Z' },
      dataset: { granularity: 'Daily', aggregation: { totalCost: { name: 'PreTaxCost', function: 'Sum' } },
        filter: { dimensions: { name: 'ServiceName', operator: 'In', values: ['Cognitive Services'] } } } };
    const byDay = new Map();
    let url = `https://management.azure.com${scope}/providers/Microsoft.CostManagement/query?api-version=2025-03-01`, pages = 0;
    while (url && pages++ < 20) {   // nextLink pages carry the same POST body
      const j = await cloudJSON(url, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) }, auth);
      const props = (j && j.properties) || {};
      const cols = (props.columns || []).map(c => String(c.name || '').toLowerCase());
      const iCost = cols.indexOf('pretaxcost'), iDate = cols.indexOf('usagedate');
      if (iCost < 0 || iDate < 0) throw new Error('Cost Management returned no PreTaxCost / UsageDate columns');
      for (const r of props.rows || []) {
        const raw = String(r[iDate] ?? '');   // yyyymmdd number (20260810) — or an ISO string on some API versions
        const d = /^\d{8}$/.test(raw) ? `${raw.slice(0, 4)}-${raw.slice(4, 6)}-${raw.slice(6, 8)}` : dayKeyUTC(raw);
        if (!d) continue;
        byDay.set(d, (byDay.get(d) || 0) + finAmt(parseFloat(r[iCost])));
      }
      url = props.nextLink || null;
    }
    billed = [...byDay.entries()].map(([d, amount]) => ({ d, amount })).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1);
  }
  const nModels = new Set(rows.map(r => r.model)).size;
  return { label: `${resources.length} resource${resources.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`,
    detail: `${rows.length} usage rows · ${nModels} model${nModels === 1 ? '' : 's'} · last ${days} days` + (billed ? ` · billed ${billed.length} day${billed.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} from Cost Management` : ' · no cost scope — add one for billed dollars'),
    rows, ...(billed ? { billed } : {}), meta: { days, resources: resources.length } };
}
```

### Task 11: Google Vertex AI (Monitoring `token_count` + optional BigQuery billing export)

**Requires Task 2** (key driver — it also ships the single `.session-only` note this task's check counts) and **Task 10** (`cloudJSON`; if Task 10 has not landed yet, add `cloudJSON` from Task 10 Step 4 first — same code, same anchor).

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, `tests/checks/87d-wave2-cloud.mjs` (create with Task 10 Step 2's skeleton if it does not exist yet)

Research row (`Google Vertex AI`): no API-key path — the Monitoring API rejects API keys; everything is IAM/OAuth. Tokens: Cloud Monitoring `GET https://monitoring.googleapis.com/v3/projects/{project}/timeSeries` with `metric.type="aiplatform.googleapis.com/publisher/online_serving/token_count"`, aligned `86400s` / `ALIGN_SUM` / `REDUCE_SUM`, grouped by `resource.labels.model_user_id` and `metric.labels.type` (input | output) → exact tokens per publisher model (Gemini, Claude on Vertex, …); roles/monitoring.viewer; CORS: googleapis echo the Origin (incl. null) on preflight (probed). Cost: there is NO Cloud Billing REST endpoint for incurred cost — only the Cloud Billing export to BigQuery, queried with `POST https://bigquery.googleapis.com/bigquery/v2/projects/{project}/queries` (roles/bigquery.jobUser + dataViewer) using the same pasted token (`gcloud auth print-access-token`, about 1 h). Quality **exact** tokens + **billed** dollars (when the export exists). Distinct from the Gemini Developer API card.

```js
  // T11 — Cloud Monitoring timeSeries.list, TWO pages (page 1 carries nextPageToken 'p2'). Points are 24-h aligned windows; the fetcher
  //   attributes each to the UTC date of (endTime − 1 s): endTime 2026-08-11T12:00Z → 08-11, 2026-08-10T12:00Z → 08-10.
  //   page 1: gemini-2.5-pro input 5000 (08-11) + 3000 (08-10); output 700 (08-11) + 400 (08-10)
  //   page 2: gemini-2.5-flash input 1200 (08-11), output 150 (08-11); a series WITHOUT model_user_id → model 'vertex-ai', input 10 (08-10);

    resource: { type: 'aiplatform.googleapis.com/PublisherModel', labels: model ? { model_user_id: model, project_id: 'proj-a', location: 'us-central1' } : { project_id: 'proj-a', location: 'us-central1' } },

    vxSeries('output', 'gemini-2.5-pro', [vxPt('2026-08-10T12:00:00Z', '2026-08-11T12:00:00Z', 700), vxPt('2026-08-09T12:00:00Z', '2026-08-10T12:00:00Z', 400)]),
  ], nextPageToken: 'p2', unit: '1' }, null, 1));
  w('wave2/vertex/timeseries-2.json', JSON.stringify({ timeSeries: [

    vxSeries('output', 'gemini-2.5-flash', [vxPt('2026-08-10T12:00:00Z', '2026-08-11T12:00:00Z', 150)]),
```

```js
        && m.period === '86400s' && m.aligner === 'ALIGN_SUM' && m.reducer === 'REDUCE_SUM' && m.group.join() === 'resource.labels.model_user_id,metric.labels.type')
      && vx.mon[0].pageToken === null && vx.mon[1].pageToken === 'p2'
      && vx.bq.length === 1 && vx.bq[0].method === 'POST' && vx.bq[0].auth === 'Bearer ya29.test-token-vertex' && /application\/json/.test(vx.bq[0].ct || '') && vx.bq[0].body.useLegacySql === false
      && /^SELECT DATE\(usage_start_time\) d, SUM\(cost\) c FROM `proj-a\.billing\.gcp_billing_export_v1_ABC` WHERE service\.description = 'Vertex AI' AND usage_start_time >= TIMESTAMP\('\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}'\) GROUP BY d ORDER BY d$/.test(vx.bq[0].body.query),
      JSON.stringify({ mon: vx.mon, bq: vx.bq }));
    ok('vertex: token never persisted (no key:vertex; token in no aiusage:* value; project field persists); 401 → "token expired — mint a new one (gcloud auth print-access-token)"; 403 → token rejected; no billing table → tokens-only, no BigQuery call, status says so',
      !vx.missing && vx.storage.keyEntry === null && !vx.storage.tokenLeaked && vx.storage.project === 'proj-a'
      && vx.expired.err === 'token expired — mint a new one (gcloud auth print-access-token)' && vx.expired.status === 'warn' && /token rejected \(403\)/.test(vx.denied.err || '') && vx.denied.status === 'warn'
      && vx.noTable.err === null && vx.noTable.status === 'ok' && /no billing table/.test(vx.noTable.statusText) && vx.noTable.billed === 2, JSON.stringify({ storage: vx.storage, expired: vx.expired, denied: vx.denied, noTable: vx.noTable }));
```

(`noTable.billed === 2`: the earlier billed rows stay in `Store.billed.vertex` because a tokens-only result returns no `billed` key and the driver leaves billed untouched — the status line, not the data, says "no billing table".)

- [ ] **Step 3 — `fetchVertexAI`.** Insert directly before the line that reads `async function fetchCopilotAll(token) {` (i.e. after Task 10's `fetchAzureOpenAI`):

```js
/** Google Vertex AI — cloud-token fetcher. token: OAuth access token (gcloud auth print-access-token), fields: { project, billingTable? },
 *  opts: { days }. (1) Cloud Monitoring timeSeries.list for aiplatform.googleapis.com/publisher/online_serving/token_count, aligned to
 *  86400 s (ALIGN_SUM), summed across series (REDUCE_SUM), grouped by model_user_id and the metric's `type` label (input | output) →
 *  exact tokens per publisher model. Monitoring anchors alignment windows to interval.endTime (= the sync time), so each point is the 24 h
 *  ending then, attributed to the UTC date of (window end − 1 s). Request counts are not pulled → msgs 0. (2) when billingTable is given:
 *  BigQuery jobs.query against the Cloud Billing export (service.description 'Vertex AI') → billed per usage day; there is no Cloud Billing
 *  REST endpoint for incurred cost, the export is the only path. 401 → 'token expired — mint a new one (gcloud auth print-access-token)';
 *  403 → token rejected (expired or missing roles). API keys are not accepted by either API. */
async function fetchVertexAI(token, fields, opts = {}) {
  const days = Math.max(1, opts.days || 90);
  const project = String(fields.project || '').trim();
  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9\-:.]{4,}$/i.test(project)) throw new Error('Enter the GCP project id (e.g. my-project-123)');
  const table = String(fields.billingTable || '').trim();
  if (table && !/^[\w\-]+\.[\w\-]+\.[\w\-]+$/.test(table)) throw new Error('Billing table must look like project.dataset.table (letters, digits, _ and - only)');   // also keeps the SQL below injection-free
  const auth = { expired: 'token expired — mint a new one (gcloud auth print-access-token)', denied: 'token rejected (403) — expired, or missing roles/monitoring.viewer (bigquery.jobUser for cost); mint a new one' };
  const headers = { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
  const endMs = Date.now(), startMs = endMs - days * 864e5;
  const agg = makeAgg();
  let pageToken = null, pages = 0;
  do {
    const q = new URLSearchParams();
    q.set('filter', 'metric.type="aiplatform.googleapis.com/publisher/online_serving/token_count"');
    q.set('interval.startTime', new Date(startMs).toISOString());
    q.set('interval.endTime', new Date(endMs).toISOString());
    q.set('aggregation.alignmentPeriod', '86400s');
    q.set('aggregation.perSeriesAligner', 'ALIGN_SUM');
    q.set('aggregation.crossSeriesReducer', 'REDUCE_SUM');
    q.append('aggregation.groupByFields', 'resource.labels.model_user_id');
    q.append('aggregation.groupByFields', 'metric.labels.type');
    if (pageToken) q.set('pageToken', pageToken);
    const j = await cloudJSON(`https://monitoring.googleapis.com/v3/projects/${encodeURIComponent(project)}/timeSeries?${q}`, { headers }, auth);
    for (const s of (j && j.timeSeries) || []) {
      const type = String((((s.metric || {}).labels) || {}).type || '').toLowerCase();
      const field = type === 'input' ? 'in' : type === 'output' ? 'out' : null;
      if (!field) continue;   // any other label value is not a token bucket this row model can place
      const model = (((s.resource || {}).labels) || {}).model_user_id || 'vertex-ai';
      for (const p of s.points || []) {
        const v = (p && p.value) || {};
        const n = finTok(Number(v.int64Value ?? v.doubleValue ?? 0));
        if (!n) continue;
        const end = Date.parse(((p.interval || {}).endTime) || '');
        if (!Number.isFinite(end)) continue;
        agg.add(dayKeyUTC(end - 1000), 'Google Vertex AI', model, { [field]: n, msgs: 0 });
      }
    }
    pageToken = (j && j.nextPageToken) || null;
  } while (pageToken && ++pages < 50);
  const rows = agg.rows();
  let billed;
  if (table) {
    const body = { query: "SELECT DATE(usage_start_time) d, SUM(cost) c FROM `" + table + "` WHERE service.description = 'Vertex AI' AND usage_start_time >= TIMESTAMP('" + dayKeyUTC(startMs) + "') GROUP BY d ORDER BY d", useLegacySql: false, timeoutMs: 30000, maxResults: 1000 };
    const j = await cloudJSON(`https://bigquery.googleapis.com/bigquery/v2/projects/${encodeURIComponent(project)}/queries`, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) }, auth);
    if (j && j.jobComplete === false) throw new Error('BigQuery did not finish within 30 s — sync again');
    billed = ((j && j.rows) || []).map(r => { const f = r.f || []; return { d: String((f[0] || {}).v || ''), amount: finAmt(parseFloat((f[1] || {}).v)) }; })
      .filter(x => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(x.d)).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1);
  }
  const nModels = new Set(rows.map(r => r.model)).size;
  return { label: project,
    detail: `${rows.length} usage rows · ${nModels} model${nModels === 1 ? '' : 's'} · last ${days} days` + (billed ? ` · billed ${billed.length} day${billed.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} from the billing export` : ' · no billing table — add the BigQuery export table for billed dollars'),
    rows, ...(billed ? { billed } : {}), meta: { days, project } };
}
```

### Task 12: `sigv4Sign` (WebCrypto) + AWS Bedrock (CloudWatch tokens + Cost Explorer billed)

**Requires Task 2** (key driver — it also ships the single `.session-only` note this task's check counts) and **Task 10** (`cloudJSON`; if Task 10 has not landed yet, add `cloudJSON` from Task 10 Step 4 first — same code, same anchor).

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, `tests/checks/87d-wave2-cloud.mjs` (create with Task 10 Step 2's skeleton if it does not exist yet)

Research row (`AWS Bedrock`): AWS endpoints are CORS-open (`*`, probed) but every request must be SigV4-signed, so the page signs in-page with WebCrypto (`crypto.subtle` — available on `file://` and https; the check below verified `sha256('')` on `file://` in the test browser). Tokens: CloudWatch `POST https://monitoring.{region}.amazonaws.com/` (`X-Amz-Target: GraniteServiceVersion20100801.ListMetrics` / `GetMetricData`, `Content-Type: application/x-amz-json-1.0`), namespace `AWS/Bedrock`, metrics `InputTokenCount` / `OutputTokenCount`, dimension `ModelId`, Sum per day = exact tokens, 15-month retention; `ListMetrics` only lists metrics that reported in the last two weeks. Cost: Cost Explorer `POST https://ce.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/` (`X-Amz-Target: AWSInsightsIndexService.GetCostAndUsage`, `application/x-amz-json-1.1`), DAILY UnblendedCost filtered to SERVICE `Amazon Bedrock` — $0.01 per request, ~24 h lag; third-party models may be billed under their own "(Amazon Bedrock Edition)" service names (not matched by that filter — said on the card). No local usage logs (`~/.aws` holds credentials only). Recommend a read-only IAM user (`cloudwatch:GetMetricData`, `cloudwatch:ListMetrics`, `ce:GetCostAndUsage`) or STS keys. Quality **exact** tokens + **billed** dollars.

```js
// Reference SigV4 (node:crypto) for the unit check — independent of the page's WebCrypto implementation. Same canonicalization / HMAC
// chain as the AWS SigV4 test suite; note the suite's "get-vanilla" vector (Signature=5fa00fa3…) signs host;x-amz-date ONLY, while this
// signer (and the page's) always adds x-amz-content-sha256 — so that vector is not reproduced verbatim; the two PINNED constants below
// (recomputed independently by the plan reviewer with node:crypto — they match) are the reference for this implementation.
const sha256 = (s) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
const hmac = (key, s) => crypto.createHmac('sha256', key).update(s).digest();
function refSigV4({ method, url, headers, body, service, region, accessKeyId, secretAccessKey, sessionToken, now }) {
  const u = new URL(url);
  const amzDate = now.toISOString().replace(/[:-]|\.\d{3}/g, ''), date = amzDate.slice(0, 8);
  const payloadHash = sha256(body || '');
  const all = {}; for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(headers)) all[k.toLowerCase()] = String(v).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
  all.host = u.host; all['x-amz-date'] = amzDate; all['x-amz-content-sha256'] = payloadHash;
  if (sessionToken) all['x-amz-security-token'] = sessionToken;
  const names = Object.keys(all).sort();
  const canonical = [method, u.pathname || '/', '', names.map(n => n + ':' + all[n] + '\n').join(''), names.join(';'), payloadHash].join('\n');
  const scope = `${date}/${region}/${service}/aws4_request`;
  const toSign = ['AWS4-HMAC-SHA256', amzDate, scope, sha256(canonical)].join('\n');
  let k = hmac('AWS4' + secretAccessKey, date); k = hmac(k, region); k = hmac(k, service); k = hmac(k, 'aws4_request');
  return { amzDate, payloadHash, authorization: `AWS4-HMAC-SHA256 Credential=${accessKeyId}/${scope}, SignedHeaders=${names.join(';')}, Signature=${hmac(k, toSign).toString('hex')}` };
}
```

```js
/* ---------- AWS Signature V4 with WebCrypto (used by the Bedrock cloud-token fetcher; no SDK) ---------- */
const WEBCRYPTO_MSG = 'WebCrypto unavailable — open the file over file:// or https';
/** SHA-256 hex of a string or byte array. */
async function sha256Hex(data) {
  if (!(globalThis.crypto && globalThis.crypto.subtle)) throw new Error(WEBCRYPTO_MSG);
  const bytes = typeof data === 'string' ? new TextEncoder().encode(data) : data;
  const h = await globalThis.crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes);
  return [...new Uint8Array(h)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}
/** HMAC-SHA256(keyBytes, data) → Uint8Array (data: string or bytes). */
async function hmacSha256(keyBytes, data) {
  if (!(globalThis.crypto && globalThis.crypto.subtle)) throw new Error(WEBCRYPTO_MSG);
  const key = await globalThis.crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', keyBytes, { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']);
  const sig = await globalThis.crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, typeof data === 'string' ? new TextEncoder().encode(data) : data);
  return new Uint8Array(sig);
}
/** RFC 3986 percent-encoding (encodeURIComponent + the five characters it leaves alone). */
const awsEncode = (s) => encodeURIComponent(s).replace(/[!'()*]/g, (ch) => '%' + ch.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase());
/** AWS SigV4. Returns the headers to send: the input headers (caller's casing) + host, x-amz-date, x-amz-content-sha256, optional
 *  x-amz-security-token, authorization. Canonical request: method · canonical URI (the URL parser already percent-encodes each path
 *  segment once; AWS wants non-S3 paths encoded twice, so each segment is encoded once more — for the '/' root path CloudWatch and
 *  Cost Explorer use this is a no-op) · canonical query (RFC 3986, sorted by key then value) · canonical headers (lower-cased names,
 *  sorted; values trimmed with inner whitespace collapsed) · signed-header list · SHA-256 of the body (or of the empty string).
 *  String to sign: 'AWS4-HMAC-SHA256' / amzDate / date/region/service/aws4_request / hash(canonical). Signing key: HMAC chain from
 *  'AWS4' + secret over date → region → service → 'aws4_request'. Same algorithm as the AWS SigV4 test suite (whose get-vanilla vector
 *  omits x-amz-content-sha256 — this signer always includes it; the pinned vectors in 87d-wave2-cloud are the reference). */
async function sigv4Sign({ method = 'POST', url, headers = {}, body = '', service, region, accessKeyId, secretAccessKey, sessionToken, now = new Date() }) {
  const u = new URL(url);
  const amzDate = now.toISOString().replace(/[:-]|\.\d{3}/g, '');   // 20260810T120000Z
  const date = amzDate.slice(0, 8);
  const payloadHash = await sha256Hex(body || '');
  const canon = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(headers)) canon[k.toLowerCase()] = String(v).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
  canon.host = u.host; canon['x-amz-date'] = amzDate; canon['x-amz-content-sha256'] = payloadHash;
  if (sessionToken) canon['x-amz-security-token'] = sessionToken;
  const names = Object.keys(canon).sort();
  const signedHeaders = names.join(';');
  const canonicalUri = u.pathname.split('/').map(awsEncode).join('/') || '/';
  const canonicalQuery = [...u.searchParams.entries()].map(([k, v]) => [awsEncode(k), awsEncode(v)])
    .sort((a, b) => (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : a[1] < b[1] ? -1 : a[1] > b[1] ? 1 : 0)).map(([k, v]) => k + '=' + v).join('&');
  const canonicalRequest = [method.toUpperCase(), canonicalUri, canonicalQuery, names.map(n => n + ':' + canon[n] + '\n').join(''), signedHeaders, payloadHash].join('\n');
  const scope = `${date}/${region}/${service}/aws4_request`;
  const stringToSign = ['AWS4-HMAC-SHA256', amzDate, scope, await sha256Hex(canonicalRequest)].join('\n');
  let k = await hmacSha256(new TextEncoder().encode('AWS4' + secretAccessKey), date);
  k = await hmacSha256(k, region); k = await hmacSha256(k, service); k = await hmacSha256(k, 'aws4_request');
  const signature = [...await hmacSha256(k, stringToSign)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  const out = { ...headers, host: u.host, 'x-amz-date': amzDate, 'x-amz-content-sha256': payloadHash };   // fetch drops the forbidden `host` header itself; it is listed because it is signed
  if (sessionToken) out['x-amz-security-token'] = sessionToken;
  out.authorization = `AWS4-HMAC-SHA256 Credential=${accessKeyId}/${scope}, SignedHeaders=${signedHeaders}, Signature=${signature}`;
  return out;
}

/** AWS Bedrock — cloud-token fetcher. key: access key id; fields: { secret, session?, region }; opts: { days }. Every request is SigV4-signed
 *  in-page (sigv4Sign) — key, secret and session token are used for these requests only, never stored. (1) CloudWatch ListMetrics
 *  (AWS/Bedrock · InputTokenCount · dimension ModelId only) → the model ids that reported in the last two weeks (an idle model's older
 *  days are therefore not pulled); (2) CloudWatch GetMetricData: InputTokenCount + OutputTokenCount, Sum, Period 86400 from a UTC
 *  midnight (≤ 500 queries per call, NextToken paged) → exact tokens per model × UTC day, msgs 0 (request counts not pulled);
 *  (3) Cost Explorer GetCostAndUsage (served from us-east-1 only; $0.01 per request; ~24 h lag), DAILY UnblendedCost filtered to
 *  SERVICE 'Amazon Bedrock' → billed per day (zero days included). A Cost Explorer failure (e.g. no ce:GetCostAndUsage) degrades to
 *  tokens-only and is reported in the status line; CloudWatch failures are errors (403 → the AWS rejection message). */
async function fetchBedrock(key, fields, opts = {}) {
  const days = Math.max(1, opts.days || 90);
  const accessKeyId = String(key || '').trim(), secretAccessKey = String(fields.secret || '').trim(), sessionToken = String(fields.session || '').trim() || undefined;
  const region = (String(fields.region || '').trim() || 'us-east-1').toLowerCase();
  if (!secretAccessKey) throw new Error('Enter the secret access key (use a read-only IAM user: cloudwatch:GetMetricData, cloudwatch:ListMetrics, ce:GetCostAndUsage)');
  if (!/^[a-z]{2}(-gov|-iso[a-z]?)?-[a-z]+-\d$/.test(region)) throw new Error('Region must look like us-east-1');
  const auth = { denied: 'AWS returned 403 — bad key/secret/session token or region, or the IAM user lacks the read-only policy' };
  const call = async (host, service, svcRegion, target, contentType, body) => {
    const url = `https://${host}/`;
    const headers = await sigv4Sign({ method: 'POST', url, headers: { 'Content-Type': contentType, 'X-Amz-Target': target }, body, service, region: svcRegion, accessKeyId, secretAccessKey, sessionToken, now: new Date() });
    return cloudJSON(url, { method: 'POST', headers, body }, auth);
  };
  const cw = (target, body) => call(`monitoring.${region}.amazonaws.com`, 'monitoring', region, 'GraniteServiceVersion20100801.' + target, 'application/x-amz-json-1.0', JSON.stringify(body));
  // (1) model ids — only metrics whose dimension set is exactly [ModelId]; other sets (ModelId+ImageSize …) are separate metrics GetMetricData would not match
  const models = []; let next = null, guard = 0;
  do {
    const j = await cw('ListMetrics', { Namespace: 'AWS/Bedrock', MetricName: 'InputTokenCount', ...(next ? { NextToken: next } : {}) });
    for (const m of (j && j.Metrics) || []) {
      const dims = m.Dimensions || [];
      if (dims.length === 1 && dims[0].Name === 'ModelId' && dims[0].Value && !models.includes(dims[0].Value)) models.push(dims[0].Value);
    }
    next = (j && j.NextToken) || null;
  } while (next && ++guard < 20);
  // (2) exact tokens per model × UTC day — StartTime on a UTC midnight so the 86400-s periods are calendar days
  const agg = makeAgg();
  const now = new Date(), nowSec = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
  const startSec = Math.floor(Date.UTC(now.getUTCFullYear(), now.getUTCMonth(), now.getUTCDate()) / 1000) - days * 86400;
  for (let i = 0; i < models.length; i += 250) {   // 2 queries per model → ≤ 500 MetricDataQueries per call (the API limit)
    const batch = models.slice(i, i + 250), queries = [];
    batch.forEach((m, k) => {
      for (const [pfx, metric] of [['in', 'InputTokenCount'], ['out', 'OutputTokenCount']])
        queries.push({ Id: pfx + (i + k), MetricStat: { Metric: { Namespace: 'AWS/Bedrock', MetricName: metric, Dimensions: [{ Name: 'ModelId', Value: m }] }, Period: 86400, Stat: 'Sum' }, ReturnData: true });
    });
    let token = null, pages = 0;
    do {
      const j = await cw('GetMetricData', { StartTime: startSec, EndTime: nowSec, MetricDataQueries: queries, ScanBy: 'TimestampAscending', ...(token ? { NextToken: token } : {}) });
      for (const r of (j && j.MetricDataResults) || []) {
        const mm = /^(in|out)(\d+)$/.exec(r.Id || ''); if (!mm) continue;
        const model = models[+mm[2]]; if (!model) continue;
        const ts = r.Timestamps || [], vs = r.Values || [];
        for (let k = 0; k < ts.length; k++) {
          const v = finTok(Number(vs[k])); if (!v) continue;
          const d = dayKeyUTC(ts[k]); if (!d) continue;
          agg.add(d, 'AWS Bedrock', model, { [mm[1]]: v, msgs: 0 });
        }
      }
      token = (j && j.NextToken) || null;
    } while (token && ++pages < 50);
  }
  const rows = agg.rows();
  // (3) billed per day — Cost Explorer is served from us-east-1 only; failure degrades to tokens-only
  let billed, costNote = '';
  try {
    const start = dayKeyUTC(startSec * 1000), end = dayKeyUTC(now.getTime() + 864e5);   // End is exclusive → tomorrow
    const byDay = new Map(); let tok = null, pages = 0;
    do {
      const j = await call('ce.us-east-1.amazonaws.com', 'ce', 'us-east-1', 'AWSInsightsIndexService.GetCostAndUsage', 'application/x-amz-json-1.1',
        JSON.stringify({ TimePeriod: { Start: start, End: end }, Granularity: 'DAILY', Metrics: ['UnblendedCost'], Filter: { Dimensions: { Key: 'SERVICE', Values: ['Amazon Bedrock'] } }, ...(tok ? { NextPageToken: tok } : {}) }));
      for (const r of (j && j.ResultsByTime) || []) {
        const d = String(((r.TimePeriod || {}).Start) || '').slice(0, 10); if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d)) continue;
        byDay.set(d, (byDay.get(d) || 0) + finAmt(parseFloat((((r.Total || {}).UnblendedCost) || {}).Amount)));
      }
      tok = (j && j.NextPageToken) || null;
    } while (tok && ++pages < 20);
    billed = [...byDay.entries()].map(([d, amount]) => ({ d, amount })).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1);
  } catch (e) { costNote = ' · cost unavailable: ' + (e.message || String(e)).slice(0, 80); }
  return { label: `${region} · key …${accessKeyId.slice(-4)}`,
    detail: `${models.length} model${models.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} seen by CloudWatch (last 2 weeks) · ${rows.length} usage rows · last ${days} days` + (billed ? ` · billed ${billed.length} day${billed.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} from Cost Explorer ($0.01 per sync)` : costNote),
    rows, ...(billed ? { billed } : {}), meta: { days, region, models: models.length } };
}
```

### Task 13: `export-file` kind + chat-export parsers (Grok, DeepSeek chat, Meta AI, Perplexity, Microsoft Copilot CSV) + cards for ChatGPT / Claude.ai / Grok / MS Copilot / Perplexity / Meta AI / DeepSeek chat

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, `tests/checks/87e-wave2-exports.mjs`, `tests/checks/40-sniff.mjs`, `tests/checks/98-hub.mjs`

**Requires Task 1 and Task 2** (the hub/card kind branches they introduce — this task adds its `export-file` arm next to theirs) and **Task 8** (`readZipEntries`, `App._importJson`, the ZIP branch in `handleImportFiles`, the `zipBuffer` fixture helper, and `87e-wave2-exports.mjs` itself).

Research rows (all **estimated**, all export-only, none has a usage API a local page can call, none of these cards claims web-login access): **Grok** — grok.com → Settings → Data → Export Data mails one JSON `{conversations:[{conversation:{title, create_time}, responses:[{message, sender:'human'|'assistant', create_time:{$date:{$numberLong:'ms'}}}]}]}` (MongoDB extended JSON; ISO strings also seen), no model/tokens. **DeepSeek chat** — chat.deepseek.com (web only) → Settings → Data → Export data → JSON archive of conversations with messages (role, text, timestamps; model reportedly recorded), no tokens; exact file names undocumented → the importer needs the word "deepseek" in the file name (or the ZIP name) or a top-level `source`. **Meta AI** — Accounts Center → Download your information → Meta AI (JSON) / Meta AI app / WhatsApp `/download-all-ai-info`; threads arrive as Facebook-DYI `message_*.json` `{participants:[{name}], messages:[{sender_name, timestamp_ms, content}]}` (newest first), no model/tokens. **Perplexity** — Settings → Account → Request data export (or privacy@perplexity.ai) → ZIP of JSON threads (query, answer, timestamps, title), up to ~30 days later; third-party exporter JSON has the same query/answer shape. **Microsoft Copilot (consumer)** — account.microsoft.com/privacy → Copilot activity history → Export all activity history → CSV downloaded immediately (prompts + responses with timestamps, possibly truncated; columns undocumented → tolerant). Input for every estimated reply is the preceding prompt only (a lower bound), like the Gemini Takeout parser; all rows `est:1`.
