```js
  // T13a — Grok export (grok.com → Settings → Data → Export Data). estTok = round(chars/4); input = preceding human message.
  //   t1: human 40 → assistant 80 (in 10, out 20, $numberLong ms) · human 20 → assistant 8 (in 5, out 2, ISO) ·
  //   t2: human 'abcd' → 'ok!!!' (in 1, out 1; no create_time → conversation create_time) · 'broken' 6 chars (out 2, in 1; create_time 'not a date' → conversation time) ·
  //   t3: assistant with no date anywhere → skipped.   Expected: in=17 out=25 msgs=4 convs=3 skipped=1 · platform Grok · model grok · est
  const ms = (iso) => ({ $date: { $numberLong: String(T(iso)) } });
  w('wave2/grok/grok-export.json', JSON.stringify({ conversations: [
    { conversation: { title: 't1', create_time: ms('2026-08-10T09:00:00.000Z') }, responses: [
      { message: 'x'.repeat(40), sender: 'human', create_time: ms('2026-08-10T09:00:00.000Z') },
      { message: 'y'.repeat(80), sender: 'assistant', create_time: ms('2026-08-10T09:00:05.000Z') },
      { message: 'z'.repeat(20), sender: 'human', create_time: '2026-08-10T21:30:00.000Z' },
      { message: 'w'.repeat(8), sender: 'assistant', create_time: '2026-08-10T21:30:05.000Z' } ] },
    { conversation: { title: 't2', create_time: '2026-08-11T08:00:00.000Z' }, responses: [
      { message: 'abcd', sender: 'human' }, { message: 'ok!!!', sender: 'assistant' }, { message: 'broken', sender: 'assistant', create_time: 'not a date' } ] },
    { conversation: { title: 't3' }, responses: [{ message: 'no date anywhere', sender: 'assistant' }] },
  ] }, null, 1));
  // T13b — DeepSeek chat export (chat.deepseek.com → Settings → Data → Export data). Tolerant shape: array of conversations with messages[{role, content, inserted_at|created_at, model?}].
  //   c1: user 40 → assistant 80 (in 10, out 20, model deepseek-reasoner) · user 20 → assistant 8 (in 5, out 2; epoch SECONDS; no model → deepseek-chat) ·
  //   c2: user content[] 'abcd' → 'ok!!!' (in 1, out 1) · assistant with created_at null and no conversation time → skipped.
  //   Expected: in=16 out=23 msgs=3 convs=2 skipped=1 · platform 'DeepSeek chat' · models deepseek-chat + deepseek-reasoner · est
  w('wave2/deepseek/deepseek-conversations.json', JSON.stringify([
    { id: 'c1', title: 'hello', inserted_at: '2026-08-10T09:00:00.000Z', messages: [
      { role: 'user', content: 'x'.repeat(40), inserted_at: '2026-08-10T09:00:00.000Z' },
      { role: 'assistant', content: 'y'.repeat(80), inserted_at: '2026-08-10T09:00:05.000Z', model: 'deepseek-reasoner' },
      { role: 'user', content: 'z'.repeat(20), inserted_at: T('2026-08-10T21:30:00.000Z') / 1000 },
      { role: 'assistant', content: 'w'.repeat(8), inserted_at: T('2026-08-10T21:30:05.000Z') / 1000 } ] },
    { id: 'c2', title: 'two', messages: [
      { role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'abcd' }], created_at: '2026-08-11T08:00:00.000Z' },
      { role: 'assistant', content: 'ok!!!', created_at: '2026-08-11T08:00:03.000Z' },
      { role: 'assistant', content: 'no time', created_at: null } ] },
  ], null, 1));
  // T13c — Meta AI thread (Download your information, JSON → message_1.json; Facebook DYI shape, newest first).
  //   sorted ascending: 08-09 'Here is the image' 17 chars (out 4, no preceding user → in 0) · user 40 → Meta AI 80 (in 10, out 20) · user 20 → Meta AI 8 (in 5, out 2) · one undated Meta AI message → skipped.
  //   Expected: in=15 out=26 msgs=3 skipped=1 · platform 'Meta AI' · model meta-ai · sid = thread_path
  w('wave2/meta-ai/message_1.json', JSON.stringify({ participants: [{ name: 'Alaa' }, { name: 'Meta AI' }], title: 'Meta AI', thread_path: 'inbox/metaai_123', messages: [
    { sender_name: 'Meta AI', content: 'undated' },
    { sender_name: 'Meta AI', timestamp_ms: T('2026-08-10T21:30:05.000Z'), content: 'w'.repeat(8) },
    { sender_name: 'Alaa', timestamp_ms: T('2026-08-10T21:30:00.000Z'), content: 'z'.repeat(20) },
    { sender_name: 'Meta AI', timestamp_ms: T('2026-08-10T09:00:05.000Z'), content: 'y'.repeat(80) },
    { sender_name: 'Alaa', timestamp_ms: T('2026-08-10T09:00:00.000Z'), content: 'x'.repeat(40) },
    { sender_name: 'Meta AI', timestamp_ms: T('2026-08-09T08:00:00.000Z'), content: 'Here is the image' },
  ] }, null, 1));
  // T13d — Perplexity data export (array of threads; tolerant field names). q1: 40 → 80 (in 10, out 20, ISO) · 20 → 8 (in 5, out 2, epoch ms) · 'abcd' → 'ok!!!' (in 1, out 1, date-only) · undated → skipped.
  //   Expected: in=16 out=23 msgs=3 threads=4 skipped=1 · platform Perplexity · model perplexity. Also shipped inside a ZIP (the way the export arrives) next to an unrelated account.json.
  const pxThreads = JSON.stringify([
    { title: 'q1', query_str: 'x'.repeat(40), answer: 'y'.repeat(80), created_at: '2026-08-10T09:00:00.000Z' },
    { query: 'z'.repeat(20), response: 'w'.repeat(8), timestamp: T('2026-08-10T21:30:00.000Z') },
    { question: 'abcd', text: 'ok!!!', date: '2026-08-11' },
    { query: 'undated', answer: 'nope' },
  ], null, 1);
  w('wave2/perplexity/threads.json', pxThreads);
  w('wave2/perplexity/perplexity-export.zip', zipBuffer([{ name: 'threads.json', data: pxThreads, deflate: true }, { name: 'account.json', data: '{"email":"x@y.z"}' }]));
  // T13e — Microsoft Copilot activity CSV (account.microsoft.com/privacy → Copilot → Export all activity history; columns undocumented → tolerant).
  //   row1 ISO: 40 → 80 (in 10, out 20) · row2 'M/D/YYYY H:MM PM' local: 20 → 8 (in 5, out 2, hour 21) · row3 ISO: 'abcd' → 'ok!!!' (in 1, out 1) · row4 unparseable date → skipped.
  //   Expected: in=16 out=23 msgs=3 skipped=1 · platform 'Microsoft Copilot' · model copilot · apps [Copilot app, Copilot in Edge]
  w('wave2/mscopilot/copilot-activity.csv', [
    'Date,App,Prompt,Response',
    '2026-08-10T09:00:00Z,Copilot app,' + 'x'.repeat(40) + ',' + 'y'.repeat(80),
    '8/10/2026 9:30 PM,Copilot in Edge,' + 'z'.repeat(20) + ',' + 'w'.repeat(8),
    '2026-08-11T08:00:00Z,Copilot app,abcd,ok!!!',
    'not a date,Copilot app,undated,skip me',
  ].join('\n') + '\n');
```

```js
    const grokTxt = read('wave2', 'grok', 'grok-export.json'), dscTxt = read('wave2', 'deepseek', 'deepseek-conversations.json'), metaTxt = read('wave2', 'meta-ai', 'message_1.json'),

      Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.projects = []; Store.connectors = {};
      const sum = (rows) => rows.reduce((a, r) => ({ in: a.in + r.in, out: a.out + r.out, msgs: a.msgs + r.msgs, est: a.est && r.est === 1 }), { in: 0, out: 0, msgs: 0, est: true });
      const H = (iso) => new Date(iso).getHours();
      const hours = (rows) => [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.hour))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const expH = (...isos) => [...new Set(isos.map(H))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      // parsers, direct
      const g = parseGrokExport(JSON.parse(grokTxt));
      const d = parseDeepSeekChatExport(JSON.parse(dscTxt));
      const m = parseMetaAiExport(JSON.parse(metaTxt), 'message_1.json');
      const p = parsePerplexityExport(JSON.parse(pxTxt), 'threads.json');
      const head = (parseCSV(cpCsv.split(/\r?\n/, 1)[0])[0] || []).map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, ''));
      const cpDetect = { yes: isCopilotActivityCSV(head), generic: isCopilotActivityCSV(['date', 'platform', 'model', 'input_tokens', 'output_tokens', 'requests']), cursor: isCursorUsageCSV(head), deepseek: isDeepSeekAmountCSV(head, 'copilot-activity.csv') };
      const cp = parseCopilotActivityCSV(cpCsv, 'copilot-activity.csv');
      const kinds = [sniffJSON(JSON.parse(grokTxt), 'grok-export.json'), sniffJSON(JSON.parse(dscTxt), 'deepseek-conversations.json'), sniffJSON(JSON.parse(dscTxt), 'conversations.json'), sniffJSON(JSON.parse(metaTxt), 'message_1.json'), sniffJSON(JSON.parse(pxTxt), 'threads.json')];
      // Imports routing: four loose files + the Perplexity ZIP (JSON inside) in one batch
      const toFile = (b, name) => new File([Uint8Array.from(atob(b), ch => ch.charCodeAt(0))], name, { type: 'application/zip' });
      await App.handleImportFiles([
        new File([grokTxt], 'grok-export.json', { type: 'application/json' }), new File([dscTxt], 'deepseek-conversations.json', { type: 'application/json' }),
        new File([metaTxt], 'message_1.json', { type: 'application/json' }), new File([cpCsv], 'copilot-activity.csv', { type: 'text/csv' }), toFile(pxZipB64, 'perplexity-export.zip')]);
      const note = document.getElementById('import-status').textContent;
      const srcs = ['grok-export', 'deepseek-chat-export', 'meta-ai-export', 'perplexity-export', 'copilot-activity-csv'].map(id => Store.sources[id] ? sum(Store.sources[id].rows).msgs : null);
      const pxSid = Store.sources['perplexity-export'].rows[0].sid;
      // Meta AI threads: a second thread file merges (3 + 1 = 4 replies); re-importing the first replaces only its own thread (still 4, 2 threads)

      await App.handleImportFiles([new File([JSON.stringify(meta2)], 'message_2.json', { type: 'application/json' })]);
      const metaAfterSecond = sum(Store.sources['meta-ai-export'].rows).msgs;
      await App.handleImportFiles([new File([metaTxt], 'message_1.json', { type: 'application/json' })]);
      const metaAfterReimport = { msgs: sum(Store.sources['meta-ai-export'].rows).msgs, threads: Store.sources['meta-ai-export'].meta.threads };
      App.renderHub();
      const hub = ['grok', 'deepseek-chat', 'meta-ai', 'perplexity', 'mscopilot'].map(id => (document.querySelector(`#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="${id}"] .dot`) || {}).className || 'missing');
      const imports = connectorById('imports').srcIds, order = PLATFORM_ORDER, badge = document.querySelector('#src-import .badge').textContent;
      Store.sources = {}; Store.connectors = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.persist(); App._render();
      return { g: { ...sum(g.rows), meta: g.meta, hours: hours(g.rows), expHours: expH('2026-08-10T09:00:05.000Z', '2026-08-10T21:30:05.000Z', '2026-08-11T08:00:00.000Z'), plat: g.rows[0].platform, model: g.rows[0].model },
        d: { ...sum(d.rows), meta: d.meta, models: [...new Set(d.rows.map(r => r.model))].sort(), plat: d.rows[0].platform },
        m: { ...sum(m.rows), meta: m.meta, plat: m.rows[0].platform, model: m.rows[0].model, sid: m.rows[0].sid },
        p: { ...sum(p.rows), meta: p.meta, plat: p.rows[0].platform, model: p.rows[0].model },
        cp: { ...sum(cp.rows), meta: cp.meta, hours: hours(cp.rows), h1: H('2026-08-10T09:00:00Z'), h3: H('2026-08-11T08:00:00Z'), plat: cp.rows[0].platform, model: cp.rows[0].model }, cpDetect,
        kinds, note, srcs, pxSid, metaAfterSecond, metaAfterReimport, hub, imports, order, badge };
    }, grokTxt, dscTxt, metaTxt, pxTxt, cpCsv, b64('wave2', 'perplexity', 'perplexity-export.zip'));
    ok('grok: export parsed (est. in=17 out=25 msgs=4; 3 conversations; 1 undated reply skipped; $numberLong / ISO / conversation-time fallbacks; hours kept) as platform Grok, model grok',
      ex.g.in === 17 && ex.g.out === 25 && ex.g.msgs === 4 && ex.g.est && ex.g.meta.convs === 3 && ex.g.meta.skipped === 1 && JSON.stringify(ex.g.hours) === JSON.stringify(ex.g.expHours) && ex.g.plat === 'Grok' && ex.g.model === 'grok', JSON.stringify(ex.g));
    ok('deepseek chat: export parsed (est. in=16 out=23 msgs=3; 2 conversations; 1 undated skipped; epoch-seconds + content[] handled; model per message, default deepseek-chat) as platform "DeepSeek chat"',
      ex.d.in === 16 && ex.d.out === 23 && ex.d.msgs === 3 && ex.d.est && ex.d.meta.convs === 2 && ex.d.meta.skipped === 1 && JSON.stringify(ex.d.models) === JSON.stringify(['deepseek-chat', 'deepseek-reasoner']) && ex.d.plat === 'DeepSeek chat', JSON.stringify(ex.d));
    ok('meta ai: DYI thread parsed newest-first → ascending (est. in=15 out=26 msgs=3; 1 undated skipped; sid = thread_path) as platform "Meta AI", model meta-ai',
      ex.m.in === 15 && ex.m.out === 26 && ex.m.msgs === 3 && ex.m.est && ex.m.meta.skipped === 1 && ex.m.plat === 'Meta AI' && ex.m.model === 'meta-ai' && ex.m.sid === 'inbox/metaai_123', JSON.stringify(ex.m));
    ok('perplexity: thread array parsed with tolerant field names (est. in=16 out=23 msgs=3 of 4 threads; 1 undated skipped; ISO / epoch-ms / date-only) as platform Perplexity, model perplexity',
      ex.p.in === 16 && ex.p.out === 23 && ex.p.msgs === 3 && ex.p.est && ex.p.meta.threads === 4 && ex.p.meta.skipped === 1 && ex.p.plat === 'Perplexity' && ex.p.model === 'perplexity', JSON.stringify(ex.p));
    ok('ms copilot: activity CSV detected (generic / Cursor / DeepSeek headers are not) and parsed (est. in=16 out=23 msgs=3; 1 unparseable date skipped; ISO and M/D/YYYY H:MM PM → hour 21; apps listed) as platform "Microsoft Copilot", model copilot',
      ex.cpDetect.yes && !ex.cpDetect.generic && !ex.cpDetect.cursor && !ex.cpDetect.deepseek && ex.cp.in === 16 && ex.cp.out === 23 && ex.cp.msgs === 3 && ex.cp.est && ex.cp.meta.skipped === 1
      && ex.cp.hours.includes(21) && ex.cp.hours.includes(ex.cp.h1) && ex.cp.hours.includes(ex.cp.h3) && JSON.stringify(ex.cp.meta.apps) === JSON.stringify(['Copilot app', 'Copilot in Edge']) && ex.cp.plat === 'Microsoft Copilot' && ex.cp.model === 'copilot', JSON.stringify({ cp: ex.cp, cpDetect: ex.cpDetect }));
    ok('sniff: grok-export / deepseek-chat-export (name-gated: conversations.json → unknown) / meta-ai-export / perplexity-export',
      JSON.stringify(ex.kinds) === JSON.stringify(['grok-export', 'deepseek-chat-export', 'unknown', 'meta-ai-export', 'perplexity-export']), JSON.stringify(ex.kinds));
    ok('imports: one batch routes all five formats (incl. Perplexity JSON inside its ZIP, sid = archive/entry) to their own sources; Meta AI threads merge and a re-imported thread replaces only itself; export-file hub cards turn green; srcIds/PLATFORM_ORDER/badge list them (ChatGPT once)',
      JSON.stringify(ex.srcs) === JSON.stringify([4, 3, 3, 3, 3]) && ex.pxSid === 'perplexity-export.zip/threads.json' && ex.metaAfterSecond === 4 && ex.metaAfterReimport.msgs === 4 && ex.metaAfterReimport.threads === 2
      && /Grok export — 3 conversations, 4 replies/.test(ex.note) && /DeepSeek chat export — 2 conversations, 3 replies/.test(ex.note) && /Meta AI thread — 3 replies/.test(ex.note) && /Microsoft Copilot activity CSV — 3 prompts/.test(ex.note) && /Perplexity export — 3 threads/.test(ex.note)
      && ex.hub.every(cls => /\bok\b/.test(cls)) && ['grok-export', 'deepseek-chat-export', 'meta-ai-export', 'perplexity-export', 'copilot-activity-csv'].every(id => ex.imports.includes(id))
      && ['Grok', 'Microsoft Copilot', 'Perplexity', 'Meta AI', 'DeepSeek chat', 'ChatGPT', 'Claude.ai'].every(pl => ex.order.includes(pl)) && ex.order.filter(pl => pl === 'ChatGPT').length === 1 && ex.order[ex.order.length - 1] === 'Other'
      && /Grok/.test(ex.badge) && /Perplexity/.test(ex.badge) && /Takeout/.test(ex.badge) && /Cursor/.test(ex.badge), JSON.stringify({ srcs: ex.srcs, pxSid: ex.pxSid, metaAfterSecond: ex.metaAfterSecond, metaAfterReimport: ex.metaAfterReimport, hub: ex.hub, note: ex.note, imports: ex.imports, order: ex.order, badge: ex.badge }));
```

- [ ] **Step 4 — parsers + sniff.** Insert directly BEFORE the comment `/** Parses a large chat export off the main thread` (i.e. after `parseGeminiTakeout`):

```js
/* ---------- Wave 2 chat-app exports (Grok, DeepSeek chat, Meta AI, Perplexity). All estimated from text; input = the preceding
 * prompt only (a lower bound, like Takeout); every row est:1; platform strings are the export-file cards' platforms. ---------- */
/** Timestamp of an exported record in any shape these exports use: ISO string, date-only string, 'M/D/YYYY H:MM[:SS] [AM|PM]' (local),
 *  epoch seconds or milliseconds (number or numeric string), MongoDB extended JSON {$date:{$numberLong:'ms'}} / {$date:'iso'}.
 *  → { d, hour } (local day; hour undefined for date-only values) or null. */
function exportTimestamp(v) {
  if (v == null || v === '') return null;
  if (typeof v === 'object') { if (v.$date != null) return exportTimestamp(v.$date); if (v.$numberLong != null) return exportTimestamp(Number(v.$numberLong)); return null; }
  const s = String(v).trim();
  if (typeof v === 'string' && RE_DATEONLY.test(s)) return { d: s, hour: undefined };
  const us = /^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})(?:[ ,T]+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?\s*([AaPp][Mm])?)?$/.exec(s);
  if (us) {
    let h = us[4] ? +us[4] : 0;
    if (us[7]) { const pm = /p/i.test(us[7]); if (pm && h < 12) h += 12; if (!pm && h === 12) h = 0; }
    const t = new Date(+us[3], +us[1] - 1, +us[2], h, us[5] ? +us[5] : 0, us[6] ? +us[6] : 0);
    return isNaN(t) ? null : { d: dayKey(t), hour: us[4] ? t.getHours() : undefined };
  }
  let ms = typeof v === 'number' ? v : (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(s) ? Number(s) : NaN);
  if (Number.isFinite(ms)) { if (ms < 1e11) ms *= 1000; }   // epoch seconds → ms (no chat export predates 1973)
  else ms = Date.parse(s);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return null;
  const t = new Date(ms);
  return { d: dayKey(t), hour: t.getHours() };
}
/** Text of an exported message in the shapes seen: string · [parts] · {text} · {content} · {parts:[…]}. */
function exportText(c) {
  if (c == null) return '';
  if (typeof c === 'string') return c;
  if (Array.isArray(c)) return c.map(exportText).join('\n');
  if (typeof c === 'object') { if (typeof c.text === 'string') return c.text; if (c.content != null) return exportText(c.content); if (Array.isArray(c.parts)) return exportText(c.parts); return ''; }
  return String(c);
}
/** grok.com export: {conversations:[{conversation:{title, create_time}, responses:[{message, sender:'human'|'assistant', create_time}]}]}.
 *  create_time is MongoDB extended JSON ({$date:{$numberLong}}), an ISO string or a number; a reply without one falls back to the
 *  conversation's create_time; no time anywhere → skipped and counted. Model 'grok' (the export names none). */
function parseGrokExport(obj) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); let msgs = 0, skipped = 0;
  const convs = Array.isArray(obj && obj.conversations) ? obj.conversations : [];
  for (const c of convs) {
    const meta = (c && c.conversation) || {}, list = Array.isArray(c && c.responses) ? c.responses : [];
    const convTs = exportTimestamp(meta.create_time);
    let lastHuman = 0;
    for (const r of list) {
      if (!r || typeof r !== 'object') continue;
      const chars = exportText(r.message).length;
      if (String(r.sender || '').toLowerCase() !== 'assistant') { lastHuman = chars; continue; }
      const ts = exportTimestamp(r.create_time) || convTs;
      if (!ts) { skipped++; continue; }
      agg.add(ts.d, 'Grok', 'grok', { in: estTok(lastHuman), out: estTok(chars), msgs: 1, est: 1 }, undefined, ts.hour);
      msgs++;
    }
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), meta: { msgs, convs: convs.length, skipped } };
}
/** chat.deepseek.com export (shape undocumented → tolerant): an array of conversations, or {conversations|data:[…]}, each with
 *  messages[{role|sender, content|text, inserted_at|created_at|timestamp, model?}]. Assistant messages → rows; model = message.model
 *  or 'deepseek-chat'; a message without a time falls back to the conversation's; none → skipped and counted. */
function parseDeepSeekChatExport(obj) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); let msgs = 0, skipped = 0;
  const convs = Array.isArray(obj) ? obj : (obj && (Array.isArray(obj.conversations) ? obj.conversations : Array.isArray(obj.data) ? obj.data : [])) || [];
  for (const c of convs) {
    if (!c || typeof c !== 'object') continue;
    const list = Array.isArray(c.messages) ? c.messages : [];
    const convTs = exportTimestamp(c.inserted_at != null ? c.inserted_at : c.created_at != null ? c.created_at : c.timestamp);
    let lastUser = 0;
    for (const m of list) {
      if (!m || typeof m !== 'object') continue;
      const chars = exportText(m.content != null ? m.content : m.text).length;
      const role = String(m.role || m.sender || '').toLowerCase();
      if (role !== 'assistant') { if (role === 'user' || role === 'human') lastUser = chars; continue; }
      const ts = exportTimestamp(m.inserted_at != null ? m.inserted_at : m.created_at != null ? m.created_at : m.timestamp) || convTs;
      if (!ts) { skipped++; continue; }
      agg.add(ts.d, 'DeepSeek chat', String(m.model || 'deepseek-chat'), { in: estTok(lastUser), out: estTok(chars), msgs: 1, est: 1 }, undefined, ts.hour);
      msgs++;
    }
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), meta: { msgs, convs: convs.length, skipped } };
}
/** Meta AI thread from Download-your-information (Facebook DYI JSON): {participants:[{name}], messages:[{sender_name, timestamp_ms,
 *  content}], thread_path}. Messages are listed newest first → sorted ascending; replies whose sender_name is "Meta AI" are output, the
 *  preceding message input. Rows carry sid = thread_path (or the file name) so each thread file replaces only itself on re-import. */
function parseMetaAiExport(obj, fileName) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); let msgs = 0, skipped = 0;
  const list = Array.isArray(obj && obj.messages) ? obj.messages.filter(m => m && typeof m === 'object') : [];
  const isAi = (m) => /meta\s*ai/i.test(String(m.sender_name || ''));
  const sid = String((obj && obj.thread_path) || fileName || 'meta-ai');
  const sorted = [...list].sort((a, b) => (+a.timestamp_ms || 0) - (+b.timestamp_ms || 0));
  let lastUser = 0;
  for (const m of sorted) {
    const chars = exportText(m.content).length;
    if (!isAi(m)) { lastUser = chars; continue; }
    const ts = exportTimestamp(m.timestamp_ms != null ? m.timestamp_ms : m.timestamp);
    if (!ts) { skipped++; continue; }
    agg.add(ts.d, 'Meta AI', 'meta-ai', { in: estTok(lastUser), out: estTok(chars), msgs: 1, est: 1 }, sid, ts.hour);
    msgs++;
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), meta: { msgs, skipped, sid } };
}
/** Perplexity data export / exporter JSON: an array of threads (or {threads|data:[…]}); a thread is {query|query_str|question|title,
 *  answer|response|text, created_at|timestamp|date} or carries entries[]/steps[] of that shape. Rows carry sid = file name so each file
 *  (the export may be one JSON per thread) replaces only itself on re-import. */
function parsePerplexityExport(input, fileName) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); let msgs = 0, skipped = 0;
  const list = Array.isArray(input) ? input : (input && (Array.isArray(input.threads) ? input.threads : Array.isArray(input.data) ? input.data : [])) || [];
  const sid = String(fileName || 'perplexity');
  const q = (t) => exportText(t.query != null ? t.query : t.query_str != null ? t.query_str : t.question != null ? t.question : t.title);
  const a = (t) => exportText(t.answer != null ? t.answer : t.response != null ? t.response : t.text);
  for (const t of list) {
    if (!t || typeof t !== 'object') continue;
    const items = Array.isArray(t.entries) ? t.entries : Array.isArray(t.steps) ? t.steps : [t];
    for (const it of items) {
      if (!it || typeof it !== 'object') continue;
      const ts = exportTimestamp(it.created_at != null ? it.created_at : it.timestamp != null ? it.timestamp : it.date != null ? it.date : t.created_at != null ? t.created_at : t.timestamp);
      if (!ts) { skipped++; continue; }
      agg.add(ts.d, 'Perplexity', 'perplexity', { in: estTok(q(it).length), out: estTok(a(it).length), msgs: 1, est: 1 }, sid, ts.hour);
      msgs++;
    }
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), meta: { msgs, skipped, threads: list.length, sid } };
}

```
Insert directly BEFORE the ` * 4) Provider API payloads (fetched directly or imported as JSON files)` comment block (above its opening `/* ====` line; Task 8's DeepSeek CSV block sits there too — either order):

```js
/* Microsoft Copilot (consumer) activity-history CSV — account.microsoft.com/privacy → Copilot → Export all activity history. Columns are
 * not documented (prompts + responses with timestamps, an app/surface column on some exports) → matched tolerantly: a date/time column,
 * a prompt/query/request/user column, a response/answer/copilot/bot column, optional app/surface/product; a CSV with any token column is
 * NOT this format. Dates: ISO or 'M/D/YYYY H:MM[ AM|PM]' (local); unparseable → skipped and counted. Estimated tokens (prompt = input,
 * response = output), platform 'Microsoft Copilot', model 'copilot'; rows carry sid = file name (exports per surface merge). */
function isCopilotActivityCSV(head) {
  const has = (re) => head.some(h => re.test(h));
  return has(/date|time/) && has(/prompt|query|request|user/) && has(/response|answer|copilot|bot/) && !has(/token/);
}
function parseCopilotActivityCSV(text, fileName) {
  const rows = parseCSV(text);
  if (rows.length < 2) throw new Error('Empty CSV');
  const head = rows[0].map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, ''));
  const find = (re) => head.findIndex(h => re.test(h));
  const iDate = find(/date|time/), iPrompt = find(/prompt|query|request|user/), iResp = find(/response|answer|copilot|bot/), iApp = find(/app|surface|product/);
  if (iDate < 0 || iPrompt < 0 || iResp < 0) throw new Error('Not a Copilot activity CSV (needs date + prompt + response columns)');
  const agg = makeAgg(); let n = 0, skipped = 0; const apps = new Set();
  const sid = String(fileName || 'copilot-activity');
  for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const c = rows[r];
    const ts = exportTimestamp(String(c[iDate] || '').trim());
    if (!ts) { skipped++; continue; }
    if (iApp >= 0 && c[iApp]) apps.add(String(c[iApp]).trim());
    agg.add(ts.d, 'Microsoft Copilot', 'copilot', { in: estTok(String(c[iPrompt] || '').length), out: estTok(String(c[iResp] || '').length), msgs: 1, est: 1 }, sid, ts.hour);
    n++;
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), meta: { rows: n, skipped, apps: [...apps], sid } };
}
```

```js
    if (obj[0] && obj[0].chat_messages) return 'claudeai-export';
    if (dsConv(obj[0]) && dsMark(obj, fileName)) return 'deepseek-chat-export';
    if (pxItem(obj[0]) || (obj[0] && Array.isArray(obj[0].entries) && pxItem(obj[0].entries[0]))) return 'perplexity-export';
  }
  if (obj && !Array.isArray(obj) && typeof obj === 'object') {
    const convs = Array.isArray(obj.conversations) ? obj.conversations : null;
    if (convs && convs[0] && Array.isArray(convs[0].responses)) return 'grok-export';   // grok.com: {conversations:[{conversation, responses:[{message, sender, create_time}]}]}
    const dsList = convs || (Array.isArray(obj.data) ? obj.data : null);
    if (dsList && dsConv(dsList[0]) && dsMark(obj, fileName)) return 'deepseek-chat-export';
    if (Array.isArray(obj.participants) && Array.isArray(obj.messages) && (obj.participants.some(p => /meta\s*ai/i.test(p && p.name)) || obj.messages.some(m => /meta\s*ai/i.test(m && m.sender_name)))) return 'meta-ai-export';
    if (Array.isArray(obj.threads) && obj.threads[0] && (pxItem(obj.threads[0]) || (Array.isArray(obj.threads[0].entries) && pxItem(obj.threads[0].entries[0])))) return 'perplexity-export';
    if (convs && (!convs.length || (convs[0] && convs[0].mapping))) return 'chatgpt-export-wrapped';   // only ChatGPT mapping trees — any other {conversations:[…]} stays unknown
  }
  return 'unknown';
```

### Task 14: `readSqliteTable` + Ollama desktop `db.sqlite` history (est)

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, `tests/checks/87b-wave2-local.mjs`

Research row (Ollama, `local-logs-folder` #2): the desktop app (2025+) persists GUI chats in SQLite — Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Ollama\db.sqlite` (+ `-wal`/`-shm`), macOS `~/Library/Application Support/Ollama/db.sqlite`, Linux `~/.ollama/db.sqlite`; tables `chats(id, title, created_at)` and `messages(id, chat_id, role, content, thinking, model_name, model_cloud, created_at, updated_at, …)`; only desktop-UI chats appear (API/CLI usage does not); **no token counts are stored** → estimate from text; the WAL may hold un-checkpointed rows. Plan 4a deferred this for lack of sql.js; this task adds a ~90-line dependency-free SQLite b-tree reader instead. Quality of these rows: **estimated** (`est:1`); the Ollama card keeps `quality:'exact'` (the spec's platform-table row and the usage-logger path are exact) and its note now leads with "Exact for API traffic you log, estimated for desktop-app chats". Requires nothing from Tasks 1–2; only the `od.scanned.pick` sub-assertion ("Choose usage-log or Ollama data folder…") needs Task 6's `folderLabel` wiring — see the note under Step 2 for the ordering fallback. Fixture needs `node:sqlite` (Node ≥ 22.5; present in Node 25) — guarded: without it nothing is written and the check reports itself skipped.

- [ ] **Step 1 — fixture.** In `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, inside `generate()`, append after the last `wave2/` write (add the `// ---- Plan 4b (Wave 2) fixtures: wave2/ subtree ----` comment line first if no 4b task has created it yet — see Task 6 Step 1 for the anchor):

```js
  // T14 (4b) — Ollama desktop-app db.sqlite (schema subset of ollama/app/store/schema.sql). Needs node:sqlite; when it is unavailable nothing is
  //   written and 87b's ollama-db block reports itself skipped. One chat, 2 user + 2 assistant messages. m2's record is 6000 + 400 chars → it
  //   exceeds one 4096-byte page → overflow page. created_at shapes: ISO 'Z' text (m1/m2), SQLite 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS' (m3, UTC), unix-seconds
  //   text (m4 = 1786396260 = 2026-08-10T21:11:00Z). kv is WITHOUT ROWID → readSqliteTable must refuse it.
  //   Expected (estTok = round(chars/4)): m2 in=estTok(40)=10 out=estTok(6000+400)=1600 model gemma3:4b · m4 in=estTok(24)=6 out=estTok(100)=25 model 'ollama'
  //   (model_name NULL) → in=16 out=1625 msgs=2, both est:1, platform Ollama, $0.
  let sqlite = null;
  const origEmit = process.emitWarning;   // some Node versions print an ExperimentalWarning when node:sqlite loads — keep the suite output clean
  process.emitWarning = (w, ...a) => { if (/SQLite/i.test(String((w && w.message) || w))) return; return origEmit.call(process, w, ...a); };
  try { sqlite = await import('node:sqlite'); } catch { sqlite = null; }
  process.emitWarning = origEmit;
  if (sqlite && sqlite.DatabaseSync) {
    const dbPath = path.join(OUT, 'wave2', 'ollama-app', 'db.sqlite');
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dbPath), { recursive: true });
    const db = new sqlite.DatabaseSync(dbPath);
    db.exec('PRAGMA journal_mode=DELETE');   // no -wal sidecar: every row lands in db.sqlite (what the parser reads)
    db.exec('CREATE TABLE chats(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, title TEXT, created_at TEXT)');
    db.exec('CREATE TABLE messages(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, chat_id TEXT, role TEXT, content TEXT, thinking TEXT, model_name TEXT, model_cloud INTEGER, created_at TEXT, updated_at TEXT)');
    db.exec('CREATE TABLE kv(k TEXT PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT) WITHOUT ROWID');
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO chats VALUES (?,?,?)').run('c1', 'test chat', '2026-08-10T09:00:00Z');
    const ins = db.prepare('INSERT INTO messages VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)');
    ins.run('m1', 'c1', 'user', 'u'.repeat(40), null, null, 0, '2026-08-10T09:00:00Z', '2026-08-10T09:00:00Z');
    ins.run('m2', 'c1', 'assistant', 'a'.repeat(6000), 't'.repeat(400), 'gemma3:4b', 0, '2026-08-10T09:00:30Z', '2026-08-10T09:00:30Z');
    ins.run('m3', 'c1', 'user', 'u'.repeat(24), null, null, 0, '2026-08-10 21:10:00', '2026-08-10 21:10:00');
    ins.run('m4', 'c1', 'assistant', 'b'.repeat(100), null, null, 0, '1786396260', '1786396260');
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO kv VALUES (?,?)').run('schema', '1');
    db.close();
  }
```
Smoke: `node tests/fixtures/gen.mjs && node -e "const fs=require('fs');const b=fs.readFileSync('tests/fixtures/out/wave2/ollama-app/db.sqlite');console.log(b.slice(0,15).toString(), b.readUInt16BE(16), b.length)"` → `SQLite format 3 4096 24576` (page size 4096 → `maxLocal` = 4061 < 6400 → the m2 record uses an overflow page; the exact byte length may differ by SQLite version, the header and page size must not).

```js
        const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), ch => ch.charCodeAt(0));

        try { readSqliteTable(bytes.buffer, 'kv'); } catch (e) { worowid = e.message; }

        Store.sources = {}; Store.connectors = {}; Store.persist();

          paths: c.paths.some(p => /db\.sqlite/.test(p)), scanned, bad };
```

- [ ] **Step 4 — `readSqliteTable` + Ollama parsers.** Insert directly BEFORE the comment line that begins `/** ScanCache key prefix — bump whenever a parser's OUTPUT changes for unchanged bytes` (after the Task 6 parsers if present — order is irrelevant):

```js
/* =================================================================
 * Minimal SQLite 3 reader (no library): enough to read ONE rowid table out of a .sqlite file in the browser.
 * Supported: page sizes 512..65536 + reserved bytes, sqlite_master on page 1, table b-trees (interior 0x05 / leaf 0x0D),
 * overflow chains, record serial types 0-9, 12+ (blob → Uint8Array), 13+ (UTF-8 text), INTEGER PRIMARY KEY rowid aliases,
 * columns added later by ALTER TABLE (short records are NULL-padded). Throws Error('unsupported SQLite layout …') for WITHOUT
 * ROWID tables (index b-trees 0x02/0x0A), UTF-16 databases and reserved serial types; Error('not a SQLite file') for garbage;
 * Error('table not found: <name>') when sqlite_master has no such table. The -wal sidecar is NEVER read — rows still sitting in
 * the write-ahead log are invisible until the owning app checkpoints (close the Ollama app first).
 * File format: https://www.sqlite.org/fileformat2.html
 * ================================================================= */
function readSqliteTable(buffer, tableName) {
  const u8 = buffer instanceof Uint8Array ? buffer : new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const dv = new DataView(u8.buffer, u8.byteOffset, u8.byteLength);
  const bad = (why) => new Error('unsupported SQLite layout' + (why ? ' (' + why + ')' : ''));
  const MAGIC = 'SQLite format 3\0';
  if (u8.length < 100 || [...MAGIC].some((ch, i) => u8[i] !== ch.charCodeAt(0))) throw new Error('not a SQLite file');
  let pageSize = dv.getUint16(16); if (pageSize === 1) pageSize = 65536;   // offset 16: big-endian u16; 1 means 65536
  const usable = pageSize - u8[20];                                         // offset 20: reserved bytes at the END of every page
  const textEnc = dv.getUint32(56);                                          // 1 = UTF-8 (0 on a never-written db); 2/3 = UTF-16 → unsupported
  if (textEnc > 1) throw bad('text encoding ' + textEnc);
  const nPages = Math.floor(u8.length / pageSize);
  const pageOff = (n) => (n - 1) * pageSize;                                 // pages are 1-based
  const dec = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
  // varint: 1–9 bytes, big-endian 7-bit groups; a 9th byte carries all 8 bits
  const varint = (a, pos) => {
    let v = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const b = a[pos + i]; v = v * 128 + (b & 0x7f); if (!(b & 0x80)) return [v, i + 1]; }
    return [v * 256 + a[pos + 8], 9];
  };
  // table-leaf cell payload: if it does not fit, the local part follows the SQLite formula (X = U-35, M = ((U-12)*32/255)-23,
  // K = M + ((P-M) % (U-4)); local = K ≤ X ? K : M), then a u32 first-overflow-page number; each overflow page = u32 next + data
  const maxLocal = usable - 35, minLocal = Math.floor((usable - 12) * 32 / 255) - 23;
  const readPayload = (pos, size) => {
    if (size <= maxLocal) return u8.subarray(pos, pos + size);
    const K = minLocal + ((size - minLocal) % (usable - 4));
    const local = K <= maxLocal ? K : minLocal;
    const out = new Uint8Array(size);
    out.set(u8.subarray(pos, pos + local), 0);
    let filled = local, next = dv.getUint32(pos + local), guard = 0;
    while (next && filled < size) {
      if (next > nPages || guard++ > nPages) throw bad('overflow chain');
      const o = pageOff(next), chunk = Math.min(usable - 4, size - filled);
      out.set(u8.subarray(o + 4, o + 4 + chunk), filled);
      filled += chunk; next = dv.getUint32(o);
    }
    if (filled < size) throw bad('truncated overflow chain');
    return out;
  };
  // record: varint header size, one serial type per column, then the values back to back
  const decodeRecord = (pay) => {
    const pdv = new DataView(pay.buffer, pay.byteOffset, pay.byteLength);
    const [hdrSize, n0] = varint(pay, 0);
    const types = []; let hp = n0;
    while (hp < hdrSize) { const [t, n] = varint(pay, hp); types.push(t); hp += n; }
    const vals = []; let bp = hdrSize;
    for (const t of types) {
      let v = null, len = 0;
      if (t === 0) v = null;
      else if (t === 1) { v = pdv.getInt8(bp); len = 1; }
      else if (t === 2) { v = pdv.getInt16(bp); len = 2; }
      else if (t === 3) { v = (pdv.getInt8(bp) << 16) | pdv.getUint16(bp + 1); len = 3; }
      else if (t === 4) { v = pdv.getInt32(bp); len = 4; }
      else if (t === 5) { v = pdv.getInt16(bp) * 4294967296 + pdv.getUint32(bp + 2); len = 6; }
      else if (t === 6) { v = Number(pdv.getBigInt64(bp)); len = 8; }
      else if (t === 7) { v = pdv.getFloat64(bp); len = 8; }
      else if (t === 8) v = 0;
      else if (t === 9) v = 1;
      else if (t >= 12 && t % 2 === 0) { len = (t - 12) / 2; v = pay.slice(bp, bp + len); }          // blob
      else if (t >= 13) { len = (t - 13) / 2; v = dec.decode(pay.subarray(bp, bp + len)); }           // text
      else throw bad('serial type ' + t);                                                             // 10, 11 are reserved
      vals.push(v); bp += len;
    }
    return vals;
  };
  // b-tree walk. Page header: type byte, first freeblock u16, cell count u16 (+3), cell content start u16 (+5), fragmented bytes (+7),
  // right-most child u32 (+8, interior only); then the cell pointer array (u16 offsets from the START of the page). Page 1's header
  // sits after the 100-byte file header, but its cell offsets are still relative to byte 0 of the page.
  const walk = (pageNo, onRow, depth) => {
    if (depth > 64 || pageNo < 1 || pageNo > nPages) throw bad('page ' + pageNo);
    const base = pageOff(pageNo), hdr = base + (pageNo === 1 ? 100 : 0);
    const type = u8[hdr], nCells = dv.getUint16(hdr + 3);
    if (type === 0x0D) {                                                      // table leaf: cells = varint payload size, varint rowid, payload
      for (let i = 0; i < nCells; i++) {
        const cell = base + dv.getUint16(hdr + 8 + i * 2);
        const [size, a] = varint(u8, cell), [rowid, b] = varint(u8, cell + a);
        onRow(rowid, decodeRecord(readPayload(cell + a + b, size)));
      }
    } else if (type === 0x05) {                                               // table interior: cells = u32 left child, varint key (unused); then the right-most child
      for (let i = 0; i < nCells; i++) walk(dv.getUint32(base + dv.getUint16(hdr + 12 + i * 2)), onRow, depth + 1);
      walk(dv.getUint32(hdr + 8), onRow, depth + 1);
    } else throw bad('page type 0x' + type.toString(16));                     // 0x02 / 0x0A = index b-tree (WITHOUT ROWID tables live there)
  };
  // sqlite_master is the table b-tree rooted at page 1: columns type, name, tbl_name, rootpage, sql
  let master = null;
  walk(1, (rowid, r) => { if (!master && r[0] === 'table' && String(r[1]).toLowerCase() === String(tableName).toLowerCase()) master = r; }, 0);
  if (!master) throw new Error('table not found: ' + tableName);
  const sql = String(master[4] || '');
  if (/WITHOUT\s+ROWID/i.test(sql)) throw bad('WITHOUT ROWID');
  // column names from CREATE TABLE: the parenthesized list split on commas at depth 0, first token per entry (quotes stripped);
  // table constraints (PRIMARY KEY(…), UNIQUE, CHECK, FOREIGN KEY, CONSTRAINT) are not columns
  const body = sql.slice(sql.indexOf('(') + 1, sql.lastIndexOf(')'));
  const parts = []; let d = 0, cur = '';
  for (const ch of body) { if (ch === '(') d++; else if (ch === ')') d--; if (ch === ',' && d === 0) { parts.push(cur); cur = ''; } else cur += ch; }
  parts.push(cur);
  const columns = [], rowidAlias = [];
  for (const p of parts) {
    const s = p.trim(); if (!s || /^(CONSTRAINT|PRIMARY\s+KEY|UNIQUE|CHECK|FOREIGN\s+KEY)\b/i.test(s)) continue;
    const m = /^("([^"]*)"|`([^`]*)`|\[([^\]]*)\]|'([^']*)'|([^\s]+))\s*([\s\S]*)$/.exec(s);
    const name = m[2] ?? m[3] ?? m[4] ?? m[5] ?? m[6];
    if (/^INTEGER\s+PRIMARY\s+KEY\b/i.test(m[7] || '')) rowidAlias.push(columns.length);   // rowid alias: the record holds NULL, the cell's rowid is the value
    columns.push(name);
  }
  const rows = [];
  walk(master[3], (rowid, vals) => {
    while (vals.length < columns.length) vals.push(null);   // columns added by ALTER TABLE are absent from older records
    for (const i of rowidAlias) if (vals[i] == null) vals[i] = rowid;
    rows.push(vals.slice(0, columns.length));
  }, 0);
  return { columns, rows };
}

/** Ollama desktop app — db.sqlite (GUI chats only; API/CLI traffic is not in here). Table messages(id, chat_id, role, content,
 *  thinking, model_name, model_cloud, created_at, updated_at, …). No token counts are stored: out = estTok(content + thinking),
 *  in = estTok(the previous user message in the same chat) → every row est:1. created_at may be ISO text, SQLite
 *  'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS' (UTC), or unix seconds/ms (number or digit string). Dedupe id 'ollama-db:<message id>' (seen/addedIds)
 *  so the same db scanned again under another path never double counts; row sid 'ollama-db:<chat_id>' groups rows per chat.
 *  An unreadable file (WITHOUT ROWID / not SQLite) THROWS — parseFolderCached counts it as one unreadable file and moves on.
 *  The -wal sidecar is not read: close the Ollama app first so the WAL is checkpointed into db.sqlite. */
async function parseOllamaDb(files, onProgress, seen = new Set(), c, siblingFiles) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); const addedIds = []; let msgs = 0, lines = 0;
  const list = files.filter(f => /(^|\/)db\.sqlite$/i.test(f.path));
  const when = (v, fallback) => {
    if (typeof v === 'string' && /^\d{9,13}(\.\d+)?$/.test(v.trim())) v = +v;
    if (typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0) return new Date(v < 1e12 ? v * 1000 : v);   // unix seconds vs milliseconds
    if (typeof v === 'string') {
      let s = v.trim().replace(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}) (\d)/, '$1T$2').replace(/(\.\d{3})\d+/, '$1');        // 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS[.ffffff][zone]' → ISO, ≤ 3 fraction digits
      if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(s) && !/(Z|[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2})$/i.test(s)) s += 'Z';                   // SQLite CURRENT_TIMESTAMP / Go UTC text carry no zone → UTC
      const d = new Date(s); if (!isNaN(d)) return d;
    }
    return new Date(fallback);
  };
  for (let fi = 0; fi < list.length; fi++) {
    const { file } = list[fi];
    const table = readSqliteTable(await file.arrayBuffer(), 'messages');   // throws on a bad file (see above)
    const col = Object.fromEntries(table.columns.map((n, i) => [String(n).toLowerCase(), i]));
    const get = (r, name) => { const i = col[name]; return i == null ? null : r[i]; };
    if (col.role == null || col.content == null) throw new Error('unsupported SQLite layout (messages has no role/content columns)');
    const lastUser = new Map();   // chat_id → text of the latest user message so far (rows arrive in rowid = insertion order)
    for (const r of table.rows) {
      lines++;
      const role = String(get(r, 'role') || '').toLowerCase();
      const chat = String(get(r, 'chat_id') ?? '');
      const text = String(get(r, 'content') ?? '');
      if (role === 'user') { lastUser.set(chat, text); continue; }
      if (role !== 'assistant') continue;
      const id = 'ollama-db:' + String(get(r, 'id') ?? (fi + ':' + lines));
      if (seen.has(id)) continue;
      seen.add(id); addedIds.push(id);
      const ts = when(get(r, 'created_at'), file.lastModified);
      agg.add(dayKey(ts), (c && c.platform) || 'Ollama', String(get(r, 'model_name') || 'ollama'),
        { in: estTok((lastUser.get(chat) || '').length), out: estTok(text.length + String(get(r, 'thinking') ?? '').length), msgs: 1, est: 1 },
        'ollama-db:' + chat, ts.getHours());
      msgs++;
    }
    if (onProgress) onProgress(fi + 1, list.length);
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), projects: [], meta: { files: list.length, msgs, lines, badLines: 0, addedIds } };
}
/** Ollama folder dispatcher (registry parser): usage-logger JSONL (exact, parseUsageLoggerJsonl) + desktop db.sqlite (estimated,
 *  parseOllamaDb) through one card; results concatenated. parseFolderCached hands in one file at a time, so each call sees one kind. */
async function parseOllamaFiles(files, onProgress, seen = new Set(), c, siblingFiles) {
  const a = await parseUsageLoggerJsonl(files.filter(f => /\.jsonl$/i.test(f.path)), null, seen, c);
  const b = await parseOllamaDb(files.filter(f => /(^|\/)db\.sqlite$/i.test(f.path)), null, seen, c, siblingFiles);
  if (onProgress) onProgress(files.length, files.length);
  return { rows: a.rows.concat(b.rows), projects: [], meta: { files: a.meta.files + b.meta.files, msgs: a.meta.msgs + b.meta.msgs, lines: a.meta.lines + b.meta.lines,
    badLines: a.meta.badLines + b.meta.badLines, addedIds: (a.meta.addedIds || []).concat(b.meta.addedIds || []) } };
}
```

### Task 15: Folder-log projects into the Projects table (Codex cwd, Gemini CLI project hash, Copilot CLI cwd, Junie project dir)

**Files:** Modify `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, `tests/fixtures/gen.mjs`, `tests/checks/87-folder-logs.mjs`

Background (read before editing): the folder-log parser contract already returns `projects` (always `[]` today) and `parseFolderCached` already merges `entry.projects` across files by `project + model` (the line `const k = p.project + '<sep>' + p.model;` inside its "merge project rows across files" block — it contains the 6-char separator escape; **do not edit that line**). `parseClaudeLogs` emits project rows shaped `{ project, model, in, cw, cr, out, msgs }` with `project = prettyProject(cwd || projectDir)`; `renderProjects` reads `Store.projects` only. This task makes four parsers emit the same row shape (plus `platform`, and `local: 1` when the session ran on a local provider, so `Pricing.cost(pb.model, pb)` keeps them at $0), stores each folder-log connector's rows under `Store.projectsBy[c.id]` (persisted in the `data` blob), and merges them into the Projects table. Project names: Codex and Copilot CLI carry a working directory → `prettyProject(cwd)` (same normalization as Claude Code, so the same repo used from several tools is ONE table row); Gemini CLI records no cwd → the `tmp/<project_hash>` folder stands in (`'~/.gemini/tmp/' + hash`); Junie → the `projects/<project>/matterhorn/.matterhorn/` directory name with JetBrains' `.<hex>` cache suffix stripped; anything unknown → `prettyProject(null)` = `'(unknown project)'` (same bucket the Claude parser uses — no usage is silently dropped). Parser OUTPUT changes for unchanged bytes → `SCAN_CACHE_VER` is bumped (one-time re-parse, same precedent as v4).

- [ ] **Step 4 — `makeProjAgg` helper.** In `AI Usage Dashboard.html`, insert directly BEFORE the comment line `/** files: [{path, file}] → aggregated usage */` (the line above `async function parseClaudeLogs`):

```js
/** Project×model bundles for the Projects table — the row shape parseClaudeLogs' projMap emits
 *  ({ project, model, in, cw, cr, out, msgs }) plus `platform` and `local: 1` when the request ran on a local
 *  provider (Pricing.cost honours both). Nested Maps (no separator bytes); parseFolderCached merges the rows of
 *  one connector across files. Used by the Codex / Gemini CLI / Copilot CLI / Junie parsers. */
function makeProjAgg(platform) {
  const byProj = new Map();   // project → Map(model → bundle)
  return {
    add(project, model, t) {
      let m = byProj.get(project); if (!m) { m = new Map(); byProj.set(project, m); }
      let b = m.get(model);
      if (!b) { b = { project, model, platform, in: 0, cw: 0, cr: 0, out: 0, msgs: 0 }; m.set(model, b); }
      b.in += t.in || 0; b.cw += t.cw || 0; b.cr += t.cr || 0; b.out += t.out || 0; b.msgs += t.msgs || 0;
      if (t.local) b.local = 1;
    },
    rows() { const out = []; for (const m of byProj.values()) out.push(...m.values()); return out; },
  };
}

```

**(a) Codex** — replace `async function parseCodexRollouts(files, onProgress, seen = new Set()) {` … `}` with:

```js
async function parseCodexRollouts(files, onProgress, seen = new Set()) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); const proj = makeProjAgg('OpenAI Codex CLI'); const addedIds = []; let msgs = 0; const k = { lines: 0, badLines: 0 };
  const list = files.filter(f => /(^|\/)rollout-[^/]*\.jsonl$/i.test(f.path));
  for (let fi = 0; fi < list.length; fi++) {
    const { path, file } = list[fi];
    const sid = (path.split('/').pop() || '').replace(/\.jsonl$/i, '');
    if (seen.has(sid)) { if (onProgress) onProgress(fi + 1, list.length); continue; }   // same rollout re-appearing under sessions/ vs archived_sessions/ — whole file already counted
    seen.add(sid); addedIds.push(sid);
    let model = 'codex', prev = null, local = 0, cwd = null;   // cwd = session_meta / turn_context payload.cwd (latest wins) → Projects table (prettyProject, same as Claude Code)
    await eachJsonl(file, (o) => {
      const p = o.payload || {};
      if (o.type === 'session_meta' && p.model_provider != null) local = (isLocalProvider(p.model_provider) || /^oss$/i.test(String(p.model_provider).trim())) ? 1 : 0;   // built-in OSS provider ids ('ollama' / 'lmstudio'; 'oss' in older builds) — the model name alone never decides
      if (o.type === 'session_meta' || o.type === 'turn_context') { if (p.model) model = String(p.model); if (p.cwd) cwd = String(p.cwd); return; }
      if (o.type !== 'event_msg' || p.type !== 'token_count' || !p.info || !p.info.total_token_usage) return;
      const u = p.info.total_token_usage;
      const cur = { in: finTok(+u.input_tokens || 0), cr: finTok(+u.cached_input_tokens || 0), out: finTok(+u.output_tokens || 0) };
      const base = prev && cur.in >= prev.in && cur.out >= prev.out ? prev : { in: 0, cr: 0, out: 0 };
      const d = { in: cur.in - base.in, cr: Math.max(0, cur.cr - base.cr), out: cur.out - base.out };
      prev = cur;
      if (d.in <= 0 && d.out <= 0) return;   // no new usage since the last snapshot (duplicate line / heartbeat)
      const ts = o.timestamp ? new Date(o.timestamp) : null, okTs = ts && !isNaN(ts);
      const t = { in: Math.max(0, d.in - d.cr), cr: d.cr, out: Math.max(0, d.out), msgs: 1 };
      if (local) t.local = 1;
      agg.add(dayKey(okTs ? ts : file.lastModified), 'OpenAI Codex CLI', model, t, sid, okTs ? ts.getHours() : undefined);
      proj.add(prettyProject(cwd), model, t);
      msgs++;
    }, k);
    if (onProgress) onProgress(fi + 1, list.length);
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), projects: proj.rows(), meta: { files: list.length, msgs, lines: k.lines, badLines: k.badLines, addedIds } };
}
```

**(b) Gemini CLI** — replace `async function parseGeminiCliSessions(files, onProgress, seen = new Set()) {` … `}` with:

```js
async function parseGeminiCliSessions(files, onProgress, seen = new Set()) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); const proj = makeProjAgg('Gemini CLI'); const addedIds = []; let msgs = 0, lines = 0, badLines = 0;
  const list = files.filter(f => /(^|\/)session-[^/]*\.json$/i.test(f.path));
  for (let fi = 0; fi < list.length; fi++) {
    const { path, file } = list[fi];
    let obj; try { obj = JSON.parse(await file.text()); } catch { badLines++; continue; }
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(obj.messages)) { badLines++; continue; }
    lines++;
    const sid = String(obj.sessionId || (path.split('/').pop() || '').replace(/\.json$/i, ''));
    // Projects table: Gemini CLI does not record the working directory — sessions live under tmp/<project_hash>/chats/, so the
    // hash folder stands in for the project (a cwd-like field is honoured if a future CLI version writes one).
    const hm = path.match(/(?:^|\/)tmp\/([^/]+)\/chats\//i);
    const cwd = obj.cwd || obj.projectRoot || obj.workspaceRoot || null;
    const hash = (obj.projectHash != null && String(obj.projectHash)) || (hm ? hm[1] : null);
    const projName = cwd ? prettyProject(String(cwd)) : hash ? '~/.gemini/tmp/' + hash : prettyProject(null);
    for (const m of obj.messages) {
      if (!m || m.type === 'user') continue;
      const um = m.usageMetadata;
      const t0 = m.tokens || (um ? { input: um.promptTokenCount, output: um.candidatesTokenCount, cached: um.cachedContentTokenCount, thoughts: um.thoughtsTokenCount, tool: um.toolUsePromptTokenCount } : null);
      if (!t0) continue;
      if (m.id != null) { const id = sid + ':' + m.id; if (seen.has(id)) continue; seen.add(id); addedIds.push(id); }
      const input = finTok(+t0.input || 0), cached = finTok(+t0.cached || 0), output = finTok(+t0.output || 0), thoughts = finTok(+t0.thoughts || 0), tool = finTok(+t0.tool || 0);   // tool = toolUsePromptTokenCount: billed (uncached) input for built-in tools, not part of promptTokenCount
      const ts = m.timestamp ? new Date(m.timestamp) : null, okTs = ts && !isNaN(ts);
      const model = String(m.model || obj.model || 'gemini');
      const t = { in: Math.max(0, input - cached) + tool, cr: cached, out: output + thoughts, msgs: 1 };
      agg.add(dayKey(okTs ? ts : file.lastModified), 'Gemini CLI', model, t, sid, okTs ? ts.getHours() : undefined);
      proj.add(projName, model, t);
      msgs++;
    }
    if (onProgress) onProgress(fi + 1, list.length);
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), projects: proj.rows(), meta: { files: list.length, msgs, lines, badLines, addedIds } };
}
```

**(c) Copilot CLI** — replace `async function parseCopilotCliEvents(files, onProgress, seen = new Set()) {` … `}` with:

```js
async function parseCopilotCliEvents(files, onProgress, seen = new Set()) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); const proj = makeProjAgg('GitHub Copilot'); const addedIds = []; let msgs = 0; const k = { lines: 0, badLines: 0 };
  const list = files.filter(f => /(^|\/)events\.jsonl$/i.test(f.path));
  const num = (o, a, b) => finTok(+(o[a] ?? o[b] ?? 0) || 0);
  for (let fi = 0; fi < list.length; fi++) {
    const { path, file } = list[fi];
    const parts = path.split('/'); const sid = parts.length > 1 ? parts[parts.length - 2] : (path.replace(/\.jsonl$/i, ''));
    let sawUsage = 0, cwd = null; const shutdowns = [];   // cwd from session.start (data.context.cwd per the chronicle docs; flat data.cwd / gitRoot / repository accepted) → Projects table
    await eachJsonl(file, (o) => {
      const d = o.data || o.payload || o;
      const type = String(o.type || o.event || '');
      if (type === 'session.start') { const ctx = (d.context && typeof d.context === 'object') ? d.context : d; const w = ctx.cwd || ctx.workingDirectory || ctx.gitRoot || ctx.repository; if (w) cwd = String(w); return; }
      if (type === 'assistant.usage') {
        sawUsage++;
        if (o.id != null) { const id = sid + ':' + o.id; if (seen.has(id)) return; seen.add(id); addedIds.push(id); }
        const ts = o.timestamp ? new Date(o.timestamp) : null, okTs = ts && !isNaN(ts);
        const model = String(d.model || 'copilot');
        const t = { in: num(d, 'inputTokens', 'input_tokens'), out: num(d, 'outputTokens', 'output_tokens'), cr: num(d, 'cacheReadTokens', 'cache_read_tokens'), cw: num(d, 'cacheWriteTokens', 'cache_write_tokens'), msgs: 1 };
        agg.add(dayKey(okTs ? ts : file.lastModified), 'GitHub Copilot', model, t, sid, okTs ? ts.getHours() : undefined);
        proj.add(prettyProject(cwd), model, t);
        msgs++;
      } else if (type === 'session.shutdown' && d.modelMetrics && typeof d.modelMetrics === 'object') {
        shutdowns.push({ ts: o.timestamp, metrics: d.modelMetrics });
      }
    }, k);
    if (!sawUsage) for (const s of shutdowns) {
      const ts = s.ts ? new Date(s.ts) : null, okTs = ts && !isNaN(ts);
      for (const [model, m] of Object.entries(s.metrics)) {
        const u = (m && m.usage) || {}, n = finTok(+((m && m.requests && m.requests.count) ?? 1) || 0) || 1;
        const t = { in: num(u, 'inputTokens', 'input_tokens'), out: num(u, 'outputTokens', 'output_tokens'), cr: num(u, 'cacheReadTokens', 'cache_read_tokens'), cw: num(u, 'cacheWriteTokens', 'cache_write_tokens'), msgs: n };
        agg.add(dayKey(okTs ? ts : file.lastModified), 'GitHub Copilot', model, t, sid, okTs ? ts.getHours() : undefined);
        proj.add(prettyProject(cwd), model, t);
        msgs += n;
      }
    }
    if (onProgress) onProgress(fi + 1, list.length);
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), projects: proj.rows(), meta: { files: list.length, msgs, lines: k.lines, badLines: k.badLines, addedIds } };
}
```

**(d) Junie** — replace `async function parseJunieEvents(files, onProgress) {` … `}` with:

```js
async function parseJunieEvents(files, onProgress) {
  const agg = makeAgg(); const proj = makeProjAgg('JetBrains Junie'); let msgs = 0; const k = { lines: 0, badLines: 0 };
  const list = files.filter(f => /(^|\/)[^/]*events\.jsonl$/i.test(f.path));
  const pick = (o, ...keys) => { for (const key of keys) if (o && o[key] != null) return finTok(+o[key] || 0); return 0; };
  for (let fi = 0; fi < list.length; fi++) {
    const { path, file } = list[fi];
    const sid = (path.split('/').pop() || '').replace(/\.jsonl$/i, '');
    // Projects table: IDE layout …/projects/<project>/matterhorn/.matterhorn/events/… → <project> (JetBrains' ".<hash>" cache suffix stripped);
    // a project-path-like field on any event (Junie CLI sessions) is honoured; otherwise '(unknown project)'.
    const pm = path.match(/(?:^|\/)([^/]+)\/matterhorn\/\.matterhorn\//i);
    let projName = pm ? pm[1].replace(/\.[0-9a-f]{6,}$/i, '') : null;
    await eachJsonl(file, (o) => {
      const ev = o.event && typeof o.event === 'object' ? o.event : o;
      if (!projName) { const w = ev.projectPath || ev.projectDir || ev.cwd || ev.workingDirectory || o.projectPath; if (w) projName = prettyProject(String(w)); }
      const type = String(ev.type || ev.eventType || o.type || '');
      if (!/LlmResponse/i.test(type)) return;
      const src = [ev, ev.answer, ev.usage, ev.response, ev.tokenUsage, ev.statistics].find(x => x && typeof x === 'object' && (x.inputTokens != null || x.input_tokens != null));
      if (!src) return;
      const model = String(ev.model || ev.modelName || (ev.answer && (ev.answer.model || ev.answer.modelName)) || src.model || 'junie');
      const tsRaw = o.timestamp || ev.timestamp || o.time; const ts = tsRaw ? new Date(tsRaw) : null, okTs = ts && !isNaN(ts);
      const t = { in: pick(src, 'inputTokens', 'input_tokens'), out: pick(src, 'outputTokens', 'output_tokens'), cr: pick(src, 'cacheTokens', 'cacheInputTokens', 'cache_read_tokens'), cw: pick(src, 'cacheCreateInputTokens', 'cacheCreationTokens', 'cache_write_tokens'), msgs: 1 };
      agg.add(dayKey(okTs ? ts : file.lastModified), 'JetBrains Junie', model, t, sid, okTs ? ts.getHours() : undefined);
      proj.add(projName || prettyProject(null), model, t);
      msgs++;
    }, k);
    if (onProgress) onProgress(fi + 1, list.length);
  }
  return { rows: agg.rows(), projects: proj.rows(), meta: { files: list.length, msgs, lines: k.lines, badLines: k.badLines, addedIds: [] } };
}
```

Then bump the cache version: replace `const SCAN_CACHE_VER = 'scan:v4:';` with `const SCAN_CACHE_VER = 'scan:v5:';   // v5: folder-log parsers emit project rows (Plan 4b T15) — output changed for unchanged bytes → one-time re-parse` (if an earlier 4b task already moved it past v4, increment by one more instead — any parser-output change for unchanged bytes needs a fresh prefix; `96-scan-cache` only asserts that `scan:v3:` misses and the current prefix survives, so it stays green).

```js
    const pr = await page.evaluate(() => {
      Pricing.load();   // fresh page → DEFAULT_PRICES (harness cleared aiusage:*)
      const L = (m) => Pricing.resolve(m).label;
      const M = { in: 1e6, out: 1e6, cw: 0, cr: 1e6 };   // 1M of each billed field
      return {
        grok4: L('grok-4-0709'), grok: L('grok'), grokMini: L('grok-3-mini'), grokFast: L('grok-4-fast-non-reasoning'),
        dsReason: L('deepseek-reasoner'), dsR1: L('deepseek-r1-distill-llama'), dsChat: L('deepseek-chat'), dsOr: L('deepseek/deepseek-v4'),
        sonar: L('sonar-pro'), pplx: L('perplexity'), meta: L('meta-ai'), copilot: L('copilot'), chatgpt: L('auto'), gpt4o: L('chatgpt-4o-latest'), unknown: L('zzz-never-heard-of-it'),
        // costs: Grok 4 = 3 + 15 + 0.75 = 18.75 · DeepSeek chat = 0.27 + 1.10 + 0.07 = 1.44 · Meta AI = 0 · Sonar = 1 + 1 + 0 = 2
        grokCost: Pricing.cost('grok-4', M), dsCost: Pricing.cost('deepseek-chat', M), metaCost: Pricing.cost('meta-ai', M), sonarCost: Pricing.cost('sonar', M),
        lastIsFallback: DEFAULT_PRICES[DEFAULT_PRICES.length - 1].pat === '.*',
      };
    });
    ok('pricing (4b): Grok 4 / Grok fast-mini / DeepSeek Reasoner / DeepSeek chat / Perplexity Sonar / Meta AI rows resolve (first-match order right; ^copilot$ joins the ChatGPT row; fallback still last)',
      pr.grok4 === 'Grok 4 (xAI list)' && pr.grok === 'Grok 4 (xAI list)' && pr.grokMini === 'Grok fast/mini' && pr.grokFast === 'Grok fast/mini'
      && pr.dsReason === 'DeepSeek Reasoner' && pr.dsR1 === 'DeepSeek Reasoner' && pr.dsChat === 'DeepSeek chat (V3 list)' && pr.dsOr === 'DeepSeek chat (V3 list)'
      && pr.sonar === 'Perplexity Sonar' && pr.pplx === 'Perplexity Sonar' && pr.meta === 'Meta AI (consumer, no API price)' && /ChatGPT/.test(pr.copilot) && pr.copilot === pr.chatgpt && pr.gpt4o === 'GPT-4o' && pr.unknown === 'Unknown model'
      && Math.abs(pr.grokCost - 18.75) < 1e-9 && Math.abs(pr.dsCost - 1.44) < 1e-9 && pr.metaCost === 0 && Math.abs(pr.sonarCost - 2) < 1e-9 && pr.lastIsFallback, JSON.stringify(pr));

    // ---- Plan 4b: registry invariants for EVERY Wave 2 entry (everything that is not a Wave 1 id) ----
    const WAVE1_IDS = ['local', 'anthropic', 'openai', 'gemini', 'copilot', 'openrouter', 'codex', 'gemini-cli', 'copilot-cli', 'continue', 'cline', 'roo', 'aider', 'junie', 'lmstudio', 'ollama', 'imports'];
    const WAVE2_PLATFORMS = ['LocalAI', 'vLLM', 'llama.cpp', 'GPT4All', 'Jan', 'text-generation-webui',
      'Fireworks AI', 'DeepSeek API', 'Mistral', 'Cohere', 'Together AI', 'Groq', 'Hugging Face', 'xAI API', 'Perplexity API', 'Replicate',
      'Azure OpenAI', 'Google Vertex AI', 'AWS Bedrock',
      'Claude Enterprise (org)', 'ChatGPT', 'Claude.ai', 'Grok', 'Microsoft Copilot', 'Perplexity', 'Meta AI', 'DeepSeek chat',
      'Amazon Q / Kiro', 'Windsurf'];
    const LOCAL_W2 = ['LocalAI', 'vLLM', 'llama.cpp', 'GPT4All', 'Jan', 'text-generation-webui'];
    const inv = await page.evaluate((wave1, wave2, localW2) => {
      const groups = HUB_GROUPS.map(g => g.id);
      const imports = connectorById('imports');
      const w2 = CONNECTORS.filter(c => !wave1.includes(c.id));
      const DATA_KINDS = ['folder-log', 'local-server', 'api-key', 'admin-key', 'cloud-token'];
      const problems = [];
      const ids = CONNECTORS.map(c => c.id);
      if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) problems.push('duplicate id: ' + ids.filter((x, i) => ids.indexOf(x) !== i).join(','));
      const plats = CONNECTORS.map(c => c.platform).filter(Boolean);
      const dupPlat = plats.filter((p, i) => plats.indexOf(p) !== i && p !== 'GitHub Copilot');
      if (dupPlat.length) problems.push('duplicate platform: ' + dupPlat.join(','));
      for (const p of wave2) if (!plats.includes(p)) problems.push('missing platform: ' + p);
      for (const c of w2) {
        const pre = c.id + ': ';
        if (!['exact', 'estimated', 'validate', 'none'].includes(c.quality)) problems.push(pre + 'quality=' + c.quality);
        if (!(typeof c.qualityNote === 'string' && c.qualityNote.length >= 80)) problems.push(pre + 'qualityNote < 80 chars');
        if (!(c.links && (c.links.get || c.links.docs))) problems.push(pre + 'no links.get/docs');
        if (!groups.includes(c.category)) problems.push(pre + 'category=' + c.category);
        if (c.statusEl !== c.id + '-status') problems.push(pre + 'statusEl=' + c.statusEl);
        if (!document.getElementById(c.statusEl)) problems.push(pre + 'statusEl not in DOM');
        if (DATA_KINDS.includes(c.kind) && !(Array.isArray(c.srcIds) && c.srcIds.includes(c.id))) problems.push(pre + 'srcIds lacks own id');
        if (c.kind === 'export-file' && !(Array.isArray(c.srcIds) && c.srcIds.length && c.srcIds.every(s => imports.srcIds.includes(s)))) problems.push(pre + 'export-file srcIds not a subset of imports.srcIds');
        if (c.kind === 'link-only' && !(typeof c.reason === 'string' && c.reason.length > 20)) problems.push(pre + 'link-only without reason');
        if (c.kind === 'link-only' && c.quality !== 'none') problems.push(pre + 'link-only quality must be none');
        if (c.kind === 'cloud-token' && c.sessionOnly !== true) problems.push(pre + 'cloud-token not sessionOnly');
        if (c.fetch && typeof globalThis[c.fetch] !== 'function') problems.push(pre + 'fetch fn missing: ' + c.fetch);
        if (c.parser && typeof globalThis[c.parser] !== 'function') problems.push(pre + 'parser fn missing: ' + c.parser);
        if (c.validate && typeof globalThis[c.validate] !== 'function') problems.push(pre + 'validate fn missing: ' + c.validate);
        if (c.poll && typeof globalThis[c.poll] !== 'function') problems.push(pre + 'poll fn missing: ' + c.poll);
        if (!c.platform) problems.push(pre + 'no platform');
        if (!c.brand || !c.mark || !c.name) problems.push(pre + 'brand/mark/name missing');
        try { Store.connectorStatus(c); } catch (e) { problems.push(pre + 'connectorStatus threw'); }
      }
      for (const s of ['grok-export', 'deepseek-chat-export', 'meta-ai-export', 'perplexity-export', 'copilot-activity-csv']) if (!imports.srcIds.includes(s)) problems.push('imports.srcIds lacks ' + s);
      for (const p of localW2) if (Pricing.cost('any-model', { platform: p, in: 1e6, out: 1e6, cw: 0, cr: 0 }) !== 0) problems.push('local platform not $0: ' + p);
      const byGroup = Object.fromEntries(groups.map(g => [g, CONNECTORS.filter(c => c.category === g).length]));
      return { n: CONNECTORS.length, w2: w2.length, problems, byGroup, orderHasAll: plats.every(p => PLATFORM_ORDER.includes(p)) };
    }, WAVE1_IDS, WAVE2_PLATFORMS, LOCAL_W2);
    ok('registry (4b): every Wave 2 entry satisfies the invariants — quality ∈ exact|estimated|validate|none, qualityNote ≥ 80 chars, links.get|docs, category ∈ HUB_GROUPS, statusEl = id+"-status" and in the DOM, data kinds list their own id in srcIds, export-file srcIds ⊂ imports.srcIds, link-only has reason + quality none, cloud-token sessionOnly, fetch/parser/validate/poll names resolve; ids unique; platforms unique except GitHub Copilot; all 29 Wave 2 platforms present; local runners $0',
      inv.problems.length === 0 && inv.w2 >= 29 && inv.n >= 46 && inv.orderHasAll, JSON.stringify(inv));
    ok('registry (4b): all five hub groups are non-empty', Object.values(inv.byGroup).every(n => n > 0), JSON.stringify(inv.byGroup));
```

```js
    const hubSearch = await page.evaluate(() => {
      Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.projects = []; Store.connectors = {};
      App._drill = null; App.filters.hiddenPlatforms = new Set(); LS.del('view'); View.load(); App._hubQuery = '';
      App._render();
      const box = document.getElementById('hub-search');
      const total = document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .acct').length;   // every registry card (17 in 4a, ~46 in 4b) → box shown
      const boxShownBefore = !box.hidden;
      box.value = 'e'; box.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
      const matched = [...document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .acct')].map(c => c.dataset.connector);
      // hide cards (matching ones first) until exactly 12 are un-hidden → box hides → query must be dropped
      const toHide = [...matched, ...CONNECTORS.map(c => c.id).filter(id => !matched.includes(id))].slice(0, total - 12);
      for (const id of toHide) View.hideCard(id);
      App._render();
      const afterHide = { boxHidden: box.hidden, boxValue: box.value, q: App._hubQuery, n: document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .acct').length,
        unhidden: CONNECTORS.filter(c => !View.isCardHidden(c.id)).length, nomatch: !!document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .hub-nomatch') };
      // (b) restore all, query that matches nothing → note carries the query text
      View.showAllCards(); App._render();
      box.value = 'zzz'; box.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
      const note = document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .hub-nomatch');
      const noMatch = { n: document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .acct').length, note: note ? note.textContent : null, boxHidden: box.hidden };
      box.value = ''; box.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
      // (c) Enter on an action button
      const orig = App.drillDown; let drills = 0; App.drillDown = () => { drills++; };
      const btn = document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="anthropic"] .acct-manage');
      btn.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter', bubbles: true }));
      const card = document.querySelector('#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="anthropic"]');
      card.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter', bubbles: true }));   // control: Enter on the card itself still drills
      App.drillDown = orig;
      const autocomplete = box.getAttribute('autocomplete');
      return { total, boxShownBefore, matched: matched.length, afterHide, noMatch, drills, autocomplete, restored: document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .acct').length };
    });
    ok('hub: hiding cards down to ≤12 while a query is typed hides the box AND drops the query (all un-hidden cards render, no stale filter)',
      hubSearch.total > 12 && hubSearch.boxShownBefore && hubSearch.matched > 0 && hubSearch.matched < hubSearch.total &&
      hubSearch.afterHide.boxHidden && hubSearch.afterHide.unhidden === 12 && hubSearch.afterHide.n === 12 && hubSearch.afterHide.q === '' && hubSearch.afterHide.boxValue === '' && !hubSearch.afterHide.nomatch,
      JSON.stringify(hubSearch));
    ok('hub: a query matching nothing renders a "No accounts match" note carrying the query text (box stays visible; autocomplete off)',
      hubSearch.noMatch.n === 0 && /No accounts match/.test(hubSearch.noMatch.note || '') && /zzz/.test(hubSearch.noMatch.note || '') && !hubSearch.noMatch.boxHidden &&
      hubSearch.autocomplete === 'off' && hubSearch.restored === hubSearch.total, JSON.stringify(hubSearch.noMatch));
    ok('hub: Enter on a card action button does not fire the card drill-down (Enter on the card itself still does)', hubSearch.drills === 1, JSON.stringify({ drills: hubSearch.drills }));
```
  (b) Then insert directly AFTER that last `ok('hub: Enter on a card action button …')` line and BEFORE `  } finally {`:

```js
    // ---- Plan 4b: the hub at ~46 cards ----
    const big = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.projects = []; Store.connectors = {}; App._drill = null; App.filters.hiddenPlatforms = new Set(); LS.del('view'); View.load(); App._hubQuery = '';
      App._render();
      const total = CONNECTORS.length;
      const box = document.getElementById('hub-search');
      const groups = [...document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .hub-group')].map(g => g.dataset.group);
      const counts = Object.fromEntries(groups.map(g => [g, document.querySelectorAll(`#accounts-hub .hub-group[data-group="${g}"] .acct`).length]));
      // perf: average of 5 synchronous renderHub() calls with every card present
      let t = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { const a = performance.now(); App.renderHub(); t += performance.now() - a; }
      const avgMs = t / 5;
      // hidden chip counts: hide 3 cards → "3 hidden — show"; the chip is a <button>, so Enter = click → all restored (App.render is rAF-batched)
      const ids = CONNECTORS.slice(-3).map(c => c.id);
      ids.forEach(id => View.hideCard(id)); App.renderHub();
      const chip = document.querySelector('#hub-chips .hub-hidden-chip');
      const chipText = chip ? chip.textContent : null;
      const nAfterHide = document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .acct').length;
      chip.focus(); chip.click();
      await new Promise(r => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(r)));
      const nAfterShow = document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .acct').length;
      const chipGone = !document.querySelector('#hub-chips .hub-hidden-chip');
      // keyboard: every card is a focusable button (tabindex=0, role=button); Enter on a data card drills into its platform
      const cards = [...document.querySelectorAll('#accounts-hub .acct')];
      const focusable = cards.length === total && cards.every(c => c.tabIndex === 0 && c.getAttribute('role') === 'button');
      const dataCard = CONNECTORS.find(c => c.platform && c.kind !== 'link-only' && c.kind !== 'export-file' && c.kind !== 'files');
      const el2 = document.querySelector(`#accounts-hub .acct[data-connector="${dataCard.id}"]`);
      el2.focus(); el2.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter', bubbles: true }));
      const drilled = App._drill === dataCard.platform;
      App._drill = null; App._render();
      return { total, boxShown: !box.hidden, groups, counts, avgMs, chipText, nAfterHide, nAfterShow, chipGone, focusable, drilled };
    });
    ok('hub (4b): ~46 registry cards → search box visible; groups render in spec order and all five are non-empty',
      big.total >= 46 && big.boxShown && JSON.stringify(big.groups) === JSON.stringify(['coding', 'chat', 'local', 'api', 'cloud']) && Object.values(big.counts).every(n => n > 0), JSON.stringify(big));
    ok('hub (4b): renderHub() averages under 60 ms with every card present (5 runs)', big.avgMs < 60, big.avgMs.toFixed(1) + ' ms');
    ok('hub (4b): hidden chip counts ("3 hidden — show"); Enter/click on the chip restores every card; all cards are keyboard-focusable buttons and Enter drills into a data card\'s platform',
      big.chipText === '3 hidden — show' && big.nAfterHide === big.total - 3 && big.nAfterShow === big.total && big.chipGone && big.focusable && big.drilled, JSON.stringify(big));

    // ---- Plan 4b: budgets editor lists Wave 2 platforms once data exists (registry-derived, once per platform) ----
```

```js
      Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.connectors = {}; Store.projects = [];
      const remembered = CONNECTORS.find(c => c.fetch && !c.sessionOnly && c.keyLS);   // a Wave 2 api-key / admin-key entry that can remember its key
      const session = CONNECTORS.find(c => c.fetch && c.sessionOnly);                  // a cloud-token entry (never persists)
      const calls = {}, orig = {};
      for (const c of [remembered, session]) { orig[c.id] = globalThis[c.fetch]; window[c.fetch] = async () => { calls[c.id] = (calls[c.id] || 0) + 1; return { label: 'stub', detail: '' }; }; }
      const setFields = (c, v) => (c.fields || []).forEach(f => { const e = document.getElementById(c.id + '-f-' + f.id); if (e) e.value = v; });
      // (a) remembered key + the input still holds it → fetch called once
      LS.set(remembered.keyLS, 'k-remembered'); const ki = document.getElementById(remembered.id + '-key'); ki.value = 'k-remembered'; setFields(remembered, 'x');
      // (b) sessionOnly with an EMPTY input → never called (nothing remembered, nothing to re-fetch)
      const si = document.getElementById(session.id + '-key'); si.value = ''; setFields(session, '');
      await App.refreshAll();
      const sessKey = session.keyLS || ('key:' + session.id);
      const out = { remembered: remembered.id, session: session.id, a: calls[remembered.id] || 0, bEmpty: calls[session.id] || 0, sessionLS: LS.get(sessKey, null) };
      // (c) sessionOnly with a value still in the input → re-fetched ("session-only" = while this page is open), still nothing persisted
      si.value = 'tok-live'; setFields(session, 'x');
      await App.refreshAll();
      out.bLive = calls[session.id] || 0;
      out.sessionLSAfter = LS.get(sessKey, null);
      out.secretFieldLS = (session.fields || []).filter(f => f.secret).map(f => LS.get('field:' + session.id + ':' + f.id, null)).every(v => v === null);
      // cleanup
      for (const c of [remembered, session]) window[c.fetch] = orig[c.id];
      LS.del(remembered.keyLS); ki.value = ''; si.value = ''; setFields(remembered, ''); setFields(session, '');
      for (const c of [remembered, session]) (c.fields || []).forEach(f => LS.del('field:' + c.id + ':' + f.id));
      Store.sources = {}; Store.billed = {}; Store.connectors = {}; Store.persist();
      return out;
    });
    ok('refreshAll (4b): remembered key → its fetch fn runs once; sessionOnly + empty input → not called; sessionOnly + live input → called; session key/secret fields never persisted',
      ra.a === 1 && ra.bEmpty === 0 && ra.bLive === 1 && ra.sessionLS === null && ra.sessionLSAfter === null && ra.secretFieldLS, JSON.stringify(ra));
```

- [ ] **Step 4 — size + hygiene gates (95-ui-misc postRun).** Replace the whole `export async function postRun({ ok }) { … }` at the end of `tests/checks/95-ui-misc.mjs` with:

```js
export async function postRun({ ok }) {
  const fs = await import('node:fs');
  const path = await import('node:path');
  const root = path.join(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..');
  const html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'AI Usage Dashboard.html'));
  const bad = [...html].filter(b => b <= 8).length;
  ok('hygiene: no raw control bytes (<0x09) in the HTML file', bad === 0, 'found ' + bad);
  ok('size: AI Usage Dashboard.html is ≤ 600 KB', html.length <= 600 * 1024, Math.round(html.length / 1024) + ' KB');
  // every .mjs under tests/ (checks, lib, fixtures, tools — not node_modules / generated out / artifacts) and docs/tools/: separators stay visible escape text
  const walk = (dir, out = []) => { for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) { if (e.name === 'node_modules' || e.name === 'out' || e.name === 'artifacts') continue; const p = path.join(dir, e.name); if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p, out); else if (p.endsWith('.mjs')) out.push(p); } return out; };
  const files = walk(path.join(root, 'tests')).concat(fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'docs', 'tools')) ? walk(path.join(root, 'docs', 'tools')) : []);
  const dirty = files.filter(p => [...fs.readFileSync(p)].some(b => b <= 8)).map(p => path.relative(root, p));
  ok('hygiene: no raw control bytes in tests/**/*.mjs and docs/tools/*.mjs', dirty.length === 0, dirty.join(', '));
}
```
